  const MUS={iv:null,step:0};
  const MOODS={

  };
  function music(mood){
    clearInterval(MUS.iv);MUS.iv=null;MUS.step=0;E.mood=mood;if(!mood||!MOODS[mood])return;
    const M=MOODS[mood],f=d=>M.b*Math.pow(2,d/12),stepMs=60000/M.bpm/4;
    MUS.iv=setInterval(()=>{
      if(!snd()||!E.on||E.susp||document.hidden)return;
      const s=MUS.step++,i=s%16,bar=Math.floor(s/16);
      try{
        if(M.kick[i]){tone('sine',M.taiko?95:130,M.taiko?38:42,.28,M.taiko?.12:.09);if(M.taiko&&i%8===4)noise('lowpass',700,90,.22,.07)}
        if(M.hat[i%4]&&(mood!=='final'||i%2===0||true))noise('highpass',7000,6000,.05,.018);
        if(i===0&&M.pad){const root=M.sc[bar%M.sc.length];[0,M.sc[2]||7,M.sc[4]||10].forEach(d=>tone('sine',f(root+d)/2,f(root+d)/2,60000/M.bpm*4/1000*.95,.02))}
        if(Math.random()<M.arp&&i%2===0){const deg=M.sc[(s*3+(bar%3))%M.sc.length],oct=(i%8<4)?1:2;tone(M.wave,f(deg)*oct,f(deg)*oct*.995,.22,M.wave==='sawtooth'?.016:.026)}
        if(mood==='final'&&i%4===2)tone('sawtooth',f(M.sc[bar%M.sc.length])/2,f(M.sc[bar%M.sc.length])/2.02,.2,.035);
        if(mood==='carn'&&i%8===6)tone('square',f(M.sc[(bar+2)%M.sc.length])*2,f(M.sc[(bar+2)%M.sc.length])*2,.08,.014);
      }catch(e){}
    },stepMs);
  }
  E.music=music;E.stopMusic=()=>{clearInterval(MUS.iv);MUS.iv=null};

  /* ================= shared 3D resources ================= */
  const GEO={sph:new THREE.SphereGeometry(1,18,14),box:new THREE.BoxGeometry(1,1,1),cyl:new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1,1,1,14,1,true),ring:new THREE.RingGeometry(.9,1,56),plane:new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1,1),cone:new THREE.ConeGeometry(1,1,12),tet:new THREE.TetrahedronGeometry(1),tor:new THREE.TorusGeometry(1,.04,8,48)};
  function tex(draw,sz){const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=sz||128;draw(c.getContext('2d'),c.width);return new THREE.CanvasTexture(c)}
  const TX={
    glow:tex((x,s)=>{const g=x.createRadialGradient(s/2,s/2,0,s/2,s/2,s/2);g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,255,255,1)');g.addColorStop(.3,'rgba(255,255,255,.55)');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,255,255,0)');x.fillStyle=g;x.fillRect(0,0,s,s)}),
    dot:tex((x,s)=>{x.fillStyle='#fff';x.beginPath();x.arc(s/2,s/2,s*.4,0,7);x.fill()}),
    star:tex((x,s)=>{x.fillStyle='#fff';x.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<10;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/5-Math.PI/2,r=i%2?s*.17:s*.46;x.lineTo(s/2+Math.cos(a)*r,s/2+Math.sin(a)*r)}x.closePath();x.fill()}),
    page:tex((x,s)=>{x.fillStyle='#fffdf5';x.fillRect(0,0,s,s);x.strokeStyle='#cbd5e1';x.lineWidth=2;for(let y=18;y<s;y+=14){x.beginPath();x.moveTo(10,y);x.lineTo(s-10,y);x.stroke()}x.strokeStyle='#1e293b';x.lineWidth=3;x.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<30;i++)x.lineTo(14+i*3.6,s*.5+Math.sin(i*1.3)*14+Math.random()*10);x.stroke();x.strokeStyle='#ef4444';x.beginPath();x.moveTo(8,0);x.lineTo(8,s);x.stroke()},128)
  };
  const mbasic=(c,o,add)=>new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:c,transparent:true,opacity:o==null?1:o,depthWrite:false,side:THREE.DoubleSide,blending:add?THREE.AdditiveBlending:THREE.NormalBlending});
  const col=c=>new THREE.Color(c);

  /* particles: two sprite pools (additive glow and solid ink) */
  const PG=[],PI=[];let pinit=false;
  function pinitf(){
    if(pinit)return;pinit=true;
    const mk=(arr,map,add,n)=>{for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const s=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({map,transparent:true,depthWrite:false,blending:add?THREE.AdditiveBlending:THREE.NormalBlending}));s.visible=false;s.renderOrder=40;scene.add(s);arr.push({s,v:V(0,0,0),l:0,m:1,g:0,d:0,gr:0,sz:1,fade:1})}};
    mk(PG,TX.glow,true,300);mk(PI,TX.dot,false,150);
  }
  function emit(x,y,z,o){
    pinitf();o=o||{};const pool=o.solid?PI:PG,n=o.n||10;
    for(let k=0;k<n;k++){
      const p=pool.find(q=>q.l<=0);if(!p)return;
      p.l=p.m=rnd(o.life?o.life*.7:.5,o.life||.9);
      const sp=o.spd==null?3:o.spd,dir=o.dir;
      let vx=rnd(-1,1),vy=rnd(-1,1),vz=rnd(-1,1);
      if(o.up){vy=Math.abs(vy)*1+o.up}
      const L=Math.hypot(vx,vy,vz)||1;
      p.v.set(vx/L*sp*rnd(.3,1),vy/L*sp*rnd(.3,1),vz/L*sp*rnd(.3,1));
      if(dir)p.v.add(V(dir[0],dir[1],dir[2]));
      const sr=o.r||0;p.s.position.set(x+rnd(-sr,sr),y+rnd(-sr*.4,sr*.4),z+rnd(-sr,sr));
      p.g=o.g==null?0:o.g;p.d=o.drag||0;p.gr=o.grow||0;p.sz=(o.sz||.3)*rnd(.6,1.3);p.fade=o.fade==null?1:o.fade;
      p.s.material.color.set(o.c==null?0xffffff:(Array.isArray(o.c)?pick(o.c):o.c));p.s.material.rotation=rnd(0,6);
      p.s.scale.setScalar(p.sz);p.s.visible=true;p.s.material.opacity=o.op==null?1:o.op;p.op=p.s.material.opacity;
    }
  }
  function ptick(dt){
    for(const pool of [PG,PI])for(const p of pool){
      if(p.l<=0)continue;p.l-=dt;
      if(p.l<=0){p.s.visible=false;continue}
      p.v.y-=p.g*dt;if(p.d)p.v.multiplyScalar(Math.max(0,1-p.d*dt));
      p.s.position.addScaledVector(p.v,dt);
      const u=p.l/p.m;p.s.material.opacity=p.op*(p.fade?Math.min(1,u*2.2):1);
      p.s.scale.setScalar(Math.max(.001,p.sz*(1+p.gr*(1-u))));
    }
  }
  E.emit=emit;
  const disposeObj=o=>{o.traverse(c=>{if(c.material){if(c.material.dispose)c.material.dispose()}})};
  function fx(o,dur,fn,fin){scene.add(o);E.fxs.push({o,t:0,d:dur,fn,fin});return o}
  function killFx(){E.fxs.splice(0).forEach(e=>{scene.remove(e.o);disposeObj(e.o)});E.props.splice(0).forEach(p=>{scene.remove(p.o);disposeObj(p.o)})}
  E.killFx=killFx;
  /* ----- shape effects ----- */
  function ringFx(x,y,z,c,size,dur,flat,add){const m=new THREE.Mesh(GEO.ring,mbasic(c,.9,add!==false));m.position.set(x,y,z);if(flat)m.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;fx(m,dur,u=>{m.scale.setScalar(.2+size*eO(u));m.material.opacity=.95*(1-u)})}
  function shock(x,z,c,size,big){ringFx(x,.06,z,c,size||8,.7,true);ringFx(x,.08,z,0xffffff,(size||8)*.7,.5,true);if(big){ringFx(x,.1,z,c,(size||8)*1.5,1,true);domeFx(x,.1,z,c,(size||8)*.5,.6)}emit(x,.2,z,{n:big?26:14,c:[c,0xffffff],spd:big?7:4,up:.5,g:7,sz:.35,life:.8})}
  function domeFx(x,y,z,c,size,dur){const m=new THREE.Mesh(GEO.sph,mbasic(c,.5,true));m.position.set(x,y,z);fx(m,dur,u=>{m.scale.setScalar(.1+size*eO(u));m.material.opacity=.5*(1-u)})}
  function pillar(x,z,c,h,wd,dur){const m=new THREE.Mesh(GEO.cyl,mbasic(c,.7,true));m.position.set(x,(h||12)/2,z);fx(m,dur||.9,u=>{const k=u<.2?u/.2:1-(u-.2)/.8;m.scale.set(wd*k+.01,h||12,wd*k+.01);m.material.opacity=.8*k});const g=new THREE.Mesh(GEO.cyl,mbasic(0xffffff,.8,true));g.position.copy(m.position);fx(g,dur||.9,u=>{const k=u<.2?u/.2:1-(u-.2)/.8;g.scale.set(wd*.4*k+.01,h||12,wd*.4*k+.01)})}
  function beam(a,b,c,wd,dur,flick){
    const d=b.clone().sub(a),len=d.length(),m=new THREE.Mesh(GEO.cyl,mbasic(c,.85,true));
    m.position.copy(a).addScaledVector(d,.5);m.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(V(0,1,0),d.clone().normalize());
    const core=new THREE.Mesh(GEO.cyl,mbasic(0xffffff,.95,true));core.position.copy(m.position);core.quaternion.copy(m.quaternion);
    fx(m,dur,u=>{const k=u<.12?u/.12:1-Math.max(0,(u-.6)/.4);const j=flick?rnd(.7,1.15):1;m.scale.set(wd*k*j+.001,len,wd*k*j+.001);m.material.opacity=.8*k});
    fx(core,dur,u=>{const k=u<.12?u/.12:1-Math.max(0,(u-.6)/.4);core.scale.set(wd*.35*k+.001,len,wd*.35*k+.001)});
  }
  function bolt(a,b,c,dur,seg){
    const g=new THREE.Group(),n=seg||9,pts=[a.clone()];
    for(let i=1;i<n;i++){const p=a.clone().lerp(b,i/n);p.x+=rnd(-.5,.5);p.z+=rnd(-.5,.5);p.y+=rnd(-.2,.2);pts.push(p)}pts.push(b.clone());
    for(let i=0;i<pts.length-1;i++){const d=pts[i+1].clone().sub(pts[i]),len=d.length(),m=new THREE.Mesh(GEO.cyl,mbasic(i%2?c:0xffffff,.95,true));m.position.copy(pts[i]).addScaledVector(d,.5);m.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(V(0,1,0),d.clone().normalize());m.scale.set(.07,len,.07);g.add(m)}
    fx(g,dur||.35,u=>{g.visible=Math.random()>.2;g.children.forEach(m=>m.material.opacity=1-u)});
    emit(b.x,b.y,b.z,{n:10,c:[c,0xffffff],spd:5,g:6,sz:.3,life:.6});
  }
  function shards(x,y,z,c,n,spd){
    for(let i=0;i<(n||14);i++){
      const m=new THREE.Mesh(GEO.tet,new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:Array.isArray(c)?pick(c):c,transparent:true,side:THREE.DoubleSide}));const s=rnd(.08,.22);m.scale.set(s,s*rnd(1,2.4),s*.4);m.position.set(x,y,z);
      const v=V(rnd(-1,1),rnd(.3,1.6),rnd(-1,1)).multiplyScalar(spd||5),rot=V(rnd(-9,9),rnd(-9,9),rnd(-9,9));
      fx(m,rnd(.9,1.5),(u,dt)=>{v.y-=9*(dt||.016);m.position.addScaledVector(v,dt||.016);m.rotation.x+=rot.x*(dt||.016);m.rotation.y+=rot.y*(dt||.016);m.material.opacity=1-u*u});
    }
  }
  function crater(x,z,size){const m=new THREE.Mesh(GEO.ring,mbasic(0x000000,.55));m.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;m.position.set(x,.03,z);m.scale.setScalar(size);E.props.push({o:m,upd:null});scene.add(m);const m2=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(1,28),mbasic(0x000000,.45));m2.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;m2.position.set(x,.025,z);m2.scale.setScalar(size*.85);E.props.push({o:m2});scene.add(m2)}
  function inkSplat(x,y,z,c,n){emit(x,y,z,{n:n||24,c:c||0x111827,solid:true,spd:6,up:.6,g:12,sz:.28,life:1.1,fade:0,drag:.5})}
  function lightFlash(c,inten,ms,pos){const L=new THREE.PointLight(col(c),inten,22);L.position.copy(pos||V(0,3,3));scene.add(L);fx(L,ms/1000,u=>{L.intensity=inten*(1-u)})}
  /* paper pages drifting through the scene */
  function pages(n,area,spd){
    const a=area||{x:9,y:6,z:6};
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
      const m=new THREE.Mesh(GEO.plane,new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({map:TX.page,side:THREE.DoubleSide,transparent:true,opacity:.95}));
      const s=rnd(.5,1);m.scale.set(s,s*1.3,1);m.position.set(rnd(-a.x,a.x),rnd(.5,a.y),rnd(-a.z,a.z)-1.5);
      const v=V(rnd(-.4,.4)*(spd||1),rnd(-.5,-.15)*(spd||1),rnd(-.3,.3)),r=V(rnd(-1.4,1.4),rnd(-1.4,1.4),rnd(-1.4,1.4));
      scene.add(m);E.props.push({o:m,upd:dt=>{m.position.addScaledVector(v,dt);m.rotation.x+=r.x*dt;m.rotation.y+=r.y*dt;m.rotation.z+=r.z*dt;if(m.position.y<-.5){m.position.y=a.y;m.position.x=rnd(-a.x,a.x)}}});
    }
  }
  /* ambient emitters running while a stage is on */
  function ambient(kind){
    E.amb=[];
    const A={
      ink:()=>emit(rnd(-8,8),rnd(3,8),rnd(-4,3),{n:1,c:0x1e1b4b,solid:true,spd:.3,g:-.2,sz:.12,life:2.5,fade:1}),
      embers:()=>emit(rnd(-7,7),0,rnd(-4,3),{n:1,c:[0xf97316,0xfbbf24],spd:.4,up:1.6,g:-1.2,sz:.14,life:2.4}),
      bubbles:()=>emit(rnd(-8,8),0,rnd(-4,3),{n:2,c:[0x7dd3fc,0xffffff],spd:.3,up:2,g:-1,sz:.16,life:3.5,fade:1}),
      petals:()=>emit(rnd(-8,8),rnd(4,7),rnd(-4,3),{n:1,c:[0xfda4af,0xffffff],spd:.4,g:.4,sz:.16,life:4,drag:.3}),
      wisps:()=>emit(rnd(-8,8),rnd(.3,3),rnd(-4,3),{n:1,c:[0xc4b5fd,0x93c5fd],spd:.3,up:.5,g:-.2,sz:.34,life:3.2}),
      data:()=>emit(rnd(-8,8),rnd(4,8),rnd(-4,3),{n:2,c:[0x22d3ee,0x4ade80],spd:.1,g:5,sz:.12,life:1.6,fade:0}),
      stars:()=>emit(rnd(-9,9),rnd(0,7),rnd(-6,2),{n:1,c:0xffffff,spd:.1,sz:.1,life:2.4}),
      sparks:()=>emit(rnd(-6,6),0,rnd(-3,2),{n:1,c:[0xfde047,0xffffff],spd:.6,up:2.4,g:2,sz:.1,life:1.6}),
      confetti:()=>emit(rnd(-7,7),7,rnd(-4,2),{n:2,c:[0xf43f5e,0xfacc15,0x22c55e,0x38bdf8,0xa855f7],spd:.8,g:2.5,sz:.18,life:3,drag:.4}),
      snow:()=>emit(rnd(-9,9),8,rnd(-5,2),{n:2,c:0xffffff,spd:.3,g:1.2,sz:.14,life:4,drag:.3})
    };
    if(kind&&A[kind])E.amb.push({fn:A[kind],acc:0,rate:kind==='bubbles'||kind==='data'||kind==='confetti'||kind==='snow'?14:7});
  }
  E.ambient=ambient;

  /* ================= 2D fx ================= */
  function flash(c,ms){return fxDiv('background:'+c,[{opacity:.95},{opacity:0}],(ms||450)/sk())}
  function vign(c,ms){return fxDiv('background:radial-gradient(transparent 32%,'+c+' 100%)',[{opacity:0},{opacity:1,offset:.25},{opacity:1,offset:.7},{opacity:0}],(ms||2200)/sk())}
  function speed(ms,c){const d=fxDiv('',[{opacity:0},{opacity:1,offset:.15},{opacity:1,offset:.8},{opacity:0}],(ms||1200)/sk());d.innerHTML='<div class="s2spd" style="'+(c?'background:repeating-conic-gradient(from 0deg at 50% 50%,'+c+' 0 .5deg,#0000 .5deg 2.6deg)':'')+'"></div>'}
  function onomat(txt,c,x,y,rot){
    const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='s2on';d.textContent=txt;d.style.setProperty('--c',c||'#fde047');
    d.style.left=(x==null?20+Math.random()*40:x)+'%';d.style.top=(y==null?20+Math.random()*30:y)+'%';fxl.appendChild(d);
    const r=rot==null?rnd(-14,14):rot;
    const a=d.animate([{transform:`scale(.2) rotate(${r-20}deg)`,opacity:0},{transform:`scale(1.35) rotate(${r}deg)`,opacity:1,offset:.18},{transform:`scale(1) rotate(${r}deg)`,opacity:1,offset:.28},{transform:`scale(1.05) rotate(${r}deg)`,opacity:1,offset:.75},{transform:`scale(1.4) rotate(${r}deg)`,opacity:0}],{duration:1100/sk(),fill:'forwards'});a.onfinish=()=>d.remove();
  }
  function title(big,sub,c,ms){
    const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='s2ti';d.style.setProperty('--c',c||'#fff');d.innerHTML='<b>'+big+'</b>'+(sub?'<i>'+sub+'</i>':'');try{d.firstChild.style.fontSize='min(15vw,'+(88/Math.max(4,String(big).length*.62)).toFixed(1)+'vw,120px)'}catch(e){}fxl.appendChild(d);
    const a=d.animate([{transform:'scale(4)',opacity:0,filter:'blur(14px)'},{transform:'scale(.94)',opacity:1,filter:'none',offset:.1},{transform:'scale(1)',opacity:1,offset:.16},{transform:'scale(1.04)',opacity:1,offset:.85},{transform:'scale(1.5)',opacity:0,filter:'blur(8px)'}],{duration:(ms||2600)/sk(),fill:'forwards'});a.onfinish=()=>d.remove();
    setTimeout(()=>{G.shake=Math.max(G.shake||0,.5)},(ms||2600)*.08/sk());sfx('boom');
  }
  function lower(name,subt,c,tag){
    const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='s2lt';d.style.setProperty('--c',c||'#fbbf24');d.innerHTML='<small>'+(tag||'NEW FACE')+'</small><b>'+name+'</b><i>'+(subt||'')+'</i>';fxl.appendChild(d);
    const a=d.animate([{transform:'translateX(-110%)'},{transform:'translateX(0)',offset:.1},{transform:'translateX(0)',offset:.82},{transform:'translateX(-110%)'}],{duration:3300/sk(),fill:'forwards'});a.onfinish=()=>d.remove();sfx('chime');
  }
  function glitch(ms){
    ms=(ms||600)/sk();sfx('glitch');
    for(let i=0;i<7;i++){const d=fxDiv('inset:auto;left:0;right:0;top:'+rnd(0,92)+'%;height:'+rnd(2,10)+'%;background:'+pick(['#22d3ee','#f43f5e','#facc15','#fff'])+';mix-blend-mode:difference',[{transform:'translateX(0)',opacity:0},{transform:'translateX('+rnd(-70,70)+'px)',opacity:.9,offset:.3},{transform:'translateX('+rnd(-90,90)+'px)',opacity:0}],ms,{delay:i*ms*.08})}
    const c=ren.domElement;let n=0;const iv=setInterval(()=>{n++;c.style.transform=n%2?`translateX(${rnd(-14,14)}px) skewX(${rnd(-4,4)}deg)`:'';c.style.filter=n%3===0?'hue-rotate(90deg) saturate(2)':(E.baseFilter||'');if(n>ms/60){clearInterval(iv);c.style.transform='';c.style.filter=E.baseFilter||''}},55);
  }
  function invert(ms){const c=ren.domElement;c.style.filter='invert(1)';setTimeout(()=>{c.style.filter=E.baseFilter||''},(ms||140)/sk())}
  function blur(ms){const c=ren.domElement;c.style.filter='blur(4px)';setTimeout(()=>{c.style.filter=E.baseFilter||''},(ms||200)/sk())}
  function tint(f,ms){const c=ren.domElement;E.baseFilter=f||'';c.style.transition='filter '+((ms==null?1500:ms)/1000)+'s';c.style.filter=E.baseFilter;}
  async function wipe(kind,ms){
    ms=ms||700;const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='s2wp';ui.appendChild(d);
    if(kind==='slash'){d.style.background='#fff';d.style.clipPath='polygon(-40% 0,-40% 0,-60% 100%,-60% 100%)';const a=d.animate([{clipPath:'polygon(-40% 0,-40% 0,-60% 100%,-60% 100%)'},{clipPath:'polygon(-10% 0,120% 0,100% 100%,-30% 100%)'}],{duration:ms/sk(),fill:'forwards',easing:'ease-in'});await a.finished.catch(()=>{});
      const b=d.animate([{opacity:1},{opacity:0}],{duration:ms/sk(),fill:'forwards'});b.onfinish=()=>d.remove();return}
    d.style.clipPath='circle(0% at 50% 55%)';
    const a=d.animate([{clipPath:'circle(0% at 50% 55%)'},{clipPath:'circle(75% at 50% 55%)'}],{duration:ms/sk(),fill:'forwards',easing:'cubic-bezier(.6,0,.3,1)'});sfx('ink');
    await a.finished.catch(()=>{});
    d.dataset.hold=1;E.wipeEl=d;
  }
  function unwipe(ms){
    const d=E.wipeEl;if(!d){return}E.wipeEl=null;
    const a=d.animate([{clipPath:'circle(75% at 50% 45%)'},{clipPath:'circle(0% at 50% 45%)'}],{duration:(ms||800)/sk(),fill:'forwards',easing:'cubic-bezier(.6,0,.3,1)'});a.onfinish=()=>d.remove();
  }
  function matrix(ms){
    const cv=$$('s2mx');cv.width=innerWidth;cv.height=innerHeight;const x=cv.getContext('2d'),cols=Math.floor(innerWidth/16),y=new Array(cols).fill(0).map(()=>Math.random()*-40);
    const ch='01アイウエオカキクケコ{}<>/;#ABCDEF∆∑';const t0=performance.now();x.font='15px monospace';
    const iv=setInterval(()=>{
      const el=performance.now()-t0;x.fillStyle='rgba(0,8,6,.18)';x.fillRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
      for(let i=0;i<cols;i++){x.fillStyle=Math.random()<.06?'#fff':'#22d3ee';x.fillText(ch[(Math.random()*ch.length)|0],i*16,y[i]*16);if(y[i]*16>cv.height&&Math.random()>.96)y[i]=0;y[i]++}
      if(el>ms){clearInterval(iv);cv.style.transition='opacity .6s';cv.style.opacity=0;setTimeout(()=>{x.clearRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);cv.style.opacity=1;cv.style.transition=''},650)}
    },48);
  }
  E.fx2d={flash,vign,speed,onomat,title,lower,glitch,invert,blur,wipe,unwipe,matrix,tint};
  const letterbox=on=>ui.classList.toggle('lb',!!on);

  /* ================= camera ================= */
  const CAM={p:V(0,2,6),l:V(0,1.2,0),fm:1,fk:0,roll:0,rig:null,sw:0};
  const baseFov=()=>{if(E.userFov)return E.userFov;const ar=innerWidth/Math.max(1,innerHeight);return ar>=1?45:Math.min(84,Math.max(62,62+(0.9-ar)*70))};
  function rig(fn,ms,ease){CAM.rig={fn,t:0,d:Math.max(.05,ms/1000),e:ease||eIO,u:0}}
  function camCut(p,l,fm,roll){CAM.rig=null;CAM.p.copy(p);CAM.l.copy(l);CAM.fm=fm||1;CAM.roll=roll||0}
  function camMove(p,l,ms,o){
    o=o||{};const p0=CAM.p.clone(),l0=CAM.l.clone(),f0=CAM.fm,r0=CAM.roll,f1=o.fm==null?CAM.fm:o.fm,r1=o.roll==null?CAM.roll:o.roll;
    rig(u=>{CAM.p.lerpVectors(p0,p,u);CAM.l.lerpVectors(l0,l,u);CAM.fm=lerp(f0,f1,u);CAM.roll=lerp(r0,r1,u)},ms,o.e);
  }
  const fovPunch=d=>{CAM.fk=(CAM.fk||0)+d};
  function fovTo(fm,ms){const f0=CAM.fm;const prev=CAM.rig;tw(ms,u=>{CAM.fm=lerp(f0,fm,u)},eIO).catch(()=>{})}
  function rollTo(r,ms){const r0=CAM.roll;tw(ms,u=>{CAM.roll=lerp(r0,r,u)},eIO).catch(()=>{})}
  function orbit(c,r0,r1,h0,h1,a0,a1,ms,o){
    o=o||{};const ctr=c.clone?c:V(c.x,c.y||1.2,c.z);
    rig(u=>{const a=lerp(a0,a1,u),r=lerp(r0,r1,u);CAM.p.set(ctr.x+Math.sin(a)*r,lerp(h0,h1,u),ctr.z+Math.cos(a)*r);CAM.l.set(ctr.x,(o.ly==null?ctr.y:o.ly),ctr.z);CAM.fm=lerp(o.f0||1,o.f1||1,u);CAM.roll=lerp(o.r0||0,o.r1||0,u)},ms,o.e);
  }
  E.cam={cut:camCut,to:camMove,rig,orbit,fovPunch,fovTo,rollTo,get:()=>CAM};
  function camTick(dt){
    const r=CAM.rig;
    if(r){r.t+=dt/r.d*sk();const u=Math.min(1,r.t);try{r.fn(r.e(u),u)}catch(e){}if(u>=1)CAM.rig=null}
    else{E.drift=(E.drift||0)+dt;CAM.p.x+=Math.sin(E.drift*.7)*.0012;CAM.p.y+=Math.cos(E.drift*.9)*.0008}
    CAM.fk*=Math.pow(.02,dt);
    camPos.copy(CAM.p);camLook.copy(CAM.l);camTo.copy(CAM.p);lookTo.copy(CAM.l);
    window.__fov=baseFov()*CAM.fm*(1+CAM.fk*.01);
    window.__roll=CAM.roll+(G.shake>0?(Math.random()-.5)*G.shake*.05:0);
  }

  function cfgOf(id){
    if(id==='copycat'){
      const g=G.pc||{};const inv=c=>{try{const k=new THREE.Color(c||'#ffffff');return '#'+new THREE.Color(1-k.r,1-k.g,1-k.b).getHexString()}catch(e){return '#111827'}};
      let cc=inv(g.color);try{const k=new THREE.Color(cc);if(k.r*.3+k.g*.6+k.b*.1<.28)cc='#7c3aed'}catch(x){}
      return {color:cc,expr:'angry',hat:g.hat||'none',hs:g.hs||1,bs:g.bs||1,shirt:g.shirt||'',shirtColor:inv(g.shirtColor||'#ffffff'),pat:g.pat||'',cape:g.cape?inv(g.cape):'',ol:'#f43f5e'};
    }
    return Object.assign({hs:1,bs:1,shirt:'',shirtColor:'#ffffff'},LOOK[id]||LOOK.scribble);
  }
  function actor(id){
    if(E.actors[id])return E.actors[id];
    let a;
    if(id==='hero')a={id,f:P,hero:true,vis:true};
    else{const f=fighter(cfgOf(id));f.g.visible=false;scene.add(f.g);a={id,f,vis:false}}
    Object.assign(a,{x:0,z:0,y:0,face:0,tgt:null,goal:null,talk:false,ly:0,lyT:0,lyD:1,pose:null,al:1,alT:1,sc:1,scT:1,fly:0,flyT:0,bounce:0,walking:false,mats:[],spin:0,glow:null,aura:null,big:BIG[id]||1,shiv:0,tilt:0,vx:0,px:0,pz:0});
    const seen=new Set();a.f.g.traverse(o=>{if(o.isMesh&&o.material&&!seen.has(o.material)&&o!==a.f.sd&&o!==a.f.sh){seen.add(o.material);a.mats.push(o.material)}});
    E.actors[id]=a;return a;
  }
  const H=()=>actor('hero');
  function setAlpha(a,v){a.mats.forEach(m=>{const tr=v<.99;if(m.transparent!==tr){m.transparent=tr;m.needsUpdate=true}m.opacity=v})}
  function tickActor(a,dt,t){
    const f=a.f;if(!a.vis)return;
    a.walking=false;const ox=a.x,oz=a.z;
    if(a.goal){const g=a.goal,dx=g.x-a.x,dz=g.z-a.z,d=Math.hypot(dx,dz),st=g.v*dt*sk();
      if(d<=st+.005){a.x=g.x;a.z=g.z;const r=g.res;a.goal=null;if(r)r()}else{a.x+=dx/d*st;a.z+=dz/d*st;a.walking=true;a.face=Math.atan2(dx,dz)}}
    if(a.tgt&&!a.walking){const T=typeof a.tgt==='string'?E.actors[a.tgt]:a.tgt;if(T&&T.vis!==false){const dx=T.x-a.x,dz=T.z-a.z;if(Math.hypot(dx,dz)>.05)a.face=Math.atan2(dx,dz)}}
    f.x=f.tx=a.x;f.ry=f.g.rotation.y+wrapA(a.face+a.spin-f.g.rotation.y);
    if(!a.hero)anim(f,dt,t);
    a.vx=(a.x-a.px)/Math.max(dt,.001);a.px=a.x;a.pz=a.z;
    if(a.fly!==a.flyT){a.fly+=(a.flyT-a.fly)*Math.min(1,4*dt)}
    const bob=a.fly>.05?Math.sin(t*2.4+f.ph)*.12*Math.min(1,a.fly):0;
    f.g.position.set(a.x+(a.shiv?rnd(-1,1)*a.shiv:0),a.y+a.fly+bob,a.z);
    if(a.al!==a.alT){a.al+=(a.alT-a.al)*Math.min(1,7*dt);if(Math.abs(a.al-a.alT)<.01)a.al=a.alT}
    if(!a.hero||a.al<1)setAlpha(a,a.al);
    if(a.sc!==a.scT){a.sc+=(a.scT-a.sc)*Math.min(1,8*dt)}
    f.g.scale.setScalar(a.sc);
    if(a.walking&&!f.em){const sw=Math.sin(t*13+f.ph);f.fl.position.set(0,.16+Math.max(0,sw)*.2,.38+sw*.26);f.fr.position.set(0,.16+Math.max(0,-sw)*.2,-.38-sw*.26);f.root.position.y=Math.abs(sw)*.05;f.root.rotation.x=.08+(a.sprint?.22:0);
      if(Math.random()<dt*7)try{blob(a.x-Math.sin(a.face)*.3,.08,a.z-Math.cos(a.face)*.3,0x94a3b8,.09,.4,1)}catch(e){}}
    if(a.fly>.3&&!f.em){f.fl.position.set(.0,.0,.18+Math.sin(t*3)*.05);f.fr.position.set(.0,.0,-.18-Math.sin(t*3)*.05);f.root.rotation.x=.12+clamp(a.vx*.03,-.3,.3)}
    if(a.bounce)a.y=Math.abs(Math.sin(t*9+f.ph))*a.bounce;
    if(a.talk&&!f.em){f.head.rotation.x=Math.sin(t*11+f.ph)*.07;f.head.rotation.z=Math.sin(t*5+f.ph)*.06;f.root.rotation.x+=Math.sin(t*4)*.02;f.hl.position.y+=Math.max(0,Math.sin(t*3.2+f.ph))*.35;f.hl.position.z+=Math.sin(t*6)*.12}
    if(a.pose){const p=a.pose;if(p.hl)f.hl.position.set(p.hl[0],p.hl[1],p.hl[2]);if(p.hr)f.hr.position.set(p.hr[0],p.hr[1],p.hr[2]);if(p.head){f.head.rotation.x=p.head[0];f.head.rotation.y=p.head[1];f.head.rotation.z=p.head[2]}if(p.rx!=null)f.root.rotation.x=p.rx;if(p.rz!=null)f.root.rotation.z=p.rz;if(p.y!=null)f.root.position.y=p.y}
    if(a.ly>.005||a.lyT){a.ly+=(a.lyT-a.ly)*Math.min(1,5*dt);f.root.rotation.z=a.ly*1.45*a.lyD;f.root.position.y=a.ly*.38;if(a.ly>.4){f.hl.position.y=.5;f.hr.position.y=.5}}
    if(a.flick){f.g.visible=Math.random()>.18}
    if(a.glow){const k=.55+.45*Math.sin(t*7+f.ph),c=col(a.glow.c);f.mat.emissive.setRGB(c.r*k*(a.glow.k||1),c.g*k*(a.glow.k||1),c.b*k*(a.glow.k||1))}
    limbs(f);
    if(a.aura){a.auraT=(a.auraT||0)-dt;if(a.auraT<=0){a.auraT=a.aura.rate||.06;emit(a.x+rnd(-.35,.35),a.y+a.fly+.1,a.z+rnd(-.3,.3),{n:2,c:[a.aura.c,0xffffff],spd:.4,up:2.6,g:-1,sz:.2,life:.9});if(Math.random()<.12)ringFx(a.x,a.y+a.fly+.05,a.z,a.aura.c,1.8,.55,true)}}
  }

  /* ---- actor ops ---- */
  const show=id=>{const a=actor(id);a.vis=true;a.f.g.visible=true;return a};
  const hide=id=>{const a=actor(id);a.vis=false;a.f.g.visible=false;a.goal=null;a.aura=null;a.glow=null};
  const place=(id,x,z,y)=>{const a=actor(id);a.x=x;a.z=z;a.goal=null;a.px=x;if(y!=null)a.y=y};
  const walk=(id,x,z,v)=>{const a=actor(id);const p=new Promise((res,rej)=>{a.goal={x,z,v:v||2.6,res,rej}});p.catch(()=>{});return p};
  const look=(id,t)=>{actor(id).tgt=t};
  const face=(id,ang)=>{const a=actor(id);a.tgt=null;a.face=ang};
  function gest(id,name,d){const a=actor(id),f=a.f;f.em={n:name,t:0,d:d||1.2};f.punch=0;f.pend=null;f.block=false}
  const hop=(id,h,ms)=>{const a=actor(id);return tw(ms||520,u=>{a.y=Math.sin(u*Math.PI)*h}).then(()=>{a.y=0}).catch(()=>{})};
  const lie=(id,on,dir)=>{const a=actor(id);a.lyT=on?1:0;if(dir)a.lyD=dir};
  const flyTo=(id,h)=>{actor(id).flyT=h};
  const aura=(id,c,rate)=>{actor(id).aura=c?{c,rate:rate||.06}:null};

  const scaleTo=(id,s)=>{actor(id).scT=s};

    a.sprint=false;sfx('whoosh');
  }
  E.ops={show,hide,place,walk,look,face,gest,hop,lie,flyTo,aura,glow,fade,scaleTo,pose,spinBy,dash,actor,H};
  const mainFoe=()=>Object.values(E.actors).find(a=>a.vis&&!a.hero&&a.foe);

  /* ================= master tick ================= */
  let tickT=0,lastNow=performance.now();
  E.tick=function(dt,t){
    const now=performance.now();let rdt=Math.min(.1,(now-lastNow)/1000);lastNow=now;
    if(!E.on||E.susp){ptick(rdt);return}
    E.rdt=rdt;E.time+=rdt;
    const dts=rdt*sk();
    for(let i=E.tws.length-1;i>=0;i--){const e=E.tws[i];e.t+=dts/e.d;const u=Math.min(1,e.t);try{e.fn(e.e(u),dts)}catch(er){}if(u>=1){E.tws.splice(i,1);e.res()}}
    for(const id in E.actors)tickActor(E.actors[id],dt,t);
    for(let i=E.fxs.length-1;i>=0;i--){const e=E.fxs[i];e.t+=dts/e.d;const u=Math.min(1,e.t);try{e.fn(u,dts)}catch(er){}if(u>=1){scene.remove(e.o);disposeObj(e.o);E.fxs.splice(i,1);if(e.fin)e.fin()}}
    for(const p of E.props)if(p.upd)p.upd(dts);
    for(const a of E.amb){a.acc+=dts*a.rate;while(a.acc>=1){a.acc-=1;a.fn()}}

    camTick(rdt);
    G.over=true;G.paused=false;
    if(E.moodL){E.moodL.intensity=E.moodBase+Math.sin(t*3)*.25}
  };
  const oRun=window.ARNrun;
  window.ARNrun=function(dt,t){if(oRun)oRun.apply(this,arguments);else ARN.forEach(fn=>fn(dt,t));try{E.tick(dt,t)}catch(e){console.warn('S2TICK',e&&e.message)}};

    typing=setInterval(()=>{i++;$$('s2t').innerHTML=fmt(fullText.slice(0,i));if(i%3===0){try{if(snd())tone('square',(window.__vb||520)+Math.random()*(window.__vr||180),(window.__vb||520)*.8,.025,.04)}catch(e){}}if(i>=fullText.length){clearInterval(typing);typing=null;const pc=document.querySelector('.s2pc .s2in');if(pc)pc.classList.remove('dfTalk')}},22);
    try{await tapWait()}finally{sp.talk=false;if(typing){clearInterval(typing);typing=null}}
  };
  E.hideBox=()=>{$$('s2b').classList.add('hide');$$('s2p').innerHTML=''};

  E.mainFoe=mainFoe;E.BIG=BIG;E.cfgOf=cfgOf;E.setAlpha=setAlpha;E.invert=invert;

      case'ink':{place(id,hx,hz);a.al=a.alT=0;a.tgt=null;sfx('ink');
        for(let i=0;i<46;i++){const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(1,8,8),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:i%5===0?col(c):0x0b0618,transparent:true}));const p0=V(hx+rnd(-5,5),rnd(.3,5),hz+rnd(-3,3)),p1=V(hx,rnd(.5,2),hz),sz=rnd(.07,.2),dl=rnd(0,.35);m.position.copy(p0);
          (function(m,p0,p1,sz,dl){const g=m;scene.add(g);const ent={o:g,t:0,d:1,fn:u=>{const k=Math.max(0,(u-dl)/(1-dl));m.position.lerpVectors(p0,p1,eO(k));m.scale.setScalar(sz*(1-k*.6))},fin:null};E.fxs.push(ent)})(m,p0,p1,sz,dl)}

      case'mirror':{place(id,hx,hz-1.2);a.al=a.alT=0;a.tgt=null;
        const g=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2.6,3.6),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xbaf2ff,transparent:true,opacity:.5,side:2,depthWrite:false,blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending}));g.position.set(hx,1.8,hz-.9);scene.add(g);

        a.al=a.alT=1;await walk(id,hx,hz,2.4);break}

      case'fall':{place(id,hx,hz);a.y=9;a.tgt=null;await tw(2200,u=>{a.y=9*(1-eIO(u));a.tilt=Math.sin(u*6)*.2});a.y=0;E.emit(hx,.2,hz,{n:12,c:0xffffff,spd:3,up:.4,g:6,sz:.3,life:.8});break}
      case'pop':{place(id,hx,hz);a.sc=a.scT=.01;a.tgt=null;sfx('pop');await tw(420,u=>{a.sc=a.scT=Math.max(.01,E.eB(u))});a.sc=a.scT=1;E.emit(hx,1,hz,{n:12,c:[col(c),0xffffff],spd:3,sz:.3,life:.7});break}
    }
    a.tgt=H();return a;
  }
  async function exit(id,style,o){
    const a=actor(id);if(!a.vis)return;const x=a.x,z=a.z,c=(E.CAST[id]||{}).c||'#ffffff';
    switch(style||'walk'){
      case'fade':a.alT=0;E.emit(x,1.2,z,{n:16,c:[col(c),0xffffff],spd:2,up:1,g:-.5,sz:.3,life:1.2});await w(1000);break;
      case'warp':F.pillar(x,z,col(c),16,1.6,1);F2.flash('#fff',300);sfx('zap');a.alT=0;await w(500);break;
      case'ink':sfx('ink');F.inkSplat(x,.8,z,[0x0b0618,col(c)],40);await tw(520,u=>{a.sc=a.scT=1-eO(u)*.97});break;
      case'blast':{sfx('whoosh');F2.speed(500);const y0=a.y;await tw(700,u=>{a.y=y0+eI(u)*14;E.emit(a.x,a.y,a.z,{n:1,c:0xffffff,spd:.3,sz:.5,life:.4})});break}
      case'sink':{a.alT=0;await tw(900,u=>{a.y=-2.4*eO(u)});break}
      default:a.tgt=null;await walk(id,x+(x<0?-1:1)*9,z,o&&o.v||4.2);
    }
    hide(id);a.entered=false;a.sc=a.scT=1;a.y=0;a.al=a.alT=1;
  }

  /* ---------------- holograms (portrait projected in 3D) ---------------- */
  function holo(who,expr,x,y,z,size,c){
    return new Promise(res=>{
      try{
        const svg=DFACE.make(who,expr).replace('<svg ','<svg width="256" height="370" '),img=new Image();
        img.onload=()=>{
          const cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=256;cv.height=370;const x2=cv.getContext('2d');x2.drawImage(img,0,0,256,370);
          x2.globalCompositeOperation='source-atop';x2.fillStyle=(c||'#22d3ee')+'66';x2.fillRect(0,0,256,370);
          x2.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';x2.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.28)';for(let yy=0;yy<370;yy+=5)x2.fillRect(0,yy,256,2);
          const sp=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({map:new THREE.CanvasTexture(cv),transparent:true,depthWrite:false,opacity:.0}));
          sp.scale.set(size*.69,size,1);sp.position.set(x,y,z);sp.renderOrder=45;scene.add(sp);
          const o={o:sp,upd:dt=>{o.t=(o.t||0)+dt;sp.material.opacity=Math.min(o.fadeTo==null?.9:o.fadeTo,sp.material.opacity+dt*2)*(Math.random()>.06?1:.5);sp.position.y=y+Math.sin(o.t*1.5)*.08}};
          E.props.push(o);
          res({kill:()=>{const i=E.props.indexOf(o);if(i>=0)E.props.splice(i,1);scene.remove(sp)},sp,o});
        };
        img.onerror=()=>res({kill(){}});
        img.src='data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,'+encodeURIComponent(svg);
      }catch(e){res({kill(){}})}
    });
  }

  /* ---------------- fog / light helpers ---------------- */
  function fogTo(c,den,ms){const f=scene.fog;if(!f)return Promise.resolve();const c0=f.color.clone(),d0=f.density,c1=col(c);return tw(ms,u=>{f.color.copy(c0).lerp(c1,u);if(den!=null)f.density=d0+(den-d0)*u;if(scene.background&&scene.background.copy)scene.background.copy(f.color)})}
  function moodTo(c,k){if(!E.moodL)return;const c1=col(c),c0=E.moodL.color.clone();if(k!=null)E.moodBase=k;tw(500,u=>{E.moodL&&E.moodL.color.copy(c0).lerp(c1,u)}).catch(()=>{})}

  /* ---------------- dialogue direction ---------------- */
  const GL=['wave','flex','hype','shrug','bow','karate','rage','laugh','facepalm','clap','salute'];
  function pickGest(t,hero){
    const L=t.replace(/[^A-Za-z]/g,''),up=L.length>6&&L.replace(/[^A-Z]/g,'').length/L.length>.5;
    if(/\bha(\s?ha)+\b|hehe|honk/i.test(t))return'laugh';
    if(/sorry|forgive|please|thank/i.test(t))return'bow';
    if(up&&/!/.test(t))return pick(['rage','flex','karate']);
    if(/\?/.test(t))return'shrug';
    if(/together|friend|fix|come with|did it|won|strong|believe/i.test(t))return'hype';
    if(/hello|hi\b|hey/i.test(t))return'wave';
    return Math.random()<.18?pick(['clap','salute','flex']):null;
  }
  function autoShot(who,force){
    const n=E.shotN++,sp=who==='narr'?null:E.actors[who],f=fs();
    const vis=Object.values(E.actors).filter(a=>a.vis);
    const portrait=innerWidth<innerHeight,dz=portrait?1.35:1;
    if(!sp||!sp.vis){
      const xs=vis.map(a=>a.x),cx=xs.length?(Math.min(...xs)+Math.max(...xs))/2:0,span=xs.length?Math.max(...xs)-Math.min(...xs):0;
      const r=Math.max(7.2,span*1.05+5.2)*dz,d=n%2?1:-1;
      E.cam.orbit(V(cx,.8,0),r,r*.86,2.2,3.1,d*.5,d*.16,7000,{e:u=>u*(2-u)});E.lastKind='wide';return;
    }
    const k=sp.big,hy=1.45+.12*(k-1)+sp.fly+sp.y,head=V(sp.x,hy-.42*k,sp.z),S0=sp.x<0?1:-1;
    const evil=!!sp.foe,hero=!!sp.hero;
    const pool=evil?['low','dutch','orbit','push','crane']:hero?['push','orbit','crane','ots','pull','track']:['push','ots','orbit','track','pull'];
    let kind=force||pool[(n+Math.floor(Math.random()*3))%pool.length];
    if(kind===E.lastKind&&!force)kind=pool[(pool.indexOf(kind)+1)%pool.length];
    E.lastKind=kind;
    const len=(E.CAST&&0)||0;
    const ms=clamp(((E.curText||'').length)*52+2200,3600,8200);
    const ad=(v,a,b,c)=>v.clone().add(V(a,b,c));
    let sh;
    switch(kind){
      case'pull':sh={p0:ad(head,S0*.9,.05,3.0*k*dz),p1:ad(head,S0*1.9,.45,5.7*k*dz),l0:ad(head,0,0,0),l1:ad(head,0,-.15,0),f0:.95,f1:1,r0:0,r1:0};break;
      case'low':sh={p0:ad(head,S0*.9,-1.0*k,3.7*k*dz),p1:ad(head,S0*.4,-.85*k,2.8*k*dz),l0:ad(head,0,.35*k,0),l1:ad(head,0,.5*k,0),f0:.9,f1:.96,r0:S0*.07,r1:0};break;
      case'dutch':sh={p0:ad(head,S0*1.8,.3,5.8*k*dz),p1:ad(head,S0*1.0,.0,3.4*k*dz),l0:ad(head,0,-.1,0),l1:ad(head,0,0,0),f0:1,f1:.95,r0:.16*S0,r1:.1*S0};break;
      case'crane':sh={p0:ad(head,S0*1.3,-1.2,4.4*k*dz),p1:ad(head,S0*.8,1.3,3.8*k*dz),l0:ad(head,0,.1,0),l1:ad(head,0,-.1,0),f0:1,f1:1.06,r0:0,r1:S0*.03};break;
      case'track':sh={p0:V(head.x-S0*2.6,hy+.15,sp.z+4.8*dz),p1:V(head.x+S0*.6,hy+.1,sp.z+4.2*dz),l0:ad(head,-S0*1.4,-.1,0),l1:ad(head,0,0,0),f0:1,f1:1,r0:0,r1:0};break;
      case'ots':{
        const li=hero?(foes()[0]||E.actors.scribble||sp):H();
        if(li&&li!==sp&&li.vis){const dx=sp.x-li.x,dz2=sp.z-li.z,d=Math.hypot(dx,dz2)||1,ux=dx/d,uz=dz2/d;
          sh={p0:V(li.x-ux*2.7-uz*.2,2.0+li.fly,li.z-uz*2.7+1.9),p1:V(li.x-ux*3.1+uz*.6,1.95+li.fly,li.z-uz*3.1+2.2),l0:ad(head,0,0,0),l1:ad(head,0,.02,0),f0:.95,f1:.98,r0:0,r1:S0*.02};break}
      }
      default:
        if(kind==='orbit'){E.cam.orbit(head,4.4*k*dz,3.3*k*dz,hy+.1,hy+.45,S0*1.05,S0*.28,ms,{e:u=>u*(2-u)});return}
        sh={p0:ad(head,S0*1.9,.35,5.8*k*dz),p1:ad(head,S0*1.05,.05,3.4*k*dz),l0:ad(head,0,-.1,0),l1:ad(head,-S0*.1,0,0),f0:1,f1:.96,r0:0,r1:0};
    }
    const prevX=E.lastSpeakX;E.lastSpeakX=sp.x;
    const whip=prevX!=null&&Math.sign(prevX)!==Math.sign(sp.x)&&Math.random()<.75;
    const cp0=E.cam.get().p.clone(),cl0=E.cam.get().l.clone();
    if(whip){F2.blur(150);E.cam.fovPunch(16)}
    E.cam.rig(u=>{
      const C=E.cam.get();let tt=u;
      C.p.lerpVectors(sh.p0,sh.p1,tt);C.l.lerpVectors(sh.l0,sh.l1,tt);C.fm=lerp(sh.f0,sh.f1,tt);C.roll=lerp(sh.r0,sh.r1,tt);
      if(whip&&u<.14){const k2=eO(u/.14);C.p.copy(cp0).lerp(C.p,k2);C.l.copy(cl0).lerp(C.l,k2)}
    },ms,u=>u*(2-u));
  }
  async function sayLine(b,ci){
    const who=b.w,o=b.o||{},sp=who==='narr'?null:actor(who);
    if(sp&&!sp.vis&&!sp.hero&&!o.noenter){await enter(who,o.enter||'walk')}
    E.curText=b.t;
    const hero=H();
    Object.values(E.actors).forEach(a=>{if(a.vis&&a.id!==who&&!a.goal&&!a.pose&&!a.nolook&&!a.hero)a.tgt=sp||a.tgt});
    if(sp){
      if(sp.hero){const fo=foes()[0]||E.actors.scribble;hero.tgt=fo&&fo.vis?fo:null}
      else if(!sp.goal&&!sp.nolook&&sp.vis)sp.tgt=hero;
      const g=sp.vis?pickGest(b.t,sp.hero):null;if(g&&!sp.goal&&!sp.f.em&&!o.nog)setTimeout(()=>{if(E.on&&!E.susp)gest(who,g,g==='laugh'||g==='bow'?1.5:1.2)},380/(E.skip?40:1));
      const c=(E.CAST[who]||{}).c;if(c)moodTo(c);
    }else{hero.tgt=null}
    if(o.fx){try{const r=o.fx(S);if(r&&r.catch)r.catch(e=>{if(e!==STALE)console.warn('S2FX',e&&e.message)})}catch(e){console.warn('S2FX',e&&e.message)}}
    if(o.cam!==false)autoShot(who,o.cam);
    await E.say(who,b.t,o);
    if(sp)sp.talk=false;
  }

  function mkBot(def){
    const raw=typeof def.bot==='function'?def.bot():def.bot;
    const b=Object.assign({s2:true},raw);b.m=Object.assign({},raw.m);return b;
  }
  function runMatch(def,diff){
    return new Promise(res=>{
      const sel=$$('bot'),as=$$('arena'),ds=$$('diff');
      const bot=mkBot(def);
      const keep={a:as.value,d:ds.value,n:G.need,b:sel.value};
      const key=def.arena||(stageCfg&&stageCfg.key)||'cyber';
      const wasOwned=!!(W.own&&W.own['arena:'+key]);if(!wasOwned)W.own['arena:'+key]=1;
      const opt=document.createElement('option');BOTS.push(bot);opt.value=String(BOTS.length-1);sel.appendChild(opt);sel.value=opt.value;
      as.value=key;ds.value=diff||def.diff||'normal';G.need=2;G.tour=false;
      fightRes=res;E.fight=true;
      try{startMatch()}finally{BOTS.pop();opt.remove();sel.value=keep.b;as.value=keep.a;ds.value=keep.d;G.need=keep.n;if(!wasOwned)delete W.own['arena:'+key]}
      try{P.sup=Math.min(70,SV.u*8+(def.sup||0)+(SV.bless?15:0))}catch(e){}
    });
  }
  function suspend(){
    E.keepFilter=E.baseFilter;E.susp=true;E.stopMusic();E.resetCam();E.killFx();E.amb=[];
    Object.values(E.actors).forEach(a=>{if(!a.hero){a.wasVis=a.vis;a.f.g.visible=false}});
    ui.classList.remove('on');ui.classList.remove('lb');

  }
  function resume(){
    if(!stageCfg)return;
    prep(stageCfg.key,stageCfg.o);
    E.susp=false;E.fight=false;if(E.keepFilter)F2.tint(E.keepFilter,0);
    Object.values(E.actors).forEach(a=>{if(!a.hero&&a.wasVis){a.f.g.visible=true;a.vis=true}});
    const hh=H();hh.vis=true;
  }
  function lossMenu(assist,tip){
    return new Promise(res=>{
      try{E.hideBox()}catch(e){}
      const k=$$('s2c');k.style.setProperty('--c','#f87171');
      const tips=['Block right as a punch lands to PARRY: it cancels their swing and fills your Super.','Every 3rd punch is an UPPERCUT. Blocking will not stop it, so dodge it.','Dodge (Space) and jump (W) both avoid a Super.','Bosses read your habits. Vary your timing instead of spamming J.','Hit them while they recover from a missed swing.'];
      k.innerHTML='<small>DEFEATED</small><h1>TRY AGAIN?</h1><div class="sub">💡 '+(tip||pick(tips))+'</div><div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:center;margin-top:18px;opacity:1"><button class="btn go" id="s2R">↻ RETRY</button>'+(assist?'':'<button class="btn" id="s2E" style="background:#0e7490">😊 EASIER</button>')+'<button class="btn" id="s2L" style="background:#475569">LEAVE</button></div>';
      k.classList.add('on');ui.classList.add('on');
      const d=v=>{k.classList.remove('on');res(v)};
      $$('s2R').onclick=()=>d('retry');if($$('s2E'))$$('s2E').onclick=()=>d('easier');$$('s2L').onclick=()=>d('leave');
    });
  }
  const EASE={extreme:'hard',hard:'normal',normal:'easy',easy:'easy'};
  async function doFight(b,ci){
    E.skip=false;
    if(b.pre)await b.pre(S);
    await E.vsSplash('hero',b.vs||b.id,b.name,b.tag,b.vsc);
    let assist=0,first=true;
    while(!E.abort){
      suspend();
      const diff=assist?EASE[b.diff]:b.diff;
      const won=await runMatch(b,diff);
      if(won===null){E.abort=true;return}
      resume();
      if(won){
        const flawless=E.lastHp>=100;
        if(flawless)give('s2perfect');
        if(b.ach)give(b.ach);
        if(b.id&&b.foeId){const a=E.actors[b.foeId];if(a&&a.vis){a.ly=a.lyT=1;a.lyD=1}}
        return won;
      }
      SV.lost++;save();
      const ch=await lossMenu(assist);
      if(ch==='leave'){E.abort=true;return}
      if(ch==='easier')assist=1;
      first=false;
    }
  }

  /* ---------------- chapter runner ---------------- */
  let running=false;
  async function playChapter(ci,from){
    const c=CUR.CH[ci];if(!c)return;
    running=true;E.abort=false;E.skip=false;E.on=true;E.susp=false;E.fight=false;E.shotN=0;E.lastKind='';E.lastSpeakX=null;
    E.fs=Math.min(1,Math.max(.72,(innerWidth/innerHeight)/1.15));
    E.userFov=window.__fov||null;
    SV.pos={c:ci,b:from||0};save();
    try{
      for(const id of Object.keys(E.actors)){const a=E.actors[id];if(a.hero){Object.assign(a,{fly:0,flyT:0,pose:null,aura:null,glow:null,ly:0,lyT:0,spin:0,tilt:0,sc:1,scT:1,al:1,alT:1,y:0,bounce:0,talk:false,goal:null,tgt:null});try{P.mat.emissive.setRGB(0,0,0)}catch(e){}}else{scene.remove(a.f.g);delete E.actors[id]}}
      E.killFx();E.baseFilter='';try{ren.domElement.style.filter=''}catch(e){}
      const lastCp=from?[...c.beats.slice(0,from)].reverse().find(x=>x.k==='cp'):null;
      const rs=lastCp&&lastCp.st?lastCp:null;
      prep(rs?rs.st.key:c.arena,rs?rs.st.o:c);
      setup(from?((lastCp&&lastCp.add)||c.cast||[]):[]);
      if(from){if(lastCp&&lastCp.lie)lastCp.lie.forEach(id=>lie(id,true,1));if(c.flyAll)['hero',...((lastCp&&lastCp.add)||c.cast||[])].forEach(id=>{const q=actor(id);q.fly=q.flyT=c.flyAll});if(c.tint)F2.tint(c.tint,0)}
      const wp=E.cam;S.wide(0);
      if(!from){ui.classList.remove('lb');await E.card('CHAPTER '+(ci+1),c.title,c.sub,c.color,3000);E.letterbox(true)}
      if(E.wipeEl)F2.unwipe(900);
      for(let bi=from||0;bi<c.beats.length&&!E.abort;bi++){
        const b=c.beats[bi];SV.pos={c:ci,b:bi};
        if(b.k==='fight'){save()}
        if(b.k==='say')await sayLine(b,ci);
        else if(b.k==='run'){E.hideBox();await b.fn(S)}
        else if(b.k==='fight'){await doFight(b,ci)}
        else if(b.k==='cp'){SV.pos={c:ci,b:bi+1};save()}
        if(E.skip&&c.beats[bi+1]&&(c.beats[bi+1].k==='fight'||c.beats[bi+1].k==='card'))E.skip=false;
      }
    }catch(e){if(e!==STALE){console.warn('S2CHAPTER',e&&e.message||e,e&&e.stack)}E.abort=true}
    if(E.abort){endAll();return}
    /* chapter complete */
    SV.u=Math.max(SV.u,ci+1);SV.pos=ci+1<CUR.CH.length?{c:ci+1,b:0}:null;const last=ci===CUR.CH.length-1;
    if(last){SV.done=true}
    save();
    try{earn(c.reward+(last?CUR.bonus:0))}catch(e){}
    try{give(CUR.pre+(ci+1));if(last){give(CUR.done);if(SV.lost===0)give(CUR.flaw)}}catch(e){}
    try{
      E.skip=false;
      await E.card(last?CUR.name+' COMPLETE':'CHAPTER CLEAR','+'+(c.reward+(last?CUR.bonus:0))+' 🪙',last?CUR.end:'',c.color,2600);
      if(!E.abort&&!last&&SV.cont!==false){return playChapter(ci+1,0)}
    }catch(e){}
    endAll();
  }
  function endAll(){
    running=false;
    try{E.cancelAll()}catch(e){}
    E.on=false;E.susp=false;E.fight=false;E.stopMusic();E.killFx();E.amb=[];E.resetCam();
    Object.values(E.actors).forEach(a=>{if(!a.hero){scene.remove(a.f.g)}else{a.lyT=a.ly=0;a.pose=null;a.aura=null;a.glow=null;a.fly=a.flyT=0;a.sc=a.scT=1;a.spin=0;P.root.rotation.set(0,0,0);P.root.position.y=0;P.g.rotation.z=0;E.setAlpha(a,1);P.g.scale.setScalar(1);P.g.visible=true;try{P.mat.emissive.setRGB(0,0,0)}catch(e){}}});
    E.actors={};clearMood();
    ui.classList.remove('on');ui.classList.remove('lb');$$('s2c').classList.remove('on');$$('s2vs').classList.remove('on');$$('s2p').innerHTML='';
    document.querySelectorAll('.s2wp').forEach(x=>x.remove());E.wipeEl=null;G.ts=1;
    try{ren.domElement.style.filter='';ren.domElement.style.transform=''}catch(e){}
    try{toLobby()}catch(e){}
    try{if(CUR.cleanup)CUR.cleanup()}catch(e){}
    CUR.menu();
  }

  /* ---------------- menu ---------------- */
  document.head.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`<style>
  #s2m .ch{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:10px;border:1px solid #334155;border-radius:12px;margin:6px 0;background:#0f172a;text-align:left}
  #s2m .ch.lk{opacity:.45}#s2m .ch b{flex:1}
  #s2m .box{max-height:86vh;max-height:86dvh;overflow-y:auto;-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch;overscroll-behavior:contain;touch-action:pan-y}#s2m{align-items:center}
  #bStory2.lk{filter:grayscale(.9) brightness(.7)}
  #bStory2.new{animation:s2new 1.2s ease-in-out infinite}
  @keyframes s2new{0%,100%{box-shadow:0 0 0 0 #f43f5e00}50%{box-shadow:0 0 18px 4px #f43f5ecc}}
  </style>`);
  document.body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<div id="s2m" class="modal hide" style="z-index:320"><div class="box featBox" style="max-width:480px"><h2 class="mk" style="color:#f43f5e;margin:0 0 4px">📖 STORY PART 2 · THE INKWELL WAR</h2><div style="font-size:13px;opacity:.85;margin-bottom:8px">7 chapters · 12 fights · fully animated cinematics. Progress saves automatically at every checkpoint.</div><div id="s2List"></div><div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:10px;flex-wrap:wrap"><button class="btn go" id="s2Go" style="flex:1">▶ START</button><button class="btn" id="s2New" style="background:#7f1d1d">↻ NEW STORY</button><button class="btn" id="s2X" style="background:#475569">CLOSE</button></div></div></div>');
  function openMenu(){
    CUR=window.S2.C2;CKEY=CUR.key;SV=load();if(window.__TESTBUILD){SV.u=Math.max(SV.u,CH.length-1)}
    $$('s2List').innerHTML=CH.map((c,i)=>{const open=i<=SV.u,done=i<SV.u;return '<div class="ch'+(open?'':' lk')+'"><span style="font-size:26px">'+(done?'✅':(open?'📖':'🔒'))+'</span><b>Ch. '+(i+1)+' · '+c.title+'</b>'+(open?'<button class="btn" data-ch="'+i+'" style="padding:6px 12px">'+(done?'REPLAY':'PLAY')+'</button>':'')+'</div>'}).join('');
    $$('s2Go').textContent=SV.done&&!SV.pos?'▶ REPLAY FINAL CHAPTER':(SV.pos&&(SV.pos.c>0||SV.pos.b>0)?'▶ CONTINUE (Ch. '+(SV.pos.c+1)+')':'▶ START STORY');
    $$('s2m').classList.remove('hide');
  }
  function begin(ci,b){$$('s2m').classList.add('hide');$$('lobby').classList.add('hide');SV.cont=true;if(window.SENSEI&&SENSEI.open)SENSEI.close();playChapter(ci,b)}
  $$('s2Go').onclick=()=>{if(SV.pos)begin(SV.pos.c,SV.pos.b);else if(SV.done)begin(CH.length-1,0);else{SV.pos={c:0,b:0};save();begin(0,0)}};
  $$('s2New').onclick=()=>{if(SV.u>0&&!confirm('Start Part 2 over? Your saved Part 2 progress will be reset.'))return;SV=Object.assign({},DEF,{pos:{c:0,b:0}});save();begin(0,0)};
  $$('s2X').onclick=()=>$$('s2m').classList.add('hide');
  $$('s2List').onclick=e=>{const b=e.target.closest('[data-ch]');if(!b)return;begin(+b.dataset.ch,0)};
  $$('s2Skip').onclick=()=>{E.skip=true;E.advance();E.advance()};
  $$('s2Quit').onclick=()=>{E.abort=true;E.cancelAll()};
  /* ---------------- lobby button ---------------- */
  function enterFromStory(){
    if(G.online||NET.role){toast('Story mode is offline only');return}
    if(!s1done()){toast('🔒 Finish all 8 chapters of Story Mode to unlock Part 2');return}
    if(!SV.seen){SV.seen=true;save();give('s2unlock')}
    openMenu();
  }
  function refresh(){}
    window.S2.open=openMenu;window.S2.enterFromStory=enterFromStory;window.S2.play=begin;window.S2.unlocked=s1done;
})();

</script>

<script>
/* ===== STORY PART 2 — chapters 2 & 3 (+ shared set-piece helpers) ===== */
(()=>{
  const X=window.S2;if(!X)return;
  const {S,CH}=X,{V,rnd,pick,lerp,eO,eI,eIO}=S,F=S.F,F2=S.F2,E=S.E;
  const L=(who,t,o)=>({k:'say',w:who,t,o});
  const D=(s,o)=>{const i=s.indexOf('|');return L(s.slice(0,i),s.slice(i+1),o)};
  const R=fn=>({k:'run',fn});
  const CP=(add,st,lie)=>({k:'cp',add,st,lie});
  const FT=def=>Object.assign({k:'fight'},def);
  const N=(t,o)=>L('narr',t,o);
  const outEase=u=>1-Math.pow(1-u,3);
  const COLS=[0xf43f5e,0xfacc15,0x22c55e,0x38bdf8,0xa855f7,0xf472b6];

  /* ---------- shared helpers (also used by later chapters) ---------- */
  const H={
    async fireworks(s,n,gap){for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const x=rnd(-6,6),y=rnd(4,8),z=rnd(-5,-1),c=pick(COLS);E.emit(x,y,z,{n:46,c:[c,0xffffff],spd:6.5,g:3,sz:.3,life:1.5,drag:.6});F.ringFx(x,y,z,c,3,.6,false,true);s.sfx('pop');await s.w(gap||260)}},
    fireRing(s,x,z,r,col){const o=new THREE.Object3D();s.scene.add(o);const p={o,upd:dt=>{for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const a=Math.random()*Math.PI*2;E.emit(x+Math.cos(a)*r,0,z+Math.sin(a)*r,{n:1,c:[0xf97316,0xfbbf24,col||0xef4444],spd:.5,up:2.6,g:-2,sz:.55,life:1.1})}}};E.props.push(p);return p},
    shafts(s,n,color){for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.3,1.9,16,16,1,true),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:color||0x7dd3fc,transparent:true,opacity:.07,depthWrite:false,side:2,blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending}));m.position.set(-9+i*(18/Math.max(1,n-1)),8,-3-Math.random()*2);m.rotation.z=.25+Math.random()*.15;s.scene.add(m);E.props.push({o:m,upd:dt=>{const t=performance.now();m.material.opacity=.06+Math.sin(t/900+i)*.025;m.rotation.z+=Math.sin(t/2000+i)*.0004}})}},
    spiral(s,n,cx,cz,speedUp){for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const m=new THREE.Mesh(E.GEO.plane,new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({map:E.TX.page,side:2,transparent:true,opacity:1}));let a=rnd(0,6.28),r=rnd(1.5,5.5),y=rnd(.2,2.6);const sp=rnd(.5,1.4),up=rnd(.8,2.2)*(speedUp||1),sc=rnd(.5,.95);m.scale.set(sc,sc*1.3,1);s.scene.add(m);let age=0;E.props.push({o:m,upd:dt=>{age+=dt;a+=sp*dt;y+=up*dt;r+=dt*.15;m.position.set(cx+Math.cos(a)*r,y,cz+Math.sin(a)*r-1);m.rotation.set(a*.6,a,a*.3);m.material.opacity=Math.min(1,age*1.5)*Math.max(0,1-Math.max(0,y-9)/4)}})}},
    async lightning(s,x,z,c){F2.flash('#fff',160);const top=V(x+rnd(-2,2),11,z+rnd(-1,1));F.bolt(top,V(x,0,z),c||0xc4b5fd,.5,10);s.shake(.35);s.sfx('zap')},
    draw(s,pts,delay,c){return (async()=>{for(const p of pts){E.emit(p[0],p[1],p[2],{n:5,c:[c||0xfde047,0xffffff],spd:.4,sz:.28,life:1.1});await s.w(delay||40)}})()},
    ring3(s,x,y,z,c,n){for(let i=0;i<(n||3);i++)setTimeout(()=>F.ringFx(x,y,z,c,4+i*2,.9,false,true),i*160/(E.skip?40:1))}
  };

<script>
/* ===== STORY PART 2 — chapters 4 & 5 ===== */
(()=>{
  const X=window.S2;if(!X)return;
  const {S,CH,H}=X,{V,rnd,pick,lerp,eO,eI,eIO}=S,F=S.F,F2=S.F2,E=S.E;
  const L=(who,t,o)=>({k:'say',w:who,t,o});
  const D=(s,o)=>{const i=s.indexOf('|');return L(s.slice(0,i),s.slice(i+1),o)};
  const R=fn=>({k:'run',fn});
  const CP=(add,st,lie)=>({k:'cp',add,st,lie});
  const FT=def=>Object.assign({k:'fight'},def);
  const N=(t,o)=>L('narr',t,o);
  const outEase=u=>1-Math.pow(1-u,3);

  /* =====================================================================
     CHAPTER 4 — THE DOJO OF A THOUSAND STROKES
     ===================================================================== */
  CH.push({title:'THE DOJO OF A THOUSAND STROKES',sub:'Some lessons are only learned twice.',color:'#f59e0b',arena:'dojo',mood:'#ffd28a',music:'dojo',amb:'petals',reward:260,cast:['scribble','blot','pixel'],beats:[
    R(async s=>{
      s.setup(['scribble','blot','pixel']);s.music('sad');s.moodTo('#ffd9a0',.8);
      const a=s.actor('scribble');s.pose('scribble',{rx:.35,y:-.12,head:[.3,0,0]});
      s.cam.cut(V(a.x+.9,.6,a.z+2.3),V(a.x,.5,a.z),.88,.1);
      s.cam.to(V(.5,2.3,7.6),V(-1.4,.9,0),5600,{fm:1,roll:0,e:outEase});
      await s.w(3000);s.pose('scribble',null);await s.w(2600);
    }),
    D('scribble|My old dojo. Ten thousand brushstrokes were practiced on that floor. A hundred students. Only one of them ever surpassed me.',{e:'sad'}),
    D('hero|Quill.'),
    D('scribble|Quill. Yes. Let me show you. Sit down. Close your eyes.',{e:'sad'}),
    R(async s=>{
      /* flashback: sepia + vignette, allies fade out, young Sensei and Quill appear */
      s.sfx('chime');F2.invert(160);F2.tint('sepia(.9) contrast(1.12) saturate(.85)',900);F2.vign('#78350f',3000);
      s.music('sad');
      ['hero','blot','pixel'].forEach(id=>s.fade(id,0));
      await s.w(1000);['blot','pixel'].forEach(id=>s.hide(id));
      s.place('scribble',-1.0,0);s.scaleTo('scribble',.86);s.actor('scribble').tgt=null;s.actor('scribble').face=Math.PI/2;
      s.cam.cut(V(-3.2,1.5,6.2),V(-.2,.9,0),1,0);s.cam.to(V(-1.5,1.6,5.0),V(.4,.9,0),3500,{e:eIO});
      await s.w(600);
    }),
    D('quill|Master! LOOK! I drew the Great Wave. Exactly like the scroll. Every stroke!',{e:'happy',enter:'walk'}),
    D('scribble|It is perfect, Quill. Perfect... and it is not yours.',{e:'worry'}),
    D('quill|But it is BETTER than the scroll! Why does it not count?',{e:'angry'}),
    D('scribble|Because nobody will remember who drew it. Draw me one crooked line of your own, and I will remember it forever.',{e:'happy'}),

<script>
/* ===== STORY PART 2 — chapters 6 & 7 (the finale) ===== */
(()=>{
  const X=window.S2;if(!X)return;
  const {S,CH,H}=X,{V,rnd,pick,lerp,eO,eI,eIO}=S,F=S.F,F2=S.F2,E=S.E;
  const L=(who,t,o)=>({k:'say',w:who,t,o});
  const D=(s,o)=>{const i=s.indexOf('|');return L(s.slice(0,i),s.slice(i+1),o)};
  const R=fn=>({k:'run',fn});
  const CP=(add,st,lie)=>({k:'cp',add,st,lie});
  const FT=def=>Object.assign({k:'fight'},def);
  const N=(t,o)=>L('narr',t,o);
  const outEase=u=>1-Math.pow(1-u,3);
  const sk=()=>E.skip?40:1;

  /* ---------- helpers ---------- */
  function emojiSprite(ch,size){
    const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=128;const x=c.getContext('2d');x.font='92px serif';x.textAlign='center';x.textBaseline='middle';x.fillText(ch,64,70);
    const sp=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({map:new THREE.CanvasTexture(c),transparent:true,depthTest:false}));sp.scale.set(size,size,1);sp.renderOrder=50;S.scene.add(sp);E.props.push({o:sp});return sp;
  }
  function throne(x,z){
    const g=new THREE.Group();
    for(let i=0;i<11;i++){const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(.22,5.6,8),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:i%2?0xf8fafc:0x1e1b4b}));const a=(i/10-.5)*2.5;m.position.set(Math.sin(a)*2.3,2.7+Math.cos(a)*.35,-Math.cos(a)*.7);m.rotation.z=-a*.55;g.add(m)}
    const base=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(2.7,3.1,.45,28),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0x0f172a}));base.position.y=.2;g.add(base);
    const ring=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.RingGeometry(2.6,2.9,40),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xf43f5e,transparent:true,opacity:.8,side:2,blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending,depthWrite:false}));ring.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;ring.position.y=.47;g.add(ring);
    g.position.set(x,0,z);S.scene.add(g);
    E.props.push({o:g,upd:dt=>{ring.material.opacity=.55+Math.sin(performance.now()/300)*.3;ring.scale.setScalar(1+Math.sin(performance.now()/500)*.03);if(Math.random()<dt*10)E.emit(x+rnd(-2.4,2.4),.5,z+rnd(-1.5,1.5),{n:1,c:[0xf43f5e,0xfda4af],spd:.4,up:2.4,g:-1.4,sz:.22,life:1.6})}});
    return g;
  }
  function doors(){
    const mk=side=>{const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.6,8,.6),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0x1f1a33}));m.position.set(side*3.8,4,-1.1);const e=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.12,8,.64),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xa78bfa}));e.position.x=-side*1.3;m.add(e);S.scene.add(m);E.props.push({o:m});return m};
    return [mk(-1),mk(1)];
  }
  function reality(x,z){
    const g=new THREE.Group(),core=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(.8,1,1,1),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0x000000,side:2})),glowM=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1.8,1,1,1),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xf43f5e,transparent:true,opacity:.6,side:2,blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending,depthWrite:false}));
    g.add(glowM);g.add(core);core.position.z=.02;g.position.set(x,5,z);g.scale.set(0.01,0.01,1);S.scene.add(g);E.props.push({o:g});return g;
  }

/* ===== Story 2 entry inside the Story UI, test-build unlock, story-save mirror ===== */
(()=>{
  const g=k=>{try{return localStorage.getItem(k)}catch(e){return null}},s=(k,v)=>{try{localStorage.setItem(k,v)}catch(e){}};
  const J=k=>{try{return JSON.parse(g(k)||'null')}catch(e){return null}};
  /* --- test build: Story 1 and Story 2 fully unlocked --- */
  if(window.__TESTBUILD){
    try{
      const n1=(window.STORY&&STORY.CH.length)||8,a=J('db_story')||{};
      let ch=false;
      if(!(a.u>=n1-1)){a.u=n1-1;ch=true}

    }catch(e){}
  }

  const mirror=()=>{
    const cur=g('db_story'),bk=g('db_story_bk');
    if(cur){if(cur!==bk){const o=J('db_story');if(o&&(o.u>0||o.pos||o.done))s('db_story_bk',cur)}}

    st.textContent='#stS2.new{animation:s2new 1.2s ease-in-out infinite}';document.head.appendChild(st);
  }

<script>
/* ===== STORY MODE: characters no longer walk through each other =====
   A soft personal-space push applied at render time only (script positions are untouched, so
   cutscenes still end exactly where they were written). Walkers slide around standing characters. */
(()=>{
  const E=window.S2E;if(!E||E.__sep)return;E.__sep=1;
  const oTick=E.tick;
  E.tick=function(dt,t){
    oTick.apply(this,arguments);
    try{
      if(!E.on||E.susp||E.fight||(window.G&&G.mode==='match'))return;
      const L=[];
      for(const id in E.actors){const a=E.actors[id];
        if(a.vis&&a.f&&a.f.g.visible&&a.al>.3&&!(a.ly>.3)&&!a.nocol)L.push(a)}
      const rd=Math.min(.1,E.rdt||dt||.016);
      for(const a of L){a.tsx=0;a.tsz=0}
      for(let i=0;i<L.length;i++)for(let j=i+1;j<L.length;j++){
        const a=L[i],b=L[j];
        if(Math.abs((a.y+a.fly)-(b.y+b.fly))>1.4)continue;
        const ra=.34*(a.big||1)*(a.sc||1),rb=.34*(b.big||1)*(b.sc||1),dm=(ra+rb)*((a.walking||a.goal||b.walking||b.goal)?1.3:1);
        const dx=b.x-a.x,dz=b.z-a.z,d=Math.hypot(dx,dz);
        if(d>=dm)continue;
        let ux,uz,push;
        const wk=a.walking?a:(b.walking?b:null);
        if(wk&&d>.01){
          /* a walker slides around sideways instead of being pushed back along its path */
          const o=wk===a?b:a,vx=Math.sin(wk.face),vz=Math.cos(wk.face),nx=-vz,nz=vx;
          const ox=o.x-wk.x,oz=o.z-wk.z,lat=ox*nx+oz*nz,sg=lat>=0?1:-1;
          const s2=(wk===a?1:-1)*sg;ux=nx*s2;uz=nz*s2;push=Math.max(.05,dm-Math.abs(lat));
        }else if(d<.01){ux=0;uz=(i%2)?-1:1;push=dm}else{ux=dx/d;uz=dz/d;push=dm-d}
        const wa=(a.walking||a.goal)?.15:1,wb=(b.walking||b.goal)?.15:1,sa=wa/(wa+wb),sb=wb/(wa+wb);
        a.tsx-=ux*push*sa;a.tsz-=uz*push*sa;b.tsx+=ux*push*sb;b.tsz+=uz*push*sb;
      }
      const k=Math.min(1,14*rd);
      for(const id in E.actors){const a=E.actors[id];
        const tx=Math.max(-1.3,Math.min(1.3,a.tsx||0)),tz=Math.max(-1.3,Math.min(1.3,a.tsz||0));
        a.sx=(a.sx||0)+(tx-(a.sx||0))*k;a.sz=(a.sz||0)+(tz-(a.sz||0))*k;
        if(a.vis&&a.f&&(Math.abs(a.sx)>.002||Math.abs(a.sz)>.002)){a.f.g.position.x+=a.sx;a.f.g.position.z+=a.sz}
        a.tsx=a.tsz=0;
      }
    }catch(e){}
  };
})();

</script>
