  rr.onclick=()=>{const p=a=>a[Math.floor(Math.random()*a.length)];
    const pickRow=(k)=>{const bs=[...cust.querySelectorAll('.opt[data-k="'+k+'"]')].filter(b=>open(k,b.dataset.v));if(bs.length)G.pc[k]=p(bs).dataset.v};
    ['hair','ears','tail','nose','brow','cheek','mouth','aura','trail','orb','mus','mf','gs','ss'].forEach(k=>{if(Math.random()<.55)pickRow(k);else G.pc[k]=''});
    const pc=(k,pal)=>{const o=pal.filter(c=>open(k,c));return o.length?p(o):''};
    G.pc.hairc=pc('hairc',PAL);G.pc.auc=Math.random()<.5?pc('auc',APAL):'';G.pc.wb=Math.random()<.5?pc('wb',BPAL):'';G.pc.ak=Math.random()<.4?pc('ak',BPAL):'';
    G.pc.aw=+(.75+Math.random()*.8).toFixed(2);G.pc.lw=+(.75+Math.random()*.8).toFixed(2);G.pc.al=+(.85+Math.random()*.45).toFixed(2);G.pc.ll=+(.85+Math.random()*.45).toFixed(2);
    save();build('p');sync()};ss.appendChild(rr);
  const rc=document.createElement('button');rc.className='btn c2b';rc.textContent='↺ Clear all extras';
  rc.onclick=()=>{SK.forEach(k=>{G.pc[k]=k==='orbn'?3:''});save();build('p');sync()};ss.appendChild(rc);

  /* tabs */
  const mine=[];
  function addTab(id,label,sec){
    const t=document.createElement('button');t.className='ctab';t.dataset.t=id;t.textContent=label;
    const size=[...bar.children].find(b=>b.dataset.t==='size');bar.insertBefore(t,size||null);
    t.onclick=()=>{bar.querySelectorAll('.ctab').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('on',b===t));body.querySelectorAll('.tabsec').forEach(s=>s.classList.toggle('on',s===sec));body.scrollTop=0};
    mine.push([t,sec])}
  addTab('c2body','💪 Body Parts',sb);addTab('c2style','✨ Style Extras',ss);
  bar.addEventListener('click',e=>{const b=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('.ctab');if(!b)return;if(mine.some(m=>m[0]===b))return;mine.forEach(([t,s])=>{t.classList.remove('on');s.classList.remove('on')})},true);

  /* keep inputs in step with G.pc */
  const oSync=window.sync;
  window.sync=function(){const r=oSync.apply(this,arguments);
    try{const c=G.pc;sliders.forEach(([k,i])=>{const v=c[k]==null?DEFN[k]:c[k];if(document.activeElement!==i)i.value=v;const l=$$('c2v_'+k);if(l)l.textContent=k==='orbn'?String(v|0):Math.round(+v*100)+'%'});
      cust.querySelectorAll('.c2r').forEach(d=>{if(d._pick){const k=d.querySelector('[data-k]').dataset.k;if(c[k]&&HEX.test(c[k]))d._pick.value=c[k]}})}catch(e){}
    return r};

  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------- 3D : decorating fighters */
  const reg=[];                       // fighters with decorations
  const cache={};
  function col(c){return new THREE.Color(c)}
  function makeMat(c,o){return new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial(Object.assign({color:c,roughness:.85,metalness:0},o||{}))}
  function basic(c,o){return new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial(Object.assign({color:c},o||{}))}

  /* little vector sprites (so they look the same on every device) */
  function tex(kind,c){
    const key=kind+c;if(cache[key])return cache[key];
    const cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=cv.height=64;const x=cv.getContext('2d');x.translate(32,32);
    const st=(r,n,inn)=>{x.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<n*2;i++){const a=i*PI/n-PI/2,rr=i%2?inn:r;x.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*rr,Math.sin(a)*rr)}x.closePath()};
    x.fillStyle=c;x.strokeStyle='#000';x.lineWidth=3;x.lineJoin='round';
    switch(kind){
      case'star':st(26,5,11);x.fill();x.stroke();break;
      case'spark':st(28,4,5);x.fill();break;
      case'heart':x.beginPath();x.moveTo(0,22);x.bezierCurveTo(-34,-2,-18,-30,0,-10);x.bezierCurveTo(18,-30,34,-2,0,22);x.fill();x.stroke();break;
      case'orb':{const g=x.createRadialGradient(-6,-6,2,0,0,28);g.addColorStop(0,'#fff');g.addColorStop(.35,c);g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(0,0,0,.35)');x.fillStyle=g;x.beginPath();x.arc(0,0,26,0,7);x.fill();x.stroke();break}
      case'glow':{const g=x.createRadialGradient(0,0,0,0,0,30);g.addColorStop(0,c);g.addColorStop(.45,c+'aa');g.addColorStop(1,c+'00');x.fillStyle=g;x.beginPath();x.arc(0,0,30,0,7);x.fill();break}
      case'ring':x.lineWidth=7;x.strokeStyle=c;x.beginPath();x.arc(0,0,24,0,7);x.stroke();break;
      case'bolt':x.beginPath();x.moveTo(6,-28);x.lineTo(-14,4);x.lineTo(0,4);x.lineTo(-8,28);x.lineTo(16,-6);x.lineTo(2,-6);x.closePath();x.fill();x.stroke();break;
      case'flake':x.strokeStyle=c;x.lineWidth=5;x.lineCap='round';for(let i=0;i<3;i++){x.rotate(PI/3);x.beginPath();x.moveTo(0,-26);x.lineTo(0,26);x.stroke()}break;
      case'note':x.beginPath();x.ellipse(-8,16,10,8,-.4,0,7);x.fill();x.stroke();x.lineWidth=5;x.beginPath();x.moveTo(1,14);x.lineTo(1,-24);x.lineTo(16,-14);x.stroke();break;
      case'skull':x.fillStyle='#f8fafc';x.beginPath();x.arc(0,-4,22,0,7);x.fill();x.stroke();x.fillRect(-12,12,24,14);x.strokeRect(-12,12,24,14);x.fillStyle='#000';x.beginPath();x.arc(-9,-4,6,0,7);x.arc(9,-4,6,0,7);x.fill();break;
      case'bubble':x.fillStyle=c+'55';x.strokeStyle=c;x.lineWidth=3;x.beginPath();x.arc(0,0,24,0,7);x.fill();x.stroke();x.fillStyle='#ffffffcc';x.beginPath();x.arc(-9,-9,6,0,7);x.fill();break;
      case'puff':{const g=x.createRadialGradient(0,0,2,0,0,28);g.addColorStop(0,c+'cc');g.addColorStop(1,c+'00');x.fillStyle=g;x.beginPath();x.arc(0,0,28,0,7);x.fill();break}
      case'petal':x.beginPath();x.ellipse(0,0,10,22,.5,0,7);x.fill();x.stroke();break;
      case'planet':x.fillStyle=c;x.beginPath();x.arc(0,0,17,0,7);x.fill();x.stroke();x.strokeStyle='#fde68a';x.lineWidth=5;x.beginPath();x.ellipse(0,0,29,8,-.5,0,7);x.stroke();break;
      case'drop':x.beginPath();x.moveTo(0,-26);x.quadraticCurveTo(24,6,0,24);x.quadraticCurveTo(-24,6,0,-26);x.fill();break;
      default:x.beginPath();x.arc(0,0,22,0,7);x.fill();
    }
    const t=new THREE.CanvasTexture(cv);cache[key]=t;return t}
  function sprite(kind,c,size){
    const m=new THREE.SpriteMaterial({map:tex(kind,c),transparent:true,depthWrite:false,fog:false});
    const s=new THREE.Sprite(m);s.scale.setScalar(size);s.renderOrder=40;return s}

  /* ---- particle pool (auras + trails) ---- */
  const parts=[];let scn=null;
  function spawn(kind,c,p,v,life,s0,s1,o){
    if(!scn)return;o=o||{};
    if(parts.length>260)return;
    const sp=sprite(kind,c,s0);sp.position.copy(p);scn.add(sp);
    parts.push({sp,v:v.clone(),t:0,life,s0,s1,g:o.g||0,spin:o.spin||0,add:o.add});
    if(o.add)sp.material.blending=THREE.AdditiveBlending}
  function stepParts(dt){
    for(let i=parts.length-1;i>=0;i--){const q=parts[i];q.t+=dt;const u=q.t/q.life;
      if(u>=1||!q.sp.parent){scn.remove(q.sp);q.sp.material.dispose();parts.splice(i,1);continue}
      q.v.y+=q.g*dt;q.sp.position.addScaledVector(q.v,dt);q.sp.scale.setScalar(q.s0+(q.s1-q.s0)*u);q.sp.material.opacity=Math.min(1,(1-u)*1.6);q.sp.material.rotation+=q.spin*dt}}

  /* ---- geometry helpers ---- */
  function addM(par,geo,mat,x,y,z,o){
    const m=new THREE.Mesh(geo,mat);m.position.set(x||0,y||0,z||0);o=o||{};
    if(o.s)m.scale.set(o.s[0],o.s[1],o.s[2]);if(o.r)m.rotation.set(o.r[0],o.r[1],o.r[2]);
    m.castShadow=!o.ns;
    if(o.ol&&!o.nol){const l=new THREE.Mesh(geo,o.ol);l.scale.setScalar(1.09);m.add(l)}
    par.add(m);return m}
  const sphere=(r,w)=>new THREE.SphereGeometry(r,w||14,w||14);
  const cone=(r,h,s)=>new THREE.ConeGeometry(r,h,s||10);
  const cyl=(r,h,s,r2)=>new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r2==null?r:r2,r,h,s||12);
  const AY=new V3(0,1,0);
  function spikeOn(par,mat,ol,dir,len,r,off){
    dir=dir.clone().normalize();const m=addM(par,cone(r,len,9),mat,0,0,0,{ol});
    m.position.copy(dir).multiplyScalar(.68-(off||.04)+len/2);m.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(AY,dir);return m}
  const dirAL=(az,lat)=>{az*=PI/180;lat*=PI/180;return new V3(Math.cos(lat)*Math.cos(az),Math.sin(lat),Math.cos(lat)*Math.sin(az))};
  function onHead(par,mesh,az,lat,off){
    const n=dirAL(az,lat);mesh.position.copy(n).multiplyScalar(.68+(off||.01));
    const m=new THREE.Matrix4();m.lookAt(n,new V3(0,0,0),new V3(0,1,0));mesh.quaternion.setFromRotationMatrix(m);par.add(mesh);return mesh}

  /* ---- hair ---- */
  function hairOf(kind,head,c,ol){
    const g=new THREE.Group(),mat=makeMat(c,{roughness:.9});
    const A=(geo,x,y,z,o)=>addM(g,geo,mat,x,y,z,Object.assign({ol},o));
    const cap=(rr,tilt)=>{const m=addM(g,new THREE.SphereGeometry(rr,22,14,0,PI*2,0,1.05),mat,0,0,0,{ol,nol:true});m.rotation.z=(tilt||0)+.3;
      addM(g,sphere(.6,16),mat,-.44,.08,0,{ol,nol:true,s:[.62,1,1.12]});return m};
    switch(kind){
      case'tuft':{const a=A(cone(.13,.42,9),-.08,.72,0);a.rotation.z=.25;const b=A(cone(.1,.32,9),.1,.7,.1);b.rotation.z=-.25;b.rotation.x=.3;break}
      case'spiky':{for(let i=0;i<9;i++){const a=i/9*PI*2,pol=i?42:0;spikeOn(g,mat,ol,new V3(Math.sin(pol*PI/180)*Math.cos(a)-.12*(i?1:0),Math.cos(pol*PI/180),Math.sin(pol*PI/180)*Math.sin(a)),.5+(i%3)*.08,.14)}break}
      case'mohawk':{for(let i=0;i<7;i++){const a=(-62+i*20.6)*PI/180;spikeOn(g,mat,ol,new V3(Math.sin(a),Math.cos(a),0),.34+Math.sin(i/6*PI)*.4,.1+(i===3?.03:0))}break}
      case'afro':{A(sphere(.82,20),-.3,.34,0);break}
      case'bun':{cap(.71,.25);A(sphere(.3,16),-.14,.9,0);A(new THREE.TorusGeometry(.22,.05,8,14),-.14,.74,0,{r:[PI/2,0,0],nol:true});break}
      case'pigtails':{cap(.71,.12);for(const s of[1,-1]){A(sphere(.18,12),-.1,.38,s*.74);const t=A(cone(.2,.8,10),-.1,-.1,s*.84);t.rotation.set(PI,0,s*.12)}break}
      case'long':{cap(.72,.3);A(sphere(.5,16),-.52,-.42,0,{s:[.55,1.5,1.25]});for(const z of[1,-1])A(sphere(.3,12),-.2,-.12,z*.6,{s:[.55,1.7,.5]});break}
      case'bob':{cap(.74,.22);for(const z of[1,-1])A(sphere(.34,14),-.15,-.1,z*.6,{s:[.8,1.2,.55]});A(sphere(.5,16),-.5,-.12,0,{s:[.6,1.1,1.15]});break}
      case'flame':{const fm=basic(c);for(let i=0;i<7;i++){const a=(i/7)*PI*2,m=addM(g,cone(.17,.62+(i%2)*.3,8),fm,Math.cos(a)*.3-.1,.78,Math.sin(a)*.3,{});m.userData.fl=i}g.userData.flame=1;break}
      case'curly':{for(let i=0;i<22;i++){const t=(i+.5)/22,pol=Math.acos(1-t*.9),az=i*2.399963;const n=new V3(Math.sin(pol)*Math.cos(az),Math.cos(pol),Math.sin(pol)*Math.sin(az));if(n.x>.6)continue;A(sphere(.17,10),n.x*.74,n.y*.74,n.z*.74)}break}
      case'ponytail':{cap(.71,.18);A(sphere(.14,10),-.62,.4,0);const p=new THREE.Group();p.position.set(-.64,.38,0);g.add(p);const a=addM(p,cone(.2,1,10),mat,0,-.5,0,{ol});a.rotation.z=PI;g.userData.pony=p;break}
      case'wild':{cap(.72,.1);for(let i=0;i<16;i++){const a=i/16*PI*2,pol=60+((i*37)%30);const d=new V3(Math.sin(pol*PI/180)*Math.cos(a)-.15,Math.cos(pol*PI/180),Math.sin(pol*PI/180)*Math.sin(a));if(d.x>.35)continue;spikeOn(g,mat,ol,d,.5,.17)}break}
    }
    return g}

  /* ---- ears ---- */
  function earsOf(kind,head,cfg,ol){
    const g=new THREE.Group(),bc=hexC(cfg.color,'#ffffff'),mat=makeMat(bc),pk=basic('#f9a8d4');
    const A=(geo,x,y,z,o)=>addM(g,geo,mat,x,y,z,Object.assign({ol},o));
    for(const s of[1,-1]){
      switch(kind){
        case'cat':{const e=A(cone(.24,.5,3),-.04,.66,s*.4,{r:[s*.3,0,0]});const i=addM(g,cone(.14,.34,3),pk,.07,.64,s*.4,{r:[s*.3,0,0],ns:true});break}
        case'bunny':{const e=A(sphere(.16,12),-.06,1.0,s*.28,{s:[.7,3.4,.55],r:[s*.18,0,0]});addM(g,sphere(.1,10),pk,.0,1.0,s*.27,{s:[.5,3,.4],r:[s*.18,0,0],ns:true});break}
        case'bear':{A(sphere(.22,14),-.06,.58,s*.52);addM(g,sphere(.13,12),pk,.08,.58,s*.52,{ns:true});break}
        case'elf':{const e=A(cone(.15,.62,8),-.05,.12,s*.78,{r:[s*PI/2,0,0]});e.rotation.set(s*(PI/2+.2),0,0);break}
        case'dog':{A(sphere(.2,12),-.06,.3,s*.7,{s:[.7,2,1]});break}
        case'mouse':{A(sphere(.34,14),-.14,.56,s*.52,{s:[.3,1,1]});addM(g,sphere(.24,12),pk,-.06,.56,s*.52,{s:[.2,1,1],ns:true});break}
        case'bolts':{A(cyl(.09,.3,8),0,-.1,s*.7,{r:[PI/2,0,0]});A(cyl(.16,.07,10),0,-.1,s*.86,{r:[PI/2,0,0]});break}
        case'fin':{const e=A(cone(.3,.9,3),-.08,.2,s*.72,{s:[1,1,.2]});e.rotation.set(s*.2,0,-.2);break}
      }}
    return g}

  /* ---- noses (head local, face toward +x) ---- */
  function noseOf(kind,cfg,ol){
    const g=new THREE.Group(),A=(geo,m,x,y,z,o)=>addM(g,geo,m,x,y,z,Object.assign({ol},o));
    const bc=hexC(cfg.color,'#ffffff');
    switch(kind){
      case'round':A(sphere(.14,14),makeMat('#ef4444',{roughness:.4}),.68,-.03,0);break;
      case'pointy':A(cone(.1,.38,10),makeMat(bc),.8,-.04,0,{r:[0,0,-PI/2]});break;
      case'long':A(cone(.12,.8,10),makeMat(bc),1.05,-.04,0,{r:[0,0,-PI/2]});break;
      case'pig':{A(cyl(.19,.16,14),makeMat('#f9a8d4'),.72,-.06,0,{r:[0,0,PI/2]});A(sphere(.035,6),basic('#7f1d1d'),.81,-.06,.07,{ns:true,nol:true});A(sphere(.035,6),basic('#7f1d1d'),.81,-.06,-.07,{ns:true,nol:true});break}
      case'beak':{const b=A(cone(.2,.44,4),makeMat('#fbbf24',{roughness:.5}),.88,-.12,0,{r:[0,PI/4,-PI/2]});b.scale.set(1,1,.6);break}
      case'dog':A(sphere(.15,12),makeMat('#0f172a',{roughness:.4}),.7,-.04,0,{s:[.9,.8,1.2]});break;
      case'button':A(sphere(.07,10),makeMat(bc),.68,-.04,0);break;
      case'tri':A(cone(.13,.14,3),makeMat('#f97316'),.7,-.04,0,{r:[0,0,-PI/2]});break;
    }
    return g}

  /* ---- brows ---- */
  function browsOf(kind,cfg,ol){
    const g=new THREE.Group(),mat=basic(hexC(cfg.fc,'#111111'));
    const B=(az,lat,w,h,rot,s)=>{const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(w,h,.045),mat);onHead(g,m,az,lat,.012);m.rotateZ(rot*s);return m};
    switch(kind){
      case'thick':for(const s of[1,-1])B(s*46,31,.32,.1,0,s);break;
      case'angry':for(const s of[1,-1])B(s*44,31,.34,.085,-.5,s);break;
      case'sad':for(const s of[1,-1])B(s*44,31,.32,.07,.4,s);break;
      case'raised':for(const s of[1,-1])B(s*46,38,.3,.07,s===1?.1:-.1,s);break;
      case'uni':{const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.82,.08,.045),mat);onHead(g,m,0,32,.012);m.scale.set(1,1,1);break}
      case'bushy':for(const s of[1,-1]){const m=B(s*46,31,.36,.15,0,s);m.geometry=new THREE.SphereGeometry(.13,10,8);m.scale.set(1.3,.55,.5)}break;
      case'flame':for(const s of[1,-1])for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const m=new THREE.Mesh(cone(.045,.2+i*.05,6),basic('#f97316'));onHead(g,m,s*(38+i*8),32+(i===1?2:0),.01);m.rotateX(PI/2);m.rotateZ(-s*.5)}break;
    }
    return g}

  /* ---- cheek marks / mouth extras ---- */
  function starShape(r,ri,n){const s=new THREE.Shape();for(let i=0;i<n*2;i++){const a=i*PI/n-PI/2,rr=i%2?ri:r;i?s.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*rr,Math.sin(a)*rr):s.moveTo(Math.cos(a)*rr,Math.sin(a)*rr)}return new THREE.ShapeGeometry(s)}
  function heartShape(sc){const s=new THREE.Shape();s.moveTo(0,-.5*sc);s.bezierCurveTo(-1*sc,.1*sc,-.5*sc,.8*sc,0,.35*sc);s.bezierCurveTo(.5*sc,.8*sc,1*sc,.1*sc,0,-.5*sc);return new THREE.ShapeGeometry(s)}
  function cheeksOf(kind,cfg){
    const g=new THREE.Group();
    const D=(geo,c,az,lat,o)=>{const m=new THREE.Mesh(geo,basic(c,Object.assign({polygonOffset:true,polygonOffsetFactor:-2,polygonOffsetUnits:-2,side:2},o||{})));onHead(g,m,az,lat,.006);return m};
    for(const s of[1,-1]){
      switch(kind){
        case'blush':{const m=D(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.16,16),'#fb7185',s*50,-14,{transparent:true,opacity:.7});m.scale.set(1.4,.8,1);break}
        case'freckles':for(let i=0;i<5;i++){D(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.035,8),'#7c2d12',s*(44+(i%3)*6),-6-Math.floor(i/3)*7+((i*5)%3))}break;
        case'stars':D(starShape(.15,.065,5),'#fde047',s*52,-12);break;
        case'hearts':D(heartShape(.3),'#f43f5e',s*52,-12);break;
        case'scar':if(s===1){const m=D(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(.04,.34),'#b91c1c',40,6);m.rotateZ(.5);const k=D(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(.14,.03),'#b91c1c',40,6);k.rotateZ(.5);k.rotateZ(PI/2)}break;
        case'tear':if(s===1){const m=D(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.07,10),'#38bdf8',50,-8);m.scale.set(.8,1.5,1)}break;
        case'paint':for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const m=D(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(.3,.06),'#ef4444',s*(50+i*2),-6-i*6);m.rotateZ(s*.15)}break;
        case'whisk':for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const m=D(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(.32,.012),'#111827',s*(56),-14+i*5);m.rotateZ((i-1)*.35*s)}break;
      }}
    return g}
  function mouthOf(kind,cfg,ol){
    const g=new THREE.Group(),A=(geo,m,az,lat,rot,o)=>{const x=addM(g,geo,m,0,0,0,Object.assign({ol,nol:true},o));const n=dirAL(az,lat);x.position.copy(n).multiplyScalar(.69);const mm=new THREE.Matrix4();mm.lookAt(n,new V3(0,0,0),AY);x.quaternion.setFromRotationMatrix(mm);x.rotateX(rot||0);return x};
    const wh=makeMat('#ffffff',{roughness:.4});
    switch(kind){
      case'fangs':for(const s of[1,-1])A(cone(.06,.22,8),wh,s*14,-36,PI,{});break;
      case'buck':A(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.2,.06,.24),wh,0,-40,0);break;
      case'tongue':{const t=A(sphere(.14,12),makeMat('#fb7185',{roughness:.5}),0,-47,0);t.scale.set(1.1,1.5,.5);break}
      case'gold':A(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.13,.08,.16),basic('#fde047'),14,-39,0);break;
      case'tusks':for(const s of[1,-1])A(cone(.06,.32,8),wh,s*24,-36,0,{});break;
      case'pacifier':{A(new THREE.TorusGeometry(.14,.035,8,14),makeMat('#38bdf8'),0,-43,0);A(sphere(.1,10),makeMat('#f472b6'),0,-43,0);break}
      case'cig':{const m=A(cyl(.025,.5,6),wh,22,-38,PI/2);m.translateY(.25);const t=A(sphere(.055,8),makeMat('#ef4444'),22,-38,0);t.translateZ(.5);break}
    }
    return g}

  /* ---- tails (nested groups so each segment can wave) ---- */
  function tailOf(kind,cfg,ol){
    const g=new THREE.Group(),bc=hexC(cfg.tailc,null),body=hexC(cfg.color,'#ffffff');
    const segs=[];
    const base=new THREE.Group();base.position.set(-.45,.8,0);g.add(base);g.scale.setScalar(1.35);
    let par=base;
    const chain=(n,len,rad,colf,curl,start)=>{
      par.rotation.z=start||0;
      for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
        const r=typeof rad==='function'?rad(i):rad,m=makeMat(colf(i));
        const seg=new THREE.Group();par.add(seg);segs.push(seg);
        addM(seg,sphere(r,12),m,0,len/2,0,{ol,s:[1,Math.max(1,len*.7/r),1]});
        const nx=new THREE.Group();nx.position.y=len;seg.add(nx);nx.userData.curl=curl;par=nx;
        seg.userData.i=i;
        nx.rotation.z=curl}
      return par};
    switch(kind){
      case'cat':chain(8,.2,i=>.1-i*.003,()=>bc||body,-.2,PI/2.6);break;
      case'fox':{const o=bc||'#f97316';chain(5,.24,i=>[.1,.17,.22,.19,.11][i],i=>i===4?'#ffffff':o,-.2,PI/2.6);break}
      case'bunny':{base.rotation.z=PI/2.4;addM(base,sphere(.26,14),makeMat(bc||'#ffffff'),0,.18,0,{ol});break}
      case'dino':{const e=chain(7,.22,i=>.2-i*.024,()=>bc||body,-.12,PI/2.5);let p=base;segs.forEach((s,i)=>{if(i<6)addM(s,cone(.06,.2,6),makeMat('#f97316'),0,.12,i%2?.1:-.1,{ol,r:[i%2?-1:1,0,0]})});break}
      case'devil':{chain(7,.22,()=>.05,()=>bc||'#ef4444',-.22,PI/2.4);const t=addM(par,cone(.14,.28,3),makeMat(bc||'#ef4444'),0,.14,0,{ol,s:[1,1,.3]});break}
      case'lizard':chain(10,.17,i=>.15-i*.012,i=>bc||'#4ade80',-.05,PI/2.6);break;
      case'scorp':{chain(8,.2,()=>.09,()=>bc||'#7c3aed',-.4,PI/2.1);addM(par,cone(.1,.3,8),makeMat('#fde047'),0,.16,0,{ol});break}
      case'lightning':{chain(5,.3,()=>.08,i=>bc||'#fde047',0,PI/2.5);segs.forEach((s,i)=>{s.userData.zig=i%2?1:-1});break}
    }
    g.userData.segs=segs;g.userData.kind=kind;return g}

  /* ---- gloves / shoes ---- */
  function gloveStyle(kind,f,ol){
    for(const h0 of[f.hl,f.hr]){const h=new THREE.Group();h.rotation.y=-PI/2;h0.add(h);
      switch(kind){
        case'cuff':addM(h,new THREE.TorusGeometry(.215,.06,8,18),makeMat('#ef4444'),0,0,0,{r:[0,PI/2,0],nol:true});break;
        case'spiked':for(let i=0;i<7;i++){const a=i/7*PI*2;const m=addM(h,cone(.05,.16,6),makeMat('#cbd5e1',{metalness:.7,roughness:.3}),0,0,0,{ol,nol:true});const d=new V3(Math.cos(a)*.5+.5,Math.sin(a)*.8,Math.cos(a+1)*.8).normalize();m.position.copy(d).multiplyScalar(.26);m.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(AY,d)}break;
        case'claws':for(const z of[-.1,0,.1]){const m=addM(h,cone(.035,.3,6),makeMat('#f1f5f9',{roughness:.3}),.3,.0,z,{ol,nol:true,r:[0,0,-PI/2]});m.rotation.y=z*2}break;
        case'paw':for(const z of[-.12,0,.12]){addM(h,sphere(.06,8),makeMat('#f9a8d4'),.2,z===0?.1:.04,z,{ns:true})}addM(h,sphere(.1,8),makeMat('#f9a8d4'),.17,-.06,0,{ns:true,s:[.5,.8,1.1]});break;
        case'bell':addM(h,sphere(.1,12),makeMat('#fbbf24',{metalness:.8,roughness:.3}),0,.26,0,{ol});break;
        case'ring':addM(h,new THREE.TorusGeometry(.25,.045,8,18),makeMat('#fbbf24',{metalness:.8,roughness:.3}),0,0,0,{ol,nol:true,r:[0,PI/2,0]});break;
      }}}
  function shoeStyle(kind,f,cfg,ol){
    const bc=hexC(cfg.shoe,hexC(cfg.color,'#ffffff'));
    for(const h0 of[f.fl,f.fr]){const h=new THREE.Group();h.rotation.y=-PI/2;h0.add(h);
      switch(kind){
        case'boots':addM(h,cyl(.17,.34,12),makeMat(bc),-.04,.22,0,{ol});break;
        case'spiked':for(let i=0;i<4;i++)addM(h,cone(.04,.16,6),makeMat('#cbd5e1',{metalness:.7}),-.1+i*.1,-.2,0,{ol:null,r:[PI,0,0]});break;
        case'rocket':{const m=addM(h,cone(.12,.5,10),basic('#fb923c'),-.05,-.38,0,{ns:true,r:[PI,0,0]});m.userData.fl=1;const m2=addM(h,cone(.07,.3,8),basic('#fde047'),-.05,-.3,0,{ns:true,r:[PI,0,0]});m2.userData.fl=2;break}
        case'wheels':for(const x of[-.12,.12])addM(h,cyl(.1,.07,14),makeMat('#334155'),x,-.14,0,{r:[PI/2,0,0],ol,nol:false});break;
        case'springs':addM(h,new THREE.TorusGeometry(.1,.025,6,12),makeMat('#94a3b8',{metalness:.8}),0,-.18,0,{r:[PI/2,0,0],ns:true});addM(h,new THREE.TorusGeometry(.1,.025,6,12),makeMat('#94a3b8',{metalness:.8}),0,-.26,0,{r:[PI/2,0,0],ns:true});break;
        case'fins':{const m=addM(h,sphere(.25,14),makeMat('#4ade80'),.25,-.05,0,{ol,s:[1.3,.18,1.2]});break}
      }}}

  /* ---- decorate a fighter ---- */
  function decorate(f,cfg){
    if(!f||!cfg||f.__c2)return f;f.__c2=1;
    const need=cfg===G.pc||cfg===G.oc||SK.some(k=>k!=='orbn'&&k!=='tailc'&&k!=='hairc'&&k!=='auc'&&cfg[k])||NK.some(k=>cfg[k]!=null&&+cfg[k]!==1);
    if(!need)return f;
    const ol=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:hexC(cfg.ol,'#000000'),side:THREE.BackSide});
    const d={cfg,tail:null,mus:[],bel:null,bands:[],ak:[],orb:null,flames:[],pony:null,last:null,vel:0,tt:Math.random()*9};
    f.dc=d;
    const head=f.head,root=f.root;
    const HF=new THREE.Group();HF.rotation.y=-PI/2;head.add(HF);const RF=new THREE.Group();RF.rotation.y=-PI/2;root.add(RF);d.RF=RF;
    try{
      /* finish */
      if(cfg.mf){let hideOl=0;const ms=[f.mat];const hm=head.children.find(c=>c.material&&c.material.map);if(hm)ms.push(hm.material);
        ms.forEach(m=>{switch(cfg.mf){
          case'glossy':m.roughness=.18;m.metalness=.15;break;
          case'matte':m.roughness=1;break;
          case'metal':m.roughness=.4;m.metalness=.5;m.emissive=m.color.clone().multiplyScalar(.3);if(m.map)m.emissiveMap=m.map;break;
          case'chrome':m.roughness=.1;m.metalness=.7;m.emissive=m.color.clone().multiplyScalar(.55);if(m.map)m.emissiveMap=m.map;break;
          case'neon':m.emissive=m.color.clone();m.emissiveIntensity=.65;if(m.map)m.emissiveMap=m.map;break;
          case'glass':m.transparent=true;m.opacity=.55;m.roughness=.05;m.depthWrite=false;hideOl=1;break;
          case'ghost':m.transparent=true;m.opacity=.4;m.emissive=m.color.clone();m.emissiveIntensity=.35;m.depthWrite=false;hideOl=1;break;
          }
          m.needsUpdate=true});
        d.hideOl=hideOl;
        if(cfg.mf==='rainbow'||cfg.mf==='lava')d.fin=ms}
      if(cfg.hair){const hc=hexC(cfg.hairc,{flame:'#f97316',afro:'#3b2a1a'}[cfg.hair]||'#3b2a1a');const h=hairOf(cfg.hair,head,hc,ol);HF.add(h);d.hair=h;
        h.traverse(o=>{if(o.userData&&o.userData.fl!=null)d.flames.push(o)});if(h.userData.pony)d.pony=h.userData.pony}
      if(cfg.ears){const e=earsOf(cfg.ears,head,cfg,ol);HF.add(e)}
      if(cfg.nose)HF.add(noseOf(cfg.nose,cfg,ol));
      if(cfg.brow)HF.add(browsOf(cfg.brow,cfg,ol));
      if(cfg.cheek)HF.add(cheeksOf(cfg.cheek,cfg));
      if(cfg.mouth)HF.add(mouthOf(cfg.mouth,cfg,ol));
      if(cfg.tail){d.tail=tailOf(cfg.tail,cfg,ol);RF.add(d.tail)}
      if(cfg.gs)gloveStyle(cfg.gs,f,ol);
      if(cfg.ss){shoeStyle(cfg.ss,f,cfg,ol);f.fl.traverse(o=>{if(o.userData&&o.userData.fl)d.flames.push(o)});f.fr.traverse(o=>{if(o.userData&&o.userData.fl)d.flames.push(o)})}
      const bm=hexC(cfg.color,'#ffffff'),mm=makeMat(bm);
      /* muscles + belly (always built, sized live) */
      for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const m=addM(root,sphere(.2,12),mm,0,0,0,{ol});m.visible=false;d.mus.push(m)}
      d.bel=addM(RF,sphere(.4,16),mm,0,.85,0,{ol,nol:true});d.bel.visible=false;
      for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const t=addM(root,new THREE.TorusGeometry(.12,.045,8,14),makeMat(hexC(cfg.wb,'#ef4444'),{roughness:.7}),0,0,0,{ol:null});t.visible=false;d.bands.push(t)}
      for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const t=addM(root,new THREE.TorusGeometry(.14,.05,8,14),makeMat(hexC(cfg.ak,'#ef4444'),{roughness:.7}),0,0,0,{ol:null});t.visible=false;d.ak.push(t)}
      if(cfg.orb){const og=new THREE.Group();og.position.y=1.9;root.add(og);d.orb=og;d.orbS=[];
        const kinds={stars:['star','#fde047'],orbs:['orb','#38bdf8'],hearts:['heart','#f43f5e'],skulls:['skull','#f8fafc'],bolts:['bolt','#fde047'],snow:['flake','#bae6fd'],notes:['note','#a78bfa'],planets:['planet','#fb923c']}[cfg.orb];
        if(kinds){const n=Math.round(num(cfg.orbn,3,1,6));for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const sp=sprite(kinds[0],kinds[1],.55);og.add(sp);d.orbS.push(sp)}}}
    }catch(e){console.warn('cust2',e&&e.message)}
    reg.push(f);return f}

  /* wrap fighter() so every fighter (player, opponent, online) is decorated */
  const oF=window.fighter;
  window.fighter=function(cfg){const f=oF.apply(this,arguments);try{decorate(f,cfg||{})}catch(e){console.warn('cust2',e&&e.message)}return f};

  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------- per-frame : sizes (transient) + animation */
  let saved=[],lastT=performance.now();
  const _a=new V3(),_b=new V3(),_q=new Q(),_q2=new Q().setFromAxisAngle(new V3(1,0,0),PI/2);
  const Y=new V3(0,1,0);
  function lim(m,a,b,th){_a.subVectors(b,a);const L=_a.length()||.01;m.position.addVectors(a,b).multiplyScalar(.5);m.scale.set(th,L,th);m.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(Y,_a.divideScalar(L))}
  function pre(){
    const now=performance.now();let dt=Math.min(.1,(now-lastT)/1000);lastT=now;
    try{scn=scene}catch(e){}
    saved=[];
    for(let i=reg.length-1;i>=0;i--){
      const f=reg[i];if(!f.g||!f.g.parent){reg.splice(i,1);continue}
      if(!f.g.visible)continue;
      const d=f.dc,c=d.cfg,t=now/1000+d.tt;
      const aw=num(c.aw,1,.6,1.8),al=num(c.al,1,.75,1.4),lw=num(c.lw,1,.6,1.8),ll=num(c.ll,1,.75,1.4),gz=num(c.gz,1,.7,1.6),fz=num(c.fz,1,.7,1.6),ht=num(c.ht,1,.85,1.2);
      const sv={f,hl:f.hl.position.clone(),hr:f.hr.position.clone(),fl:f.fl.position.clone(),fr:f.fr.position.clone(),ry:f.root.position.y,rs:f.root.scale.clone(),
        gl:f.hl.scale.clone(),gr:f.hr.scale.clone(),fls:f.fl.scale.clone(),frs:f.fr.scale.clone()};
      saved.push(sv);
      if(f.sh&&f.sh.material)f.sh.visible=f.sh.material.opacity>.02;
      if(d.hideOl){if(!d.ols){d.ols=[];f.root.traverse(o=>{if(o.isMesh&&o.material&&o.material.side===THREE.BackSide)d.ols.push(o)})}d.ols.forEach(o=>o.visible=false)}
      /* arm length: stretch the hand away from the shoulder */
      if(al!==1){f.hl.position.copy(SL_).addScaledVector(_b.subVectors(sv.hl,SL_),al);f.hr.position.copy(SR_).addScaledVector(_b.subVectors(sv.hr,SR_),al)}
      /* leg length: lift the whole body and keep the feet on the floor */
      const dl=(ll-1)*.7;
      if(dl!==0){f.root.position.y+=dl*.65;f.fl.position.y-=dl;f.fr.position.y-=dl}
      if(ht!==1)f.root.scale.multiplyScalar(ht);
      if(gz!==1){f.hl.scale.multiplyScalar(gz);f.hr.scale.multiplyScalar(gz)}
      if(fz!==1){f.fl.scale.multiplyScalar(fz);f.fr.scale.multiplyScalar(fz)}
      /* limb meshes follow, with thickness */
      lim(f.aL,SL_,f.hl.position,aw);lim(f.aR,SR_,f.hr.position,aw);lim(f.lL,HL_,f.fl.position,lw);lim(f.lR,HR_,f.fr.position,lw);
      /* muscles */
      const mu=+c.mus||0;
      d.mus.forEach((m,k)=>{m.visible=mu>0;if(mu>0){const arm=k?f.aR:f.aL,hp=k?f.hr.position:f.hl.position,sh=k?SR_:SL_;
        m.position.lerpVectors(sh,hp,.38);m.position.y+=.02;const s=(.85+.45*mu)*(.7+.3*aw);m.scale.setScalar(s)}});
      if(d.bel){const b=+c.bel||0;d.bel.visible=b>0;if(b>0){const bs=f.body.scale;d.bel.position.set((.18+b*.06)*bs.x,.9*bs.y,0);d.bel.scale.set(.75+b*.17,.9+b*.1,.8+b*.2);}}
      /* bands */
      d.bands.forEach((m,k)=>{const on=!!hexC(c.wb,'');m.visible=on;if(on){const hp=k?f.hr.position:f.hl.position,sh=k?SR_:SL_;m.position.lerpVectors(sh,hp,.8);_a.subVectors(hp,sh).normalize();m.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(new V3(0,0,1),_a);m.scale.setScalar(Math.max(.6,aw));m.material.color.set(c.wb)}});
      d.ak.forEach((m,k)=>{const on=!!hexC(c.ak,'');m.visible=on;if(on){const hp=k?f.fr.position:f.fl.position,sh=k?HR_:HL_;m.position.lerpVectors(sh,hp,.82);_a.subVectors(hp,sh).normalize();m.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(new V3(0,0,1),_a);m.scale.setScalar(Math.max(.6,lw));m.material.color.set(c.ak)}});
      /* tail sway */
      if(d.tail){const sg=d.tail.userData.segs,k=d.tail.userData.kind;
        const mv=Math.min(1,Math.abs((f.x||0)-(d.px==null?f.x:d.px))*12);d.px=f.x||0;
        sg.forEach((s,j)=>{const w=Math.sin(t*(k==='lightning'?9:4)-j*.55)*(.08+.1*mv);s.rotation.z=w+(s.userData.zig?s.userData.zig*.9:0)});
        const base=d.tail.children[0];if(base&&k==='scorp'){}}
      /* hair / flame / pony */
      d.flames.forEach((m,j)=>{m.scale.set(1,.8+.5*Math.abs(Math.sin(t*9+j*1.7)),1)});
      if(d.pony)d.pony.rotation.z=Math.sin(t*3)*.12-.1+Math.min(.5,Math.abs((f.tx||0)-(f.x||0))*.4);
      /* orbiters */
      if(d.orb){const n=d.orbS.length,R=1.25,sp=c.orb==='planets'?.7:1.4;d.orbS.forEach((s,j)=>{const a=t*sp+j/n*PI*2;s.position.set(Math.cos(a)*R,Math.sin(a*2)*.12,Math.sin(a)*R*.9);s.material.rotation=c.orb==='stars'||c.orb==='snow'?t*2:0})}
      /* finish animation */
      if(d.fin){const hue=(t*.12)%1;d.fin.forEach(m=>{if(c.mf==='rainbow'){m.color.setHSL(hue,.7,.7)}else{const p=.5+.5*Math.sin(t*3);m.emissive=m.emissive||new THREE.Color();m.emissive.setRGB(1,.25+.15*p,0);m.emissiveIntensity=.35+.5*p}})}
      /* particles : auras and trails (world space) */
      const gp=f.g.position,wx=gp.x,wz=gp.z,wy=(gp.y||0)+(f.root.position.y||0)*.65;
      if(c.aura&&scn){
        const ac=hexC(c.auc,'')||{ring:'#fbbf24',flame:'#f97316',sparkle:'#fde047',hearts:'#f43f5e',electric:'#38bdf8',snow:'#e0f2fe',smoke:'#9ca3af',bubbles:'#7dd3fc',notes:'#a78bfa',petals:'#f9a8d4',embers:'#fb923c'}[c.aura]||'#fbbf24';
        d.ac=(d.ac||0)+dt;const rate={ring:.16,flame:.04,sparkle:.06,hearts:.15,electric:.07,snow:.08,smoke:.07,bubbles:.1,notes:.2,petals:.1,embers:.05}[c.aura]||.1;
        while(d.ac>rate){d.ac-=rate;const ang=Math.random()*PI*2,rr=.15+Math.random()*.5,p=new V3(wx+Math.cos(ang)*rr*.6,wy+.1,wz+Math.sin(ang)*rr*.6);
          switch(c.aura){
            case'ring':spawn('ring',ac,new V3(wx,wy+.06,wz),new V3(0,.05,0),.7,.5,2.4,{add:1});break;
            case'flame':spawn('puff',ac,p,new V3((Math.random()-.5)*.3,1.2+Math.random(),0),.7,.55,.15,{add:1});break;
            case'sparkle':spawn('spark',ac,p.setY(wy+Math.random()*1.7),new V3(0,.25,0),.9,.5,.05,{spin:3,add:1});break;
            case'hearts':spawn('heart',ac,p,new V3((Math.random()-.5)*.3,.7,0),1.3,.25,.5);break;
            case'electric':spawn('bolt',ac,p.setY(wy+Math.random()*1.8),new V3(0,0,0),.3,.55,.4,{add:1,spin:(Math.random()-.5)*6});break;
            case'snow':spawn('flake',ac,p.setY(wy+1.9).setX(wx+(Math.random()-.5)*1.4),new V3(0,-.7,0),1.8,.35,.3,{spin:2});break;
            case'smoke':spawn('puff',ac,p,new V3((Math.random()-.5)*.3,.5,0),1.4,.4,1,{});break;
            case'bubbles':spawn('bubble',ac,p,new V3((Math.random()-.5)*.2,.6,0),1.8,.3,.5);break;
            case'notes':spawn('note',ac,p,new V3((Math.random()-.5)*.6,.8,0),1.4,.28,.28,{spin:(Math.random()-.5)*2});break;
            case'petals':spawn('petal',ac,p.setY(wy+1.8),new V3((Math.random()-.5)*.6,-.5,0),2,.2,.2,{spin:2});break;
            case'embers':spawn('glow',ac,p,new V3((Math.random()-.5)*.5,1.4,0),.9,.3,.05,{add:1});break;
          }}}
      if(c.trail&&scn){
        const px=d.lx==null?wx:d.lx;const moved=Math.abs(wx-px)+Math.abs(wy-(d.ly||0));d.lx=wx;d.ly=wy;
        d.tc=(d.tc||0)+dt;
        if(moved>.012&&d.tc>.04){d.tc=0;const p=new V3(wx-Math.sign(wx-px||1)*.15,wy+.15+Math.random()*.2,wz+(Math.random()-.5)*.3);
          switch(c.trail){
            case'sparkle':spawn('spark','#fde047',p,new V3(0,.3,0),.6,.35,.05,{spin:3,add:1});break;
            case'ink':spawn('drop','#111827',p,new V3(0,-.1,0),.9,.3,.05);break;
            case'rainbow':spawn('glow','#'+new THREE.Color().setHSL((now/700)%1,1,.55).getHexString(),p,new V3(0,.1,0),.7,.5,.1,{add:1});break;
            case'fire':spawn('puff','#fb923c',p,new V3(0,.7,0),.55,.5,.1,{add:1});break;
            case'bubbles':spawn('bubble','#7dd3fc',p,new V3(0,.6,0),1.2,.18,.3);break;
            case'hearts':spawn('heart','#f43f5e',p,new V3(0,.5,0),1,.1,.3);break;
            case'stars':spawn('star','#fde047',p,new V3(0,.2,0),.9,.3,.05,{spin:2});break;
            case'smoke':spawn('puff','#9ca3af',p,new V3(0,.3,0),.9,.3,.9);break;
            case'ghost':spawn('glow','#c4b5fd',p.setY(wy+1),new V3(0,0,0),.4,1.2,.3,{add:1});break;
          }}}
    }
    stepParts(dt);
  }
  function post(){
    for(const sv of saved){const f=sv.f;
      f.hl.position.copy(sv.hl);f.hr.position.copy(sv.hr);f.fl.position.copy(sv.fl);f.fr.position.copy(sv.fr);f.root.position.y=sv.ry;f.root.scale.copy(sv.rs);
      f.hl.scale.copy(sv.gl);f.hr.scale.copy(sv.gr);f.fl.scale.copy(sv.fls);f.fr.scale.copy(sv.frs)}
    saved=[]}
  const rr0=ren.render.bind(ren);
  ren.render=function(s,c){try{pre()}catch(e){console.warn('cust2 pre',e&&e.message);saved=[]}try{return rr0(s,c)}finally{try{post()}catch(e){}}};

  /* the customizer shows P in a lobby view: make sure the first build includes the new bits */
  try{build('p');sync()}catch(e){console.warn(e)}
})();

</script>
<script>
/* ===== NET v3: sturdier connections for strict Wi-Fi / tablets =====
   - matchmaking-server retries with clear messages
   - longer join windows + early "ICE failed" detection
   - automatic RELAY fallback: if the direct link fails, retry through a TURN relay (works on most blocked networks)
   - a RELAY MODE switch (AUTO / ALWAYS) in the Online menu
   Nothing here touches saved data except one new key: db_relay. */
(()=>{
  if(typeof NET==='undefined'||typeof ICE==='undefined')return;
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const lg=k=>{try{return localStorage.getItem(k)}catch(e){return null}},ls=(k,v)=>{try{localStorage.setItem(k,v)}catch(e){}};
  let relayMode=lg('db_relay')==='1';
  const st={relayFailed:false};
  const say=t=>{try{nstat(t)}catch(e){}};
  const sleep=ms=>new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,ms));
  const last=t=>{window.__netLast=t};

  /* keep every relay (TURN) entry available, TCP/TLS 443 included, so tablets on strict Wi-Fi have a way through */
  try{
    const have=new Set();ICE.config.iceServers.forEach(s=>[].concat(s.urls).forEach(u=>have.add(u)));
    const add=(urls,u,c)=>{const f=urls.filter(x=>!have.has(x));if(f.length){ICE.config.iceServers.push({urls:f,username:u,credential:c});f.forEach(x=>have.add(x))}};
    add(['turn:openrelay.metered.ca:80','turn:openrelay.metered.ca:443','turn:openrelay.metered.ca:80?transport=tcp','turn:openrelay.metered.ca:443?transport=tcp','turns:openrelay.metered.ca:443?transport=tcp'],'openrelayproject','openrelayproject');
    add(['turn:eu-0.turn.peerjs.com:3478','turn:us-0.turn.peerjs.com:3478'],'peerjs','peerjsp');
    ICE.config.iceCandidatePoolSize=2;
  }catch(e){}

  const cfgFor=rel=>({config:Object.assign({},ICE.config,rel?{iceTransportPolicy:'relay'}:{})});
  function one(id,rel){
    return new Promise((res,rej)=>{
      if(typeof Peer==='undefined')return rej({type:'nopeer'});
      let p;try{p=id?new Peer(id,cfgFor(rel)):new Peer(cfgFor(rel))}catch(e){return rej({type:'ctor',message:e&&e.message})}
      p.__relay=!!rel;
      const to=setTimeout(()=>{try{p.destroy()}catch(e){}rej({type:'timeout'})},12000);
      p.on('open',()=>{clearTimeout(to);res(p)});
      p.on('error',e=>{clearTimeout(to);try{if(!p.open)p.destroy()}catch(x){}rej(e)});
    });
  }
  async function mkPeer(id,rel){
    let err;
    for(let t=0;t<3;t++){
      try{return await one(id,rel)}
      catch(e){err=e;const ty=e&&e.type;last('signal:'+(ty||e));
        if(ty==='unavailable-id'||ty==='nopeer'||ty==='invalid-id'||ty==='browser-incompatible'||ty==='ctor')throw e;
        if(t<2){say('Reaching the matchmaking server… (try '+(t+2)+'/3)');await sleep(1200*(t+1))}}
    }
    throw err;
  }
  window.peerNew=function(id){return mkPeer(id,relayMode)};

  /* one join attempt with its own diagnostics */
  function attempt(p,id,ms){
    return new Promise(res=>{
      let done=false,iv=null,c=null;
      const f=(conn,why)=>{if(done)return;done=true;clearInterval(iv);last('join '+id+': '+(conn?'ok':why)+(p.__relay?' (relay)':''));res({c:conn,why})};
      try{c=p.connect(id,{serialization:'json',reliable:true})}catch(e){return f(null,'error')}
      c.on('data',d=>{if(d&&d.k==='ok')f(c);else if(d&&d.k==='busy')f(null,'busy')});
      c.on('error',()=>f(null,'error'));c.on('close',()=>f(null,'closed'));
      p.on('error',e=>{if(e&&e.type==='peer-unavailable')f(null,'gone')});
      let hooked=false;
      iv=setInterval(()=>{const pc=c&&c.peerConnection;if(pc&&!hooked){hooked=true;pc.addEventListener('iceconnectionstatechange',()=>{if(pc.iceConnectionState==='failed')f(null,'ice')})}},200);
      setTimeout(()=>f(null,'timeout'),ms);
    });
  }
  window.tryJoin=async function(p,id){
    const q=id.startsWith('dbq-');
    const r=await attempt(p,id,q?9000:14000);
    if(r.c)return r.c;
    NET.why=r.why;
    if((r.why==='timeout'||r.why==='ice')&&!p.__relay&&!st.relayFailed){
      say('🛡 Direct link failed — trying relay mode… (up to 25 seconds)');
      let p2=null;
      try{p2=await mkPeer(null,true)}catch(e){st.relayFailed=true;return null}
      const r2=await attempt(p2,id,25000);
      if(r2.c)return r2.c;
      try{p2.destroy()}catch(e){}
      st.relayFailed=true;NET.why='relay-'+r2.why;
    }
    return null;
  };
  /* if the winning connection belongs to a different Peer (the relay one), make it the active one */
  const oAtt=window.attach;
  window.attach=function(c,role){
    try{const pv=c&&c.provider;if(pv&&NET.peer&&pv!==NET.peer){const old=NET.peer;NET.peer=pv;try{old.destroy()}catch(e){}}
      else if(pv&&!NET.peer)NET.peer=pv}catch(e){}
    return oAtt.apply(this,arguments)};
  /* fresh flow each time a button is pressed */
  for(const nm of['quick','join']){const o=window[nm];if(typeof o!=='function')continue;
    window[nm]=function(){st.relayFailed=false;NET.why=null;return o.apply(this,arguments)}}
  /* friendlier status lines */
  const nst=$('nst');
  if(nst){
    const FR={network:'Cannot reach the matchmaking server. Check the internet connection (or try another network).',
      'server-error':'The matchmaking server had a problem. Try again in a moment.','socket-error':'The connection to the matchmaking server dropped. Try again.',
      'socket-closed':'The connection to the matchmaking server dropped. Try again.',timeout:'The matchmaking server did not answer in time.',
      'browser-incompatible':'This browser cannot do online play (WebRTC missing).',ctor:'Online setup failed on this device.',nopeer:'The online library did not load. Reload the page.'};
    let busy=false;
    new MutationObserver(()=>{
      if(busy)return;const t=nst.innerText||'';let m=/^Connection error: (.+)$/.exec(t);
      if(m){busy=true;nst.innerText='❌ '+(FR[m[1]]||('Connection error: '+m[1]))+'  Tap 🩺 ONLINE CHECK below.';busy=false;return}
      if(/^Room not found or full$/.test(t)){busy=true;
        const w=NET.why;
        nst.innerText=(w==='gone'||!w)?'Room not found. Check the 4 letters, and the host must keep the Online screen open.'
          :(w==='busy'?'That room is already full.':'❌ Found the room but could not link up (network blocking?). Tap 🩺 ONLINE CHECK below, or try the other player as host.');
        busy=false}
    }).observe(nst,{childList:true,characterData:true,subtree:true});
  }
  /* relay mode switch */
  const box=document.querySelector('#netm .box');
  if(box&&!$('nRelay')){
    const b=document.createElement('button');b.className='btn';b.id='nRelay';
    const paint=()=>{b.textContent='🛡 RELAY MODE: '+(relayMode?'ALWAYS ON':'AUTO');b.title=relayMode?'All online traffic goes through a relay server (slower but works on strict networks).':'Tries a direct link first, then a relay if that fails.'};
    paint();b.onclick=()=>{relayMode=!relayMode;ls('db_relay',relayMode?'1':'0');paint();say(relayMode?'Relay mode ON: every connection goes through a relay. Slower, but works on strict Wi-Fi. Do this on BOTH devices if one still fails.':'Relay mode AUTO: direct first, relay only if needed.')};
    const anchor=$('nDiag')||$('mBtn')||$('nClose');anchor.before(b);
  }
})();

</script>
<script>
/* ===== FACES 3D: live face expressions for the 3D Story Part 2 cast =====
   - every spoken line paints the matching expression on the speaker's 3D head (same mood as the 2D portrait)
   - speakers flap their mouth while talking, everyone blinks, listeners react
   - in Story 2 fights the faces react to hits, stuns, low health, KOs and wins
   Only changes the head texture while Story 2 is running, and puts the original face back afterwards. */
(()=>{
  if(window.__F3||!window.S2E||typeof THREE==='undefined'||typeof fighter!=='function')return;
  window.__F3=1;
  const E=window.S2E;
  const S=256,K=S/512;
  const cache=new Map(),order=[];
  const touched=new Set();
  const now=()=>performance.now();

  /* ---------- painter (512-space coordinates, same layout as the game's own faces) ---------- */
  function paint(expr,ms,bl,ink){
    const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=S;const x=c.getContext('2d');
    x.scale(K,K);x.fillStyle='#fff';x.fillRect(0,0,512,512);
    ink=ink||'#111';x.fillStyle=x.strokeStyle=ink;x.lineWidth=26;x.lineCap=x.lineJoin='round';
    const dot=(a,b,r)=>{x.beginPath();x.arc(a,b,r,0,7);x.fill()};
    const arc=(a,b,r,s,t)=>{x.beginPath();x.arc(a,b,r,s*Math.PI,t*Math.PI);x.stroke()};
    const ln=(...p)=>{x.beginPath();x.moveTo(p[0],p[1]);for(let i=2;i<p.length;i+=2)x.lineTo(p[i],p[i+1]);x.stroke()};
    const open=(cx,cy,w,h,tongue)=>{x.beginPath();x.ellipse(cx,cy,w,h,0,0,7);x.fill();if(tongue){x.fillStyle='#f87171';x.beginPath();x.ellipse(cx,cy+h*.45,w*.6,h*.45,0,0,7);x.fill();x.fillStyle=ink}};
    const sleepy=bl;                       // blink flag
    const eyesDot=(y,r,dx)=>{if(sleepy){ln(150,y+4,210,y+4);ln(302,y+4,362,y+4)}else{dot(180+(dx||0),y,r);dot(332+(dx||0),y,r)}};
    const talkM=(cx,cy,big)=>{const w=ms===2?50:ms===1?38:0;if(!w)return false;open(cx,cy,w*(big||1),ms===2?40:20,ms===2);return true};
    switch(expr){
      case'happy':{if(sleepy||true){arc(180,228,32,1.15,1.85);arc(332,228,32,1.15,1.85)}
        if(!talkM(256,330))arc(256,245,120,.15,.85);break}
      case'smile':{eyesDot(210,22);if(!talkM(256,330))arc(256,255,115,.2,.8);break}
      case'cool':{x.fillRect(110,165,125,80);x.fillRect(277,165,125,80);x.fillRect(235,190,42,12);if(!talkM(256,330,.8))arc(256,300,78,.12,.62);break}
      case'angry':{eyesDot(235,20);ln(110,160,225,225);ln(402,160,287,225);if(!talkM(256,350))arc(256,385,90,1.2,1.8);break}
      case'evil':{x.lineWidth=22;ln(110,168,230,236);ln(402,168,282,236);x.lineWidth=26;if(sleepy){ln(140,240,215,240);ln(297,240,372,240)}else{x.beginPath();x.moveTo(135,246);x.lineTo(215,222);x.lineTo(215,252);x.closePath();x.fill();x.beginPath();x.moveTo(377,246);x.lineTo(297,222);x.lineTo(297,252);x.closePath();x.fill()}
        if(!talkM(256,335)){x.lineWidth=20;x.beginPath();x.moveTo(150,300);x.quadraticCurveTo(256,420,362,300);x.stroke();x.lineWidth=12;for(let i=0;i<5;i++)ln(190+i*33,330,190+i*33,352)}break}
      case'shock':{if(sleepy){ln(150,210,210,210);ln(302,210,362,210)}else{x.lineWidth=16;x.beginPath();x.arc(180,205,40,0,7);x.stroke();x.beginPath();x.arc(332,205,40,0,7);x.stroke();dot(186,205,11);dot(326,205,11)}
        x.lineWidth=26;open(256,345,ms===2?58:44,ms?72:60);ln(110,125,235,150);break}
      case'sad':{eyesDot(235,20);ln(120,190,215,155);ln(392,190,297,155);if(!talkM(256,365,.8))arc(256,420,85,1.2,1.8);break}
      case'worry':{x.lineWidth=20;eyesDot(230,16);x.lineWidth=20;ln(125,180,215,158);ln(387,180,297,158);x.lineWidth=22;
        if(!talkM(256,350,.8)){x.beginPath();x.moveTo(170,350);x.bezierCurveTo(205,320,225,380,256,350);x.bezierCurveTo(287,320,307,380,342,350);x.stroke()}break}
      case'wink':{dot(180,210,22);arc(332,228,32,1.15,1.85);if(!talkM(256,330))arc(256,255,115,.2,.8);break}
      case'derp':{if(sleepy){ln(150,210,210,210);ln(302,210,362,210)}else{dot(170,210,36);dot(345,200,15)}
        if(!talkM(256,350)){x.beginPath();x.moveTo(190,340);x.quadraticCurveTo(256,380,330,350);x.stroke()}break}
      case'smug':{x.lineWidth=22;ln(135,225,225,225);ln(287,225,377,225);dot(180,240,18);dot(332,240,18);ln(130,190,230,200);ln(282,200,382,190);x.lineWidth=26;
        if(!talkM(270,345,.8)){x.beginPath();x.moveTo(175,340);x.quadraticCurveTo(290,390,350,315);x.stroke()}break}
      case'laugh':{arc(180,235,32,1.15,1.85);arc(332,235,32,1.15,1.85);open(256,330,ms===2?95:80,ms===1?52:70,true);break}
      case'cry':{x.lineWidth=22;arc(180,205,30,.1,.9);arc(332,205,30,.1,.9);x.strokeStyle='#38bdf8';x.lineWidth=18;ln(160,250,150,330);ln(352,250,362,330);x.strokeStyle=ink;x.lineWidth=26;
        if(!talkM(256,375))arc(256,420,75,1.15,1.85);break}
      case'think':{if(sleepy){ln(150,215,210,215);ln(302,215,362,215)}else{dot(195,205,22);dot(347,205,22)}x.lineWidth=20;ln(125,165,225,160);ln(287,135,387,150);x.lineWidth=26;
        if(!talkM(285,345,.7))ln(215,345,330,335);break}
      case'proud':{arc(180,250,34,1.1,1.9);arc(332,250,34,1.1,1.9);x.lineWidth=20;ln(125,170,225,160);ln(287,160,387,170);x.lineWidth=26;
        if(!talkM(256,330))arc(256,270,100,.2,.8);break}
      case'dead':{x.lineWidth=22;ln(140,170,210,240);ln(210,170,140,240);ln(302,170,372,240);ln(372,170,302,240);ln(170,365,210,335,256,365,300,335,340,365);break}
      case'dizzy':{x.lineWidth=14;for(const cx of[180,332]){x.beginPath();x.arc(cx,210,34,0,7);x.stroke();x.beginPath();x.arc(cx,210,16,0,7);x.stroke()}x.lineWidth=24;ln(190,350,250,370,310,340);break}
      case'ouch':{x.lineWidth=22;ln(140,170,215,210,140,250);ln(372,170,297,210,372,250);x.lineWidth=26;open(256,345,50,40);break}
      case'sleepy':{ln(150,215,210,215);ln(302,215,362,215);open(256,335,28,22);break}
      default:{eyesDot(210,22);if(!talkM(256,330))arc(256,255,115,.2,.8)}
    }
    return c}
  function tex(expr,ms,bl,ink){
    const k=expr+'|'+ms+'|'+bl+'|'+ink;let t=cache.get(k);if(t)return t;
    t=new THREE.CanvasTexture(paint(expr,ms,bl,ink));cache.set(k,t);order.push(k);
    if(order.length>90){const o=order.shift(),ot=cache.get(o);cache.delete(o);try{ot.dispose()}catch(e){}}
    return t}

  /* ---------- per-fighter state ---------- */
  const NORM={smiley:'smile',happy:'happy',cool:'cool',angry:'angry',shocked:'shock',shock:'shock',sad:'sad',wink:'wink',derp:'derp',smug:'smug',laugh:'laugh',cry:'cry',think:'think',proud:'proud',worry:'worry',evil:'evil',sleepy:'sleepy',love:'happy',silly:'derp',dead:'dead',dizzy:'dizzy',ouch:'ouch'};
  const norm=e=>NORM[e]||'smile';
  const head=f=>{if(f.__hm===undefined)f.__hm=(f.head&&f.head.children.find(c=>c.material&&c.material.map))||null;return f.__hm};
  function st(f){return f.__f3||(f.__f3={expr:null,hold:0,ms:0,bl:0,nextBlink:now()+1500+Math.random()*2500,blUntil:0,cur:'',talk:false,base:null})}
  function put(f,key,t){const hm=head(f);if(!hm)return;if(!f.__orig)f.__orig=hm.material.map;const s=st(f);if(s.cur===key)return;s.cur=key;hm.material.map=t;touched.add(f)}
  function restore(f){const hm=head(f);if(hm&&f.__orig){hm.material.map=f.__orig}const s=f.__f3;if(s){s.cur='';s.expr=null;s.hold=0}touched.delete(f)}
  const inkOf=f=>{try{return (f.dc&&f.dc.cfg.fc)||'#111'}catch(e){return'#111'}};

  /* public: set a held expression on an actor id (speaker / reaction) */
  const F3=window.F3={
    set(id,expr,ms){const a=E.actors&&E.actors[id];if(!a||!a.f)return;const s=st(a.f);s.expr=norm(expr);s.hold=ms==null?0:now()+ms},
    clear(id){const a=E.actors[id];if(a&&a.f){const s=st(a.f);s.expr=null;s.hold=0}},
    restoreAll(){[...touched].forEach(restore)},
    tex
  };

  /* ---------- hook the dialogue: mirror the portrait's expression onto the 3D head ---------- */
  const DF=window.DFACE;let inSay=false,capt=null;
  if(DF&&DF.make&&!DF.__f3){const om=DF.make;DF.make=function(who,expr,over){if(inSay&&capt===null)capt=expr;return om.apply(this,arguments)};DF.__f3=1}
  const oSay=E.say;
  const FOEISH0=/^(forger|forger2|mimic|ringm|siren|leviathan|brush|brush2|brush3|erased|iron|storm|copycat|quill2)$/;
  const FOEISH={test:id=>FOEISH0.test(id)||!!(window.S2&&S2.FOES&&S2.FOES.has&&S2.FOES.has(id))};
  E.say=function(who,text,o){
    let p;inSay=true;capt=null;
    try{p=oSay.apply(this,arguments)}finally{inSay=false}
    try{
      if(who!=='narr'){
        const ex=norm(capt||(o&&o.e)||'happy');
        const a=E.actors[who]||E.actors.hero;
        if(a&&a.f){const s=st(a.f);s.expr=ex;s.hold=0;s.talk=true}
        /* listeners react */
        const foe=FOEISH.test(who);
        Object.keys(E.actors).forEach(id=>{const b=E.actors[id];if(!b||!b.vis||id===who||!b.f)return;const s=st(b.f),bf=FOEISH.test(id);let r=null;
          if(foe&&!bf){r=/^(angry|evil|laugh|smug)$/.test(ex)?'worry':ex==='shock'?'shock':null}
          else if(!foe&&bf){r=/^(sad|cry|worry)$/.test(ex)?'smug':/^(cool|proud|angry)$/.test(ex)?'angry':null}
          else if(!foe&&!bf){r=/^(sad|cry|worry)$/.test(ex)?'sad':/^(laugh|happy|proud|wink)$/.test(ex)?'happy':ex==='angry'?'worry':ex==='shock'?'shock':null}
          s.expr=r;s.hold=0});
      }
    }catch(e){}
    if(p&&p.then){const done=()=>{try{const a=E.actors[who]||E.actors.hero;if(a&&a.f){const s=st(a.f);s.talk=false;s.hold=now()+1800}}catch(e){}};p.then(done,done)}
    return p};

  /* ---------- the ticker ---------- */
  const BASEX={scribble:'happy',blot:'derp',pixel:'cool',forger:'smile',forger2:'angry',mimic:'smile',ringm:'angry',siren:'smile',leviathan:'angry',brush:'cool',erased:'shock',iron:'angry',storm:'cool',gink:'sad',gzip:'happy',grobo:'sad',gtitan:'sad',quill:'sad',copycat:'angry'};
  function apply(f,id,hero,orig){
    const s=st(f),t=now();
    if(s.hold&&t>s.hold){s.expr=null;s.hold=0}
    if(t>s.nextBlink){s.blUntil=t+130;s.nextBlink=t+2200+Math.random()*3200}
    const bl=t<s.blUntil?1:0;
    let ex=s.expr;
    if(!ex&&(hero||orig)){if(s.cur!==''){const hm=head(f);if(hm&&f.__orig){hm.material.map=f.__orig}s.cur=''}return}   /* hero idle = the player's own face */
    if(!ex){ex=BASEX[id]||(f.__cfgExpr?norm(f.__cfgExpr):'smile')}
    const talk=s.talk?[0,1,2,1][Math.floor(t/110)%4]:0;
    const key=ex+'|'+talk+'|'+bl;
    if(s.cur===key)return;
    put(f,key,tex(ex,talk,bl,inkOf(f)));
  }
  function fightFace(f,isHero){
    const s=st(f),t=now();
    if(f.hit>0||f.stun>0)s.hurtUntil=t+420;
    let ex=null;
    if(f.ko||f.hp<=0)ex='dead';
    else if(f.win)ex='laugh';
    else if(t<(s.hurtUntil||0))ex=f.stun>.25?'dizzy':'ouch';
    else if(f.punch>0)ex='angry';
    else if(f.hp<30)ex='worry';
    else if(f.block)ex='smug';
    s.expr=ex;s.hold=0;s.talk=false;
    apply(f,'fighter',!ex,true);
  }
  setInterval(()=>{
    try{
      if(document.hidden)return;
      const cut=E.on&&!E.susp,fight=!!(E.fight&&typeof G!=='undefined'&&G.mode==='match'&&G.bot&&G.bot.s2);
      if(cut){
        Object.keys(E.actors).forEach(id=>{const a=E.actors[id];if(!a||!a.vis||!a.f)return;
          if(a.f.__cfgExpr===undefined){a.f.__cfgExpr=null;try{a.f.__cfgExpr=(a.f.dc&&a.f.dc.cfg&&a.f.dc.cfg.expr)||null}catch(e){}}
          apply(a.f,id,id==='hero')});
      }else if(fight){
        fightFace(P,true);if(O)fightFace(O,false);
      }else if(touched.size){F3.restoreAll()}
    }catch(e){}
  },70);
  /* cfg expr for actors (fighters carry the cfg they were built from through cust2's decorate; fall back to LOOK via actor ids) */
})();

</script>
<script>
/* ===== STORY PART 3 ENGINE: comic panels, cut-ins, tap/timing set pieces, dialogue choices, pixel mode,
   weather, giant props, hero "Ultra Doodle" form, credits roll, and new boss mechanics. Built on the Part 2 stage engine. ===== */
(()=>{
  const S2=window.S2;if(!S2||!S2.S||window.S3)return;
  const {S,E}=S2,F=S.F,F2=S.F2,V=E.V,rnd=E.rnd,pick=E.pick,lerp=E.lerp,eO=E.eO;
  const $$=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const ui=E.ui;
  const X=window.S3={};
  const PI=Math.PI;
  const sk=()=>E.skip?40:1;

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ styles */
  document.head.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`<style>
  .s3pan{position:absolute;inset:0;z-index:7;pointer-events:none;overflow:hidden}
  .s3pn{position:absolute;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;overflow:hidden;
    background:radial-gradient(circle,rgba(0,0,0,.22) 20%,transparent 22%) 0 0/13px 13px,linear-gradient(135deg,var(--a),var(--b));
    filter:drop-shadow(0 0 0 #000);animation:s3pn .32s cubic-bezier(.2,1.5,.4,1) both}
  .s3pn b{font:900 min(13vw,92px)/.95 'Fredoka',system-ui;color:#fff;-webkit-text-stroke:4px #000;paint-order:stroke fill;text-shadow:5px 5px 0 #000;text-align:center;padding:0 3%;transform:rotate(var(--r,-4deg))}
  .s3pn i{position:absolute;inset:0;border:5px solid #000;pointer-events:none}

  X.tap=function(o){
    o=Object.assign({label:'MASH!',need:22,ms:6500,c:'#fde047'},o||{});
    return new Promise(res=>{
      if(E.skip||E.abort){res({ok:true,hits:o.need,assist:true});return}
      const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='s3q';
      d.innerHTML='<div class="s3ql">'+o.label+'</div><button class="s3qb" style="--c:'+o.c+'">TAP!</button><div class="s3qm"><i></i></div>';
      ui.appendChild(d);const bar=d.querySelector('i'),btn=d.querySelector('button');
      let n=0,done=false;
      const fin=ok=>{if(done)return;done=true;clearTimeout(to);clearInterval(chk);removeEventListener('keydown',kd,true);
        d.querySelector('.s3ql').textContent=ok?'YES!!':'ASSIST!';E.sfx(ok?'heroic':'chime');
        setTimeout(()=>{d.remove();res({ok,hits:n,assist:!ok})},520)};
      const hit=e=>{if(e){e.preventDefault&&e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation&&e.stopPropagation()}if(done)return;n++;bar.style.width=Math.min(100,n/o.need*100)+'%';
        E.sfx('hit');G.shake=Math.max(G.shake||0,.12);E.cam.fovPunch(4);if(o.onhit)try{o.onhit(n/o.need)}catch(x){}
        if(Math.random()<.3)F2.onomat(pick(['POW!','BAM!','HIYA!','WHAM!','YES!']),pick(['#fde047','#f43f5e','#38bdf8']),20+Math.random()*60,15+Math.random()*50);
        if(n>=o.need)fin(true)};
      const kd=e=>{if(e.key===' '||e.key==='Enter'||e.key==='j'||e.key==='J'){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();hit()}};
      btn.addEventListener('pointerdown',hit);d.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>e.stopPropagation());addEventListener('keydown',kd,true);
      const to=setTimeout(()=>fin(false),o.ms);
      const chk=setInterval(()=>{if(E.abort||E.skip)fin(true)},150);
    });
  };
  X.timing=function(n,o){

        t.innerHTML='<div class="c"></div><div class="r"></div>';root.appendChild(t);const t0=performance.now();let used=false;

      });

      root.remove();res({hits,total:n});
    });
  };

  let ultra=false,ultraP=null,ultraObjs=[];
  function mkUltra(){
    try{
      ultraObjs.forEach(o=>{if(o.parent)o.parent.remove(o)});ultraObjs=[];
      const M=(c,o)=>new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial(Object.assign({color:c,transparent:true,opacity:.95,blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending,depthWrite:false,side:2},o||{}));

      for(const sg of[1,-1]){

      const glow=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(1.7,14,12),M(0xfde047,{opacity:.14}));glow.position.y=1.4;P.root.add(glow);ultraObjs.push(glow);
      ultraP=P;
    }catch(e){}
  }

  $$('s3New').onclick=()=>{S2.camp(CUR3);const V=sv();if(V.u>0&&!confirm('Start Part 3 over? Your saved Part 3 progress will be reset.'))return;Object.assign(V,{u:0,pos:{c:0,b:0},done:false,lost:0,perfect:0,bless:false,hug:false,tapAll:true,end:null});persist();begin(0,0)};
  $$('s3X').onclick=()=>$$('s3m').classList.add('hide');
  $$('s3List').onclick=e=>{const b=e.target.closest('[data-ch]');if(!b)return;S2.camp(CUR3);begin(+b.dataset.ch,0)};
  X.open=()=>{if(G.online||NET.role){toast('Story mode is offline only');return}openMenu()};

  /* ---------------- button inside the Story menu ---------------- */
  const stX=$$('stX');
  if(stX){
    const b=document.createElement('button');b.id='stS3';b.className='btn';
    b.style.cssText='margin-top:8px;width:100%;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#7f1d1d,#ea580c);font-weight:700';
    const ref=()=>{const u=unlocked();b.textContent=u?'🖍️ STORY PART 3 · THE FINAL DRAFT':'🔒 STORY PART 3 · tap to see what it takes';b.style.opacity=u?1:.6;b.classList.toggle('new',u&&!(J('db_story3')||{}).seen)};
    b.onclick=()=>{$$('stm').classList.add('hide');X.open()};
    const ref2=$$('stS2');(ref2||stX).parentNode.insertBefore(b,ref2?ref2.nextSibling:stX);
    ref();setInterval(ref,1500);
    try{new MutationObserver(ref).observe($$('stm'),{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']})}catch(e){}
  }
  X.ready=true;

  /* ---------------- chapter helpers shared with s3chap*.js ---------------- */
  const h={
    L:(who,t,o)=>({k:'say',w:who,t,o}),
    D:(s,o)=>{const i=s.indexOf('|');return {k:'say',w:s.slice(0,i),t:s.slice(i+1),o}},
    R:fn=>({k:'run',fn}),
    CP:(add,st,lie)=>({k:'cp',add,st,lie}),
    FT:def=>Object.assign({k:'fight'},def),
    N:(t,o)=>({k:'say',w:'narr',t,o}),
    re:name=>({k:'run',fn:async s=>{X.reapply(name)}}),
    hold:ms=>({k:'run',fn:async s=>{await s.w(ms)}}),
    out:u=>1-Math.pow(1-u,3),
    /* tap/timing challenges record whether the player aced them for the chapter achievement */
    tapped:r=>{if(!r||!r.ok||r.assist)sv().tapAll=false},
    timed:r=>{if(!r||r.hits<r.total)sv().tapAll=false},
    ending:id=>{
      const a=J('db_s3ends')||[];if(a.indexOf(id)<0)a.push(id);LS.s('db_s3ends',JSON.stringify(a));
      give(id==='forgive'?'s3end1':id==='erase'?'s3end2':'s3end3');
      if(a.length>=3)give('s3all');
    }
  };
  X.h=h;
})();

</script>

    }),
    D('redpen|There is no draft, no revision, no appeal. There is only the line that ends the story.',{e:'angry',noenter:true}),

    R(async s=>{
      const e=sv().end;

      if(e==='forgive'){

      }else if(e==='erase'){

  #c3fx .f{position:absolute;top:-30px;font-size:26px;animation:c3fall var(--t,1.2s) cubic-bezier(.4,0,.8,.6) var(--d,0s) forwards;will-change:transform,opacity}

  #c3fx .stamp{position:absolute;left:50%;top:34%;font:900 clamp(34px,10vw,64px) 'Fredoka',system-ui;color:#fde047;-webkit-text-stroke:3px #7c2d12;text-shadow:0 6px 0 #7c2d12,0 0 26px #f59e0b;white-space:nowrap;animation:c3stamp 1.2s cubic-bezier(.2,1.6,.4,1) forwards}
  @keyframes c3stamp{0%{transform:translate(-50%,-50%) scale(3) rotate(-12deg);opacity:0}25%{transform:translate(-50%,-50%) scale(.95) rotate(-4deg);opacity:1}40%{transform:translate(-50%,-50%) scale(1.08) rotate(-4deg)}75%{opacity:1}100%{transform:translate(-50%,-62%) scale(1) rotate(-4deg);opacity:0}}
  #c3fx .ring{position:absolute;width:20px;height:20px;margin:-10px 0 0 -10px;border-radius:50%;border:4px solid var(--c,#fde047);animation:c3ring .6s ease-out forwards}
  @keyframes c3ring{from{transform:scale(.3);opacity:.9}to{transform:scale(9);opacity:0}}
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){#cust *,#pvbar,#pvbar *,#c3fx *{animation:none!important}}
  `;
  document.head.appendChild(css);

  /* ---- rarity glow (by coin price) + staggered pop-in ---- */
  function decorate(){
    cust.querySelectorAll('.opt,.sw').forEach(b=>{
      const p=PRICES[b.dataset.k+':'+b.dataset.v]||0;
      const r=p>=300?'l':p>=150?'e':p>=60?'r':'';
      if(r)b.dataset.rar=r;else delete b.dataset.rar});
    cust.querySelectorAll('.tabsec').forEach(s=>{let i=0;s.querySelectorAll('.opt,.sw').forEach(b=>{b.style.setProperty('--i',Math.min(i++,22))})})}
  decorate();

  /* ---- drifting doodles behind the menu ---- */
  if(!RM){
    const bg=document.createElement('div');bg.className='c3bg';
    const E=['✏️','⭐','✨','🖍️','🎨','💫','📐','🖌️','☁️'];
    for(let i=0;i<9;i++){const e=document.createElement('i');e.textContent=E[i%E.length];
      e.style.cssText='left:'+(6+i*10.5)+'%;font-size:'+(18+(i*7)%16)+'px;animation-duration:'+(11+(i*3)%9)+'s;animation-delay:-'+((i*2.3)%11)+'s';bg.appendChild(e)}
    cust.insertBefore(bg,cust.firstChild)}

  /* ---- particle layer ---- */
  let fx=null;
  function layer(){if(!fx){fx=document.createElement('div');fx.id='c3fx';document.body.appendChild(fx)}return fx}
  function rm(e,ms){setTimeout(()=>{try{e.remove()}catch(x){}},ms)}
  function burst(x,y,em,n,spread,col){
    if(RM)return;const L=layer();
    const ring=document.createElement('div');ring.className='ring';ring.style.left=x+'px';ring.style.top=y+'px';if(col)ring.style.setProperty('--c',col);L.appendChild(ring);rm(ring,700);
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
      const p=document.createElement('div');p.className='p';p.textContent=em[i%em.length];
      const a=Math.random()*Math.PI*2,d=spread*(.45+Math.random()*.7);
      p.style.left=x+'px';p.style.top=y+'px';
      p.style.setProperty('--dx',Math.cos(a)*d+'px');p.style.setProperty('--dy',(Math.sin(a)*d-spread*.25)+'px');
      p.style.setProperty('--r',(Math.random()*540-270)+'deg');p.style.setProperty('--s',(.7+Math.random()*.9).toFixed(2));p.style.setProperty('--t',(.55+Math.random()*.5).toFixed(2)+'s');
      L.appendChild(p);rm(p,1200)}}
  function coinRain(n){
    if(RM)return;const L=layer();
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const c=document.createElement('div');c.className='f';c.textContent=i%5===0?'✨':'🪙';
      c.style.left=(Math.random()*96)+'%';c.style.setProperty('--d',(Math.random()*.5).toFixed(2)+'s');c.style.setProperty('--t',(1+Math.random()*.7).toFixed(2)+'s');c.style.setProperty('--r',(Math.random()*720-360)+'deg');
      L.appendChild(c);rm(c,2400)}}
  function stamp(t){
    if(RM)return;const L=layer(),s=document.createElement('div');s.className='stamp';s.textContent=t;L.appendChild(s);rm(s,1400)}
  function fighterPos(){
    const r=cust.getBoundingClientRect();
    if(r.width>=innerWidth*.9)return[innerWidth/2,Math.max(60,r.top*.5)];
    return[Math.max(60,r.left*.5),innerHeight*.45]}
  /* little spin + camera nudge on the fighter */
  let spinning=false;
  function spin(turns){
    if(RM||spinning||typeof P==='undefined'||!P||!P.g)return;spinning=true;
    const t0=performance.now(),D=700*turns;let prev=0;
    try{if(typeof camTo!=='undefined'){camTo.z-=.22;setTimeout(()=>{try{camTo.z+=.22}catch(e){}},200)}}catch(e){}
    (function f(now){
      const t=Math.min(1,(now-t0)/D),e=1-Math.pow(1-t,3),off=e*Math.PI*2*turns;
      try{P.ry+=off-prev}catch(x){}prev=off;
      if(t<1)requestAnimationFrame(f);
      else setTimeout(()=>{try{const w=Math.PI*2*turns;P.ry-=w;P.g.rotation.y-=w}catch(x){}spinning=false},350)})(t0)}
  function pop(el,s){try{el.animate([{transform:'scale(1)'},{transform:'scale('+(s||1.28)+')'},{transform:'scale(.95)'},{transform:'scale(1)'}],{duration:340,easing:'ease-out'})}catch(e){}}

  /* ---- equip / try-on reaction ---- */
  const EM={'':['✨'],r:['✨','⭐'],e:['💜','✨','🔮'],l:['🌈','⭐','✨','💫','🔥']};
  cust.addEventListener('click',ev=>{
    const b=ev.target.closest&&ev.target.closest('.opt,.sw');
    if(b){setTimeout(()=>{
      const rar=b.dataset.rar||'';
      pop(b,rar==='l'?1.4:1.28);
      if(!(b.classList.contains('on')||b.classList.contains('pv')))return;
      const r=b.getBoundingClientRect();
      burst(r.left+r.width/2,r.top+r.height/2,EM[rar],rar==='l'?12:rar==='e'?9:6,rar==='l'?90:70,rar==='l'?'#f43f5e':rar==='e'?'#c084fc':'#fde047');
      const [fx0,fy0]=fighterPos();burst(fx0,fy0,EM[rar],rar==='l'?14:rar==='e'?10:5,rar==='l'?150:110,rar==='l'?'#f43f5e':'#fde047');
      if(rar==='l'||rar==='e')spin(1)},30);return}
    const rb=ev.target.closest&&ev.target.closest('.c2b,#bRnd');
    if(rb){const dice=/Random/i.test(rb.textContent);
      if(dice){rb.classList.remove('c3b');void rb.offsetWidth;rb.classList.add('c3b');const[x,y]=fighterPos();burst(x,y,['🎲','✨','⭐','💫','🎉'],16,170);spin(2)}
      else if(/Reset|Clear/i.test(rb.textContent)){const[x,y]=fighterPos();burst(x,y,['💨','☁️'],8,120,'#94a3b8')}}
  },true);

  /* ---- slider value pop ---- */
  cust.addEventListener('input',ev=>{const t=ev.target;if(!t||t.type!=='range')return;
    const h=t.previousElementSibling,sp=h&&h.querySelector&&h.querySelector('span');if(sp){sp.style.display='inline-block';pop(sp,1.4)}},true);

  /* ---- buy button: refused -> shake ---- */
  document.addEventListener('click',ev=>{const b=ev.target.closest&&ev.target.closest('#pvBuy');if(!b)return;
    const before=W.c;setTimeout(()=>{if(W.c>=before){const bar=$$('pvbar');if(bar){bar.classList.remove('c3shake');void bar.offsetWidth;bar.classList.add('c3shake');setTimeout(()=>bar.classList.remove('c3shake'),450)}}},80)},true);

  /* ---- coin counter: counts up/down, flashes, rains coins when you spend ---- */
  const c2=$$('coins2');
  if(c2){
    let shown=parseInt(c2.textContent.replace(/\D/g,''))||0,mine=c2.textContent,raf=0,target=shown;
    const mo=new MutationObserver(()=>{
      if(c2.textContent===mine)return;
      const to=parseInt(c2.textContent.replace(/\D/g,''));if(isNaN(to)){mine=c2.textContent;return}
      if(to===target){c2.textContent=mine;return}
      target=to;
      const from=shown,down=to<from,vis=!cust.classList.contains('hide');
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      if(vis&&!RM){
        c2.classList.remove('c3dn','c3up');void c2.offsetWidth;c2.classList.add(down?'c3dn':'c3up');
        if(down){coinRain(Math.min(26,8+Math.round((from-to)/25)));stamp('UNLOCKED! 🎉');const[x,y]=fighterPos();burst(x,y,['🎉','✨','⭐','🪙','💫'],18,170,'#4ade80');spin(2)}
        const t0=performance.now();
        (function f(now){const t=Math.min(1,(now-t0)/700),v=Math.round(from+(to-from)*(1-Math.pow(1-t,3)));
          mine='🪙 '+v;c2.textContent=mine;shown=v;
          if(t<1)raf=requestAnimationFrame(f);else{shown=to}})(t0)}
      else{shown=to;mine=c2.textContent}
    });
    mo.observe(c2,{childList:true,characterData:true,subtree:true});
  }
  /* ---- re-decorate when the lab opens (buttons may change) ---- */
  const co=new MutationObserver(()=>{if(!cust.classList.contains('hide'))decorate()});
  co.observe(cust,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
})();

</script>
