  /* ---- title letters wave ---- */
  function wrap(node,st){
    [...node.childNodes].forEach(n=>{
      if(n.nodeType===3){
        const f=document.createDocumentFragment();
        for(const ch of n.textContent){
          if(ch===' '){f.appendChild(document.createTextNode(' '));continue}
          const s=document.createElement('span');s.className='c4l';s.style.setProperty('--i',st.i++);s.textContent=ch;f.appendChild(s)}
        n.replaceWith(f)}
      else if(n.nodeType===1)wrap(n,st)})}
  const h1=lobby.querySelector('#lobHead h1')||lobby.querySelector('h1');
  if(h1&&!RM&&h1.textContent.trim().length<24){try{wrap(h1,{i:0})}catch(e){}}

  /* ---- drifting doodles behind the lobby ---- */
  if(!RM){
    const bg=document.createElement('div');bg.className='c4bg';
    const E=['✏️','⭐','✨','🖍️','🥊','💥','🎨','💫','📐','🏆'];
    for(let i=0;i<10;i++){const e=document.createElement('i');e.textContent=E[i%E.length];
      e.style.cssText='left:'+(4+i*9.5)+'%;font-size:'+(18+(i*7)%18)+'px;animation-duration:'+(12+(i*3)%10)+'s;animation-delay:-'+((i*2.7)%12)+'s';bg.appendChild(e)}
    lobby.insertBefore(bg,lobby.firstChild)}

  /* ---- shine sweep on solid-colour buttons (gradient ones keep their gradient) ---- */
  function sheen(){
    lobby.querySelectorAll('.btn').forEach(b=>{
      if(b.classList.contains('c4sh')||b.id==='bPlay')return;
      try{if(getComputedStyle(b).backgroundImage==='none')b.classList.add('c4sh')}catch(e){}})}
  if(!RM)sheen();

  /* ---- tip text changes: fade in ---- */
  const tip=$$('lobTip');
  if(tip){new MutationObserver(()=>{tip.classList.remove('c4t');void tip.offsetWidth;tip.classList.add('c4t')}).observe(tip,{childList:true,characterData:true,subtree:true})}

  /* ---- number count-up when the lobby shows ---- */
  function countUp(el){
    const txt=el.textContent,m=txt.match(/^(\D*)(\d[\d,]*)(\D*)$/);if(!m)return;
    const to=parseInt(m[2].replace(/,/g,''));if(!(to>0)||to>1e7)return;
    const t0=performance.now();let last=null;
    (function f(now){
      if(last!==null&&el.textContent!==last)return;               // someone else changed it: stop
      const t=Math.min(1,(now-t0)/800),v=Math.round(to*(1-Math.pow(1-t,3)));
      last=m[1]+(m[2].includes(',')?v.toLocaleString():v)+m[3];el.textContent=last;
      if(t<1)requestAnimationFrame(f)})(t0)}
  let wasHid=lobby.classList.contains('hide');
  new MutationObserver(()=>{
    const h=lobby.classList.contains('hide');
    if(wasHid&&!h&&!RM)setTimeout(()=>{['lcW','lcL','lcK','lcM','lcC'].forEach(i=>{const e=$$(i);e&&countUp(e)});sheen()},120);
    wasHid=h}).observe(lobby,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  if(!RM)setTimeout(()=>{['lcW','lcL','lcK','lcM','lcC'].forEach(i=>{const e=$$(i);e&&countUp(e)})},500);

  /* ---- ON / OFF dots on setting buttons ---- */
  function tagToggles(root){
    (root||document).querySelectorAll('#setm .btn,#pause .btn,#featm .btn,.featBox .btn').forEach(b=>{
      const m=/:\s*(ON|OFF)\b/i.exec(b.textContent);
      if(m){const v=m[1].toLowerCase();if(b.dataset.st!==v){const had=b.dataset.st;b.dataset.st=v;if(had&&!RM){b.classList.remove('c4flip');void b.offsetWidth;b.classList.add('c4flip');setTimeout(()=>b.classList.remove('c4flip'),500)}}}
      else if(b.dataset.st)delete b.dataset.st})}
  tagToggles();
  new MutationObserver(()=>tagToggles()).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true,characterData:true});

  /* ---- effects layer ---- */
  let fx=null;
  function layer(){if(!fx){fx=document.createElement('div');fx.id='c4fx';document.body.appendChild(fx)}return fx}
  function rm(e,ms){setTimeout(()=>{try{e.remove()}catch(x){}},ms)}
  function tapFx(x,y){
    const L=layer(),r=document.createElement('div');r.className='r';r.style.left=x+'px';r.style.top=y+'px';L.appendChild(r);rm(r,600);
    for(let i=0;i<6;i++){const s=document.createElement('div');s.className='s';const a=i/6*Math.PI*2+Math.random()*.5,d=26+Math.random()*22;
      s.style.left=x+'px';s.style.top=y+'px';s.style.setProperty('--dx',Math.cos(a)*d+'px');s.style.setProperty('--dy',Math.sin(a)*d+'px');
      s.style.background=['#fde047','#fb923c','#f472b6','#38bdf8','#4ade80'][i%5];L.appendChild(s);rm(s,600)}}
  function confetti(n,em){
    const L=layer();
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const c=document.createElement('div');c.className='f';c.textContent=em[i%em.length];
      c.style.left=(Math.random()*96)+'%';c.style.setProperty('--d',(Math.random()*.9).toFixed(2)+'s');c.style.setProperty('--t',(1.4+Math.random()*1.1).toFixed(2)+'s');c.style.setProperty('--r',(Math.random()*720-360)+'deg');
      L.appendChild(c);rm(c,3600)}}
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    if(RM)return;const t=e.target;if(!t||!t.closest)return;
    if(t.closest('#hud,#cust,#pvbar'))return;
    try{if(typeof S2E!=='undefined'&&S2E.on)return}catch(x){}
    if(t.closest('#lobby button,#lobby select,.modal button,.modal select'))tapFx(e.clientX,e.clientY)},true);

  /* ---- result screen: confetti on a win ---- */
  const res=$$('result');
  if(res){
    let was=res.classList.contains('hide');
    new MutationObserver(()=>{
      const h=res.classList.contains('hide');
      if(was&&!h&&!RM)setTimeout(()=>{
        const t=($$('rT')||{}).textContent||'';
        const lose=/DEFEAT|LOST|LOSE|KO|OUT/i.test(t)&&!/VICTORY|WIN/i.test(t);
        res.classList.toggle('c4lose',lose);
        if(!lose)confetti(34,['🎉','🏆','⭐','✨','🎊','🪙'])},60);
      was=h}).observe(res,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  }

  /* ---- newest menu always on top (menus opened from settings used to hide behind it) ---- */
  (function(){
    const zOf=m=>parseInt(getComputedStyle(m).zIndex)||0;
    function check(m){
      if(!m||!m.classList||!m.classList.contains('modal'))return;
      if(m.classList.contains('hide')){
        if(m.dataset.c4z){m.style.zIndex=m.dataset.c4o||'';delete m.dataset.c4z;delete m.dataset.c4o}
        return}
      if(m.dataset.c4z)return;
      let top=0;document.querySelectorAll('.modal:not(.hide)').forEach(o=>{if(o!==m)top=Math.max(top,zOf(o))});
      const mine=zOf(m);
      m.dataset.c4o=m.style.zIndex||'';m.dataset.c4z='1';
      if(top>=mine)m.style.zIndex=String(top+1)}
    new MutationObserver(list=>{list.forEach(r=>{
      if(r.type==='attributes')check(r.target);
      else r.addedNodes.forEach(n=>{if(n.nodeType===1){check(n);n.querySelectorAll&&n.querySelectorAll('.modal').forEach(check)}})})
    }).observe(document.body,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class'],subtree:true,childList:true});
    document.querySelectorAll('.modal').forEach(check);
  })();
})();

</script>
<script>
/* ===== NEW MOVES: GRAB & THROW, PARRY COUNTER, AIR COMBO =====
   Offline fights only (not online, not story fights, so those keep their exact balance).
   - GRAB   (G key / 🤼 button): beats blocking, loses to dodge / jump. Throws the foe across the ring.
   - COUNTER: parry a hit (block just as it lands), then punch within ~1.4s for a big counter hit.
   - AIR COMBO: hit a foe while they are airborne (after an uppercut or throw) to juggle them. 4th hit slams them down. */
(()=>{
  if(typeof resolve==='undefined'||typeof punch==='undefined'||typeof damage==='undefined')return;
  const $$=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const LS={g:k=>{try{return localStorage.getItem(k)}catch(e){return null}},s:(k,v)=>{try{localStorage.setItem(k,v)}catch(e){}}};
  const EVT=window.EVT=window.EVT||{h:[],on(f){this.h.push(f)},emit(k,v){this.h.forEach(f=>{try{f(k,v)}catch(e){}})}};
  const active=()=>!G.online&&!(G.bot&&G.bot.s2)&&G.mode==='match';
  const clampX=x=>Math.max(-BOUND,Math.min(BOUND,x));

  /* ---------- GRAB ---------- */
  let lastMsg=0;
  const say=m=>{const n=performance.now();if(n-lastMsg<1200)return;lastMsg=n;try{text(m)}catch(e){}};
  window.grab=function(f){
    if(!active()){if(f===P&&G.mode==='match'&&!window.__2P){const n=performance.now();if(n-lastMsg>1500){lastMsg=n;try{toast('🤼 Grab only works in offline fights')}catch(e){}}}return}
    if(!canAct(f))return;
    if((f.gcd||0)>0){if(f===P)say('GRAB READY IN '+Math.ceil(f.gcd)+'s');return}
    f.gcd=3.2;punch(f,'g');
    /* lunge at the foe so a grab from a bit further away still connects */
    const t=foe(f),gap=Math.abs(t.x-f.x)-MIND;
    if(gap>0)f.tx=clampX(f.x+Math.sign(t.x-f.x)*Math.min(1.2,gap));
    if(f===P){try{SFX.dash&&SFX.dash()}catch(e){}}
  };
  /* a real grab: lift the foe over your head, hold, then throw them across the ring and slam them */
  const GBS={CLINCH:.16,LIFT:.3,SPIN:.55,THROW:.4,BOUNCE:.55};
  const GB=window.__GB={cur:null,bounce:null};
  const ease=u=>u*u*(3-2*u);
  const R=.95;
  function grabHit(f,t){
    if(GB.cur)return;
    if(dist()>REACH+.45){text('GRAB MISSED');return}
    if(t.jump||t.dodge||t.air>0||t.fall){text('MISS!');return}
    const gb=!!t.block;
    t.block=false;
    if(t.punch&&t.pk==='s'){G.zoom=false}
    t.punch=0;t.pend=null;t.aura=0;t.em=null;
    const dir=t.x>f.x?1:-1;
    t.stun=9;f.gbBusy=true;f.punch=0;
    GB.bounce=null;
    GB.cur={f,t,dir,k:0,gb,fx:f.x,tx0:t.x,z0:0,lastA:0,sfx:0};
    text(gb?'GUARD BREAK!':'GOT YA!');ring(f.x+dir*.5,1.2,0,0xa78bfa,3.2,.4);burst(f.x+dir*.5,0xa78bfa,18);shake(.25);try{SFX.hit()}catch(e){}
  }
  function gbClear(f,t){
    f.gbBusy=false;f.__lift=0;t.__lift=0;
    try{f.g.position.y=0;t.g.position.y=0;t.root.rotation.x=0;t.root.rotation.z=0;t.root.rotation.y=0;f.root.rotation.y=0;f.root.rotation.x=0;f.root.position.y=0}catch(e){}
  }
  function gbRelease(c){gbClear(c.f,c.t);GB.cur=null;GB.bounce=null}
  function gbLand(c){
    const {f,t,dir,gb}=c;
    const x=Math.max(-BOUND,Math.min(BOUND,t.x));
    t.x=t.tx=x;
    gbClear(f,t);GB.cur=null;
    damage(f,t,34,gb?'GUARD BREAK!':'THROW!');
    if(!G.over){t.stun=1.2;t.hsn=1;t.hy=0;t.hx=-1;GB.bounce={t,k:0,dir,x}}
    burst(x,0xa78bfa,44);impact(x,0xa78bfa,true);ring(x,.05,0,0xa78bfa,9,.55,true);ring(x,.05,0,0xffffff,5,.4,true);shake(.7);stop(.14);try{SFX.boom()}catch(e){}
    for(let i=0;i<7;i++)blob(x+(Math.random()-.5)*1.6,.12,(Math.random()-.5)*.6,0xd8b4fe,.28,.5,1);
    f.sup=Math.min(100,f.sup+24);
    if(f===P){EVT.emit('grab',1)}
  }
  function gbPost(fr,dt){
    /* bounce after the slam */
    const bn=GB.bounce;
    if(bn&&fr===bn.t){
      bn.k+=dt;const u=bn.k/GBS.BOUNCE;
      if(u>=1||G.mode!=='match'){bn.t.__lift=0;bn.t.g.position.y=0;bn.t.root.rotation.x=0;GB.bounce=null}
      else{const y=Math.abs(Math.sin(u*Math.PI*2.2))*.6*(1-u);bn.t.__lift=y;bn.t.g.position.y=y;bn.t.root.rotation.x=bn.dir*1.2*(1-ease(Math.min(1,u*1.4)))*-1+bn.dir*.2*Math.sin(u*9)*(1-u)}
    }
    const c=GB.cur;if(!c)return;
    const {f,t,dir}=c;
    if(G.mode!=='match'||G.over||f.hp<=0||t.hp<=0){gbRelease(c);return}
    const C=GBS.CLINCH,L=GBS.LIFT,S=GBS.SPIN,T=GBS.THROW;
    if(fr===t){
      c.k+=dt;
      const k=c.k;
      const hx=c.fx;                      /* directly over the attacker's head */
      let x,y,z=f.g.position.z,rx,gry=null;
      if(k<C){                            /* clinch: pulled in close, doubled over */
        const u=ease(k/C);x=c.tx0+(c.fx+dir*.75-c.tx0)*u;y=0;rx=dir*.55*u;
      }else if(k<C+L){                    /* lift overhead */
        const u=ease((k-C)/L);x=(c.fx+dir*.75)+(hx-(c.fx+dir*.75))*u;y=2.15*u;rx=dir*(.55+.95*u);
        gry=null;
      }else if(k<C+L+S){                  /* helicopter spin */
        const u=(k-C-L)/S;
        const a=Math.PI/2+dir*(3*Math.PI)*ease(u*.85+.15*u);
        const rad=R*Math.min(1,u*6);
        x=hx+Math.cos(a)*rad;z=f.g.position.z+Math.sin(a)*rad;y=2.15+Math.sin(u*Math.PI)*.12;
        rx=Math.PI/2*dir;gry=-a*dir+(dir<0?Math.PI:0);
        if(c.sfx<Math.floor(u*3)){c.sfx=Math.floor(u*3);try{SFX.dodge&&SFX.dodge()}catch(e){}ring(c.fx,1.9,0,0xc4b5fd,2.4,.3)}
      }else if(k<C+L+S+T){                /* release: fly across the ring */
        const u=(k-C-L-S)/T;
        x=hx+dir*3.5*u;y=2.15*(1-u*u)+1.4*Math.sin(u*Math.PI)*(1-u*.3);z=f.g.position.z-R*(1-u);rx=dir*(1.5+u*5.8);
        if(c.sfx<9){c.sfx=9;shake(.2);try{SFX.punch&&SFX.punch()}catch(e){}ring(c.fx+dir*.4,1.6,0,0xffffff,3,.3)}
        if(Math.random()<.5)blob(t.x,2.15*(1-u*u)+.6,0,0xe9d5ff,.22,.35,1);
      }else{gbLand(c);return}
      t.x=t.tx=x;t.g.position.x=x;t.__lift=y;t.g.position.y=y;
      if(z!==null)t.g.position.z=z;
      t.root.rotation.x=rx;
      if(gry!==null)t.g.rotation.y=gry;
      t.stun=Math.max(t.stun,.5);
    }else if(fr===f){
      const k=c.k;
      f.x=f.tx=c.fx;f.g.position.x=c.fx;
      let hy=1.3,hz=.1,lean=0,crouch=0,spin=0;
      if(k<C){const u=k/C;hy=1.15+.15*u;hz=.2+.65*u;lean=dir*.25*u;crouch=.12*u}
      else if(k<C+L){const u=ease((k-C)/L);hy=1.3+.95*u;hz=.85-.78*u;lean=dir*(.25-.4*u);crouch=.12*(1-u)}
      else if(k<C+L+S){const u=(k-C-L)/S;hy=2.25;hz=.07;lean=-dir*.15*Math.sin(u*Math.PI);spin=dir*(3*Math.PI)*ease(u*.85+.15*u)}
      else{const u=Math.min(1,(k-C-L-S)/T);hy=2.25-1.1*ease(u);hz=.07+.6*u;lean=dir*.45*ease(u);spin=dir*3*Math.PI}
      f.hl.position.set(0,hy,hz);f.hr.position.set(0,hy,-hz*.9);
      f.root.rotation.x=lean;f.root.rotation.y=spin;f.root.position.y=-crouch;
      try{limbs(f)}catch(e){}
    }
  }
  const oAnim=anim;
  anim=function(f,dt,t){const r=oAnim.apply(this,arguments);try{gbPost(f,dt)}catch(e){}return r};
  const oCA=canAct;
  canAct=function(f){return !f.gbBusy&&oCA.apply(this,arguments)};
  const oRF=resetFighters;
  resetFighters=function(){if(GB.cur)gbRelease(GB.cur);GB.bounce=null;return oRF.apply(this,arguments)};

  /* ---------- COUNTER + AIR COMBO (wrap resolve) ---------- */
  const oRes=resolve;
  resolve=function(f,kind){
    if(!active())return oRes.apply(this,arguments);
    const t=foe(f);
    if(kind==='g'){return grabHit(f,t)}
    const norm=!kind||kind==='n';
    const inReach=dist()<=REACH;
    const parried=norm&&inReach&&!(t.jump||t.dodge)&&t.block&&(t.bt===undefined?9:t.bt)<.18;
    const hp0=t.hp,wasAir=(t.air>0),hadCtr=(f.ctr||0)>0;
    const r=oRes.apply(this,arguments);
    if(G.over)return r;
    if(parried){
      t.ctr=1.4;
      text('COUNTER READY!');ring(t.x,1.1,0,0xfbbf24,3.5,.45);
      return r;
    }
    const landed=inReach&&(t.hp<hp0-2)&&!t.block;
    if(!landed||!(norm||kind==='u'))return r;
    const mid=(P.x+O.x)/2;
    if(hadCtr&&norm){
      f.ctr=0;
      damage(f,t,26,'COUNTER!!');burst(mid,0xfbbf24,34);impact(mid,0xfbbf24,true);ring(mid,1.3,0,0xfbbf24,5,.45);shake(.3);stop(.1);
      if(G.over)return r;
      t.stun=Math.max(t.stun||0,.7);
      if(f===P)EVT.emit('counter',1);
    }
    if(wasAir&&norm){
      f.jug=(f.jug||0)+1;f.jugT=1.6;
      const n=f.jug;
      if(n<4){
        damage(f,t,6*n,'AIR COMBO x'+(n+1));
        if(G.over)return r;
        t.air=.001;t.hsn=1;t.hy=0;t.hx=-1.1;
        ring(mid,1.7,0,0x38bdf8,3+n,.4);burst(mid,0x38bdf8,16);
      }else{
        f.jug=0;
        damage(f,t,30,'GROUND SLAM!!');burst(mid,0xf97316,50);impact(mid,0xf97316,true);ring(t.x,.05,0,0xf97316,9,.6,true);shake(.7);stop(.18);SFX.boom();
        if(G.over)return r;
        t.air=0;t.stun=1.1;t.hsn=1;knock(f,t,1.8);
        if(f===P)EVT.emit('slam',1);
      }
      if(f===P)EVT.emit('juggle',n+1);
    }
    return r;
  };

  /* ---------- timers + AI use of grab ---------- */
  function timers(dt){
    [P,O].forEach(f=>{
      f.gcd=Math.max(0,(f.gcd||0)-dt);
      f.ctr=Math.max(0,(f.ctr||0)-dt);
      if((f.jugT||0)>0){f.jugT-=dt;if(f.jugT<=0)f.jug=0}
    });
  }
  window.__mvTimers=timers;
  const oAi=ai;
  ai=function(dt){
    const r=oAi.apply(this,arguments);
    try{
      timers(dt);
      if(!active()||G.over||G.paused||G.intro>0||window.__2P||window.__SPAR)return r;
      if(!canAct(O)||(O.gcd||0)>0||O.block||dist()>MIND+.45)return r;
      const tr=G.bot&&G.bot.tr;
      let rate=tr?(tr.grab||0)*1.1:({easy:0,normal:.22,hard:.45,extreme:.75}[G.diff]||0);
      if(!tr&&G.bot&&G.bot.nogr)rate=0;
      if(!(P.block||P.stun>0))rate*=.2;
      if(rate>0&&Math.random()<rate*dt)window.grab(O);
    }catch(e){}
    return r;
  };

  /* ---------- controls: G key + button ---------- */
  addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
    if(G.mode!=='match'||G.paused||e.repeat||window.__2P)return;
    if(e.code==='KeyG')window.grab(P)});
  const kj=$$('kJ');
  if(kj&&kj.parentElement&&!$$('kG')){
    const b=document.createElement('button');b.className='c';b.id='kG';b.innerHTML='<span>🤼</span>GRAB';
    b.onpointerdown=e=>{e.preventDefault();if(!window.__2P)window.grab(P)};
    kj.parentElement.appendChild(b);
  }
  /* grab button: hidden where grab isn't available, shows the cooldown */
  setInterval(()=>{try{
    const b=$$('kG');if(!b)return;
    const ok=active()&&!window.__2P;
    b.style.display=ok?'':'none';
    if(!ok)return;
    const c=P.gcd||0;
    b.style.opacity=c>0?.45:1;
    const sp=b.lastChild;if(sp&&sp.nodeType===3){sp.nodeValue=c>0?Math.ceil(c)+'s':'GRAB'}
  }catch(e){}},150);

  /* ---------- one-time hint ---------- */
  const oSM=startMatch;
  startMatch=function(){
    const r=oSM.apply(this,arguments);
    try{
      if(!G.online&&!(G.bot&&G.bot.s2)&&!window.__2P&&!LS.g('db_mvhint')){
        LS.s('db_mvhint','1');
        const d=document.createElement('div');
        d.style.cssText='position:fixed;left:8px;right:8px;top:92px;z-index:60;background:rgba(15,23,42,.94);border:2px solid #a78bfa;border-radius:14px;padding:10px 12px;color:#fff;font:600 13px Fredoka,system-ui;text-align:center;pointer-events:none;animation:c4in .5s backwards';
        d.innerHTML='🆕 <b>NEW MOVES</b><br>🤼 <b>GRAB</b> (G) beats blocking · ✨ Parry, then punch = <b>COUNTER</b> · 🌀 Hit a flying foe = <b>AIR COMBO</b>';
        document.body.appendChild(d);setTimeout(()=>{d.style.transition='opacity .6s';d.style.opacity=0;setTimeout(()=>d.remove(),700)},7000);
      }
    }catch(e){}
    return r;
  };
})();

</script>
<script>
/* ===== GAME MODES: Survival · Boss Rush · Time Attack · 2-Player (one device) =====
   Own save key: db_modes. Modes never change your win/loss record, streak or coin total (they pay their own coins). */
(()=>{
  if(typeof startMatch==='undefined'||typeof finish==='undefined')return;
  const $$=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const LS={g:k=>{try{return localStorage.getItem(k)}catch(e){return null}},s:(k,v)=>{try{localStorage.setItem(k,v)}catch(e){}},r:k=>{try{localStorage.removeItem(k)}catch(e){}}};
  const EVT=window.EVT=window.EVT||{h:[],on(f){this.h.push(f)},emit(k,v){this.h.forEach(f=>{try{f(k,v)}catch(e){}})}};
  const BASE_BOTS=BOTS.slice();
  const DIFFS=['easy','normal','hard','extreme'];
  const DEF={surv:{best:0,runs:0},boss:{clears:0,best:0},ta:{best:0,runs:0},p2:{games:0,p1:0,p2:0},p2c:'#fecaca'};
  const load=()=>{try{const o=JSON.parse(LS.g('db_modes')||'{}');return{surv:Object.assign({},DEF.surv,o.surv),boss:Object.assign({},DEF.boss,o.boss),ta:Object.assign({},DEF.ta,o.ta),p2:Object.assign({},DEF.p2,o.p2),p2c:o.p2c||DEF.p2c}}catch(e){return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEF))}};
  let SV=load();const save=()=>LS.s('db_modes',JSON.stringify(SV));
  const fmt=t=>{t=Math.round(t);return Math.floor(t/60)+':'+String(t%60).padStart(2,'0')};
  const wait=ms=>new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,ms));
  const reward=n=>{if(n>0){W.c+=n;try{saveW()}catch(e){}try{showStat()}catch(e){}}};

  /* ============ runner ============ */
  let cur=null;                                   // current mode run
  window.MODES={get cur(){return cur},get active(){return !!cur}};
  const oFin=finish;
  finish=function(pWon){
    if(!cur)return oFin.apply(this,arguments);
    const st=LS.g('db_st'),c0=(cur.c0!=null?cur.c0:W.c),hp=P.hp,sup=P.sup,tleft=G.time;
    try{oFin.apply(this,arguments)}
    finally{
      if(st===null)LS.r('db_st');else LS.s('db_st',st);
      W.c=c0;try{saveW()}catch(e){}try{showStat()}catch(e){}
    }
    if(cur.kind==='2p'){
      SV.p2.games++;pWon?SV.p2.p1++:SV.p2.p2++;save();
      $$('rI').innerText='🤝';$$('rT').innerText=pWon?'PLAYER 1 WINS!':'PLAYER 2 WINS!';$$('rT').style.color=pWon?'#34d399':'#fb7185';
      if(pWon)cur.s1++;else cur.s2++;
      $$('rS').innerText='Match '+G.pw+'–'+G.ow+' · session P1 '+cur.s1+' – '+cur.s2+' P2\n(2-player games don’t change your record or coins)';
      $$('bAgain').innerText='REMATCH';
      return;
    }
    if(cur.res){
      const f=cur.res;cur.res=null;
      const rs=$$('result');rs.classList.add('hide');let n=0;const iv=setInterval(()=>{rs.classList.add('hide');if(++n>45)clearInterval(iv)},40);
      setTimeout(()=>{rs.classList.add('hide');f({won:!!pWon,hp,sup,tleft})},1700);
    }
  };
  const oTL=toLobby;
  toLobby=function(){
    if(cur&&!cur.ending){
      const c=cur;cur=null;
      if(c.c0!=null){W.c=c.c0;try{saveW()}catch(e){}}
      cleanup(c);
      if(c.res){const f=c.res;c.res=null;try{f({quit:true})}catch(e){}}
    }else if(!cur){cleanup(null)}
    return oTL.apply(this,arguments);
  };
  function cleanup(c){
    window.__2P=false;window.__SPAR=false;
    const p=$$('p2pad');if(p)p.style.display='none';
    const b=$$('modeBadge');if(b)b.style.display='none';
    if(c&&c.opt){try{const i=BOTS.indexOf(c.opt.bot);if(i>=0)BOTS.splice(i,1);c.opt.el.remove()}catch(e){}}
    try{document.querySelectorAll('#bot option[data-mode]').forEach(o=>o.remove())}catch(e){}
    const k=window.__modeKeep;window.__modeKeep=null;
    if(k){try{$$('bot').value=k.b;$$('arena').value=k.a;$$('diff').value=k.d;G.need=k.n;G.practice=k.p}catch(e){}}
  }
  const owned=k=>{try{return typeof open==='function'?open('arena',k):true}catch(e){return true}};
  const arenaPick=()=>{const ks=Object.keys(ARENAS).filter(owned);return rnd(ks.length?ks:['cyber'])};

  function mkBot(def){
    const b=Object.assign({},def);b.m=Object.assign({tick:1,atk:1,dmg:1,react:1},def.m||{});
    if(b.shirt===undefined){b.shirt='';b.shirtColor='#ffffff'}
    return b;
  }
  /* run one fight, resolves {won,hp,sup,tleft} or {quit:true} */
  function runFight(def,o){
    o=o||{};
    return new Promise(res=>{
      const sel=$$('bot'),as=$$('arena'),ds=$$('diff');
      const bot=mkBot(def);
      const keep={a:as.value,d:ds.value,n:G.need,b:sel.value,p:G.practice};
      if(!window.__modeKeep)window.__modeKeep=keep;
      const key=o.arena||arenaPick();
      const wasOwned=!!(W.own&&W.own['arena:'+key]);if(!wasOwned)W.own['arena:'+key]=1;
      const opt=document.createElement('option');opt.dataset.mode='1';BOTS.push(bot);opt.value=String(BOTS.length-1);sel.appendChild(opt);sel.value=opt.value;
      as.value=key;ds.value=o.diff||'normal';G.need=o.need||1;G.tour=false;G.practice=false;
      cur.res=res;cur.c0=W.c;
      if(o.keep)cur.opt={el:opt,bot,keep};
      try{startMatch()}
      finally{
        if(!o.keep){const i=BOTS.indexOf(bot);if(i===BOTS.length-1)BOTS.pop();opt.remove();sel.value=keep.b}
        if(!o.keep){as.value=keep.a;ds.value=keep.d}
        if(!wasOwned)delete W.own['arena:'+key];
      }
      try{
        if(o.hp!=null)P.hp=Math.max(5,Math.min(100,o.hp));
        if(o.sup!=null)P.sup=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,o.sup));
        if(o.pn){G.pn=o.pn}if(o.bn){G.bn=o.bn}
        hud();
      }catch(e){}
    });
  }
  /* boss armour: bosses take less damage; keeps the HP bar honest */
  const oDmg=damage;
  damage=function(f,t,d,txt,blocked){
    if(cur&&t===O&&G.bot&&G.bot.armor)d=Math.max(1,Math.round(d*G.bot.armor));
    return oDmg.call(this,f,t,d,txt,blocked);
  };

  document.body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<div id="mres" class="modal hide" style="z-index:330"><div class="box"><div class="big" id="mrI">🏆</div><h2 class="mk" id="mrT" style="color:var(--amber);margin:6px 0">RESULT</h2><p id="mrS"></p><button class="btn go" id="mrAgain">PLAY AGAIN</button><button class="btn" id="mrMenu">MODES MENU</button><button class="btn" id="mrLobby">LOBBY</button></div></div>');
  let lastMode=null;
  function summary(icon,title,color,text,again){
    $$('mrI').textContent=icon;$$('mrT').textContent=title;$$('mrT').style.color=color||'#fbbf24';$$('mrS').textContent=text;

  async function endRun(c,fn){c.ending=true;cleanup(c);cur=null;try{toLobby()}catch(e){}await wait(150);fn()}

  /* ============ SURVIVAL ============ */
  async function survival(){
    if(cur)return;
    const c=cur={kind:'surv'};
    const base=Math.max(0,DIFFS.indexOf($$('diff').value));
    let wave=0,hp=100,sup=0,coins=0;
    while(cur===c){
      wave++;
      const pool=BASE_BOTS.filter(b=>!b.s2);const b0=rnd(pool);
      const sc=Math.min(wave,24);
      const def=Object.assign({},b0,{m:{tick:Math.max(.45,(b0.m.tick)*(1-.025*sc)),atk:b0.m.atk*(1+.03*sc),dmg:b0.m.dmg*(1+.025*sc),react:b0.m.react*(1+.02*sc)},hs:b0.hs,bs:b0.bs});
      const di=Math.min(3,base+Math.floor((wave-1)/3));
      setBadge('🌊 WAVE '+wave+'  ·  best '+SV.surv.best);
      const r=await runFight(def,{diff:DIFFS[di],hp,sup});
      if(cur!==c)break;
      if(!r||r.quit){wave--;await finishSurv(c,wave,coins,true);return}
      if(!r.won){wave--;await finishSurv(c,wave,coins,false);return}
      coins+=8+2*wave;hp=Math.min(100,r.hp+30);sup=r.sup;
      setBadge('🌊 WAVE '+wave+' CLEARED!  +🪙'+(8+2*wave));
      EVT.emit('wave',wave);
    }
  }
  async function finishSurv(c,wave,coins,quit){
    const newBest=wave>SV.surv.best;if(newBest)SV.surv.best=wave;SV.surv.runs++;save();reward(coins);
    EVT.emit('survival',wave);
    if(quit){c.ending=true;cleanup(c);cur=null;try{toast('Run ended: wave '+wave+' · +🪙'+coins)}catch(e){}return}
    await endRun(c,()=>summary(wave>=10?'🏆':'🌊','SURVIVAL OVER',newBest?'#4ade80':'#fbbf24','You survived '+wave+' wave'+(wave===1?'':'s')+(newBest?'\n🎉 NEW BEST!':'\nBest: wave '+SV.surv.best)+'\n🪙 +'+coins+' coins',survival));
  }

  /* ============ BOSS RUSH ============ */
  const BOSSES=[

  async function bossRush(){
    if(cur)return;
    const c=cur={kind:'boss'};
    let hp=100,sup=0,beaten=0,coins=0;
    for(let i=0;i<BOSSES.length;i++){
      const bs=BOSSES[i];
      setBadge('👹 BOSS '+(i+1)+'/'+BOSSES.length+'  ·  '+bs.n);
      const r=await runFight(Object.assign({},bs),{diff:bs.d,hp,sup});
      if(cur!==c)return;
      if(!r||r.quit){c.ending=true;cleanup(c);cur=null;reward(coins);try{toast('Boss Rush ended: '+beaten+'/5 · +🪙'+coins)}catch(e){}return}
      if(!r.won){await finishBoss(c,beaten,coins,false);return}
      beaten++;coins+=60;hp=Math.min(100,r.hp+35);sup=r.sup;
      EVT.emit('boss',beaten);
    }
    coins+=400;
    await finishBoss(c,beaten,coins,true);
  }
  async function finishBoss(c,beaten,coins,clear){
    if(clear)SV.boss.clears++;SV.boss.best=Math.max(SV.boss.best,beaten);save();reward(coins);
    EVT.emit('bossrush',{beaten,clear});
    await endRun(c,()=>summary(clear?'👑':'👹',clear?'BOSS RUSH CLEARED!':'BOSS RUSH OVER',clear?'#4ade80':'#f43f5e',(clear?'You beat all 5 bosses!':'You beat '+beaten+' of 5 bosses.')+'\nClears: '+SV.boss.clears+' · Best: '+SV.boss.best+'/5\n🪙 +'+coins+' coins',bossRush));
  }

  /* ============ TIME ATTACK ============ */
  async function timeAttack(){
    if(cur)return;
    const c=cur={kind:'ta'};
    const pool=BASE_BOTS.filter(b=>!b.s2).slice().sort(()=>Math.random()-.5).slice(0,5);
    let total=0;
    for(let i=0;i<pool.length;i++){
      setBadge('⏱ '+fmt(total)+'  ·  FIGHT '+(i+1)+'/5  ·  best '+(SV.ta.best?fmt(SV.ta.best):'--'));
      const r=await runFight(Object.assign({},pool[i]),{diff:'normal'});
      if(cur!==c)return;
      if(!r||r.quit){c.ending=true;cleanup(c);cur=null;return}
      total+=Math.max(3,98-r.tleft);
      if(!r.won){await endRun(c,()=>summary('⏱','TIME ATTACK FAILED','#f43f5e','You lost fight '+(i+1)+' of 5.\nTry again — win all 5 as fast as you can.',timeAttack));return}
    }
    const nb=!SV.ta.best||total<SV.ta.best;if(nb)SV.ta.best=total;SV.ta.runs++;save();
    const coins=total<100?300:total<150?200:total<210?120:60;reward(coins);
    EVT.emit('timeattack',total);
    await endRun(c,()=>summary('⏱','TIME: '+fmt(total),nb?'#4ade80':'#fbbf24','All 5 bots beaten!'+(nb?'\n🎉 NEW BEST TIME!':'\nBest: '+fmt(SV.ta.best))+'\nGold under 1:40 · 🪙 +'+coins+' coins',timeAttack));
  }

  /* ============ 2-PLAYER (one device) ============ */
  const K2={l:0,r:0,b:0};
  async function twoPlayer(){
    if(cur)return;
    const c=cur={kind:'2p',s1:0,s2:0};
    window.__2P=true;
    const def={n:'PLAYER 2',s:'',c:SV.p2c,e:'smiley',h:'none',hs:1,bs:1,shirt:'tee',shirtColor:'#fb7185',nogr:true,m:{tick:1,atk:1,dmg:1/(DIFF.normal.dmg||1),react:1}};
    const pad=$$('p2pad');if(pad)pad.style.display='flex';

  }
  function p2Tick(dt){
    try{window.__mvTimers&&window.__mvTimers(dt)}catch(e){}
    if(G.mode!=='match'||G.over||G.paused)return;
    G.combo=0;
    const d2=(G.intro>0||O.stun>0)?0:K2.r-K2.l;
    if(d2&&!O.punch&&!O.dodge){let nx=O.tx+d2*5*dt;const s2=O.x<P.x?-1:1;if(Math.abs(nx-P.x)<MIND)nx=P.x+s2*MIND;O.tx=Math.max(-BOUND,Math.min(BOUND,nx))}
    O.block=!!K2.b&&O.sta>0&&!O.punch&&!(O.stun>0);O.bt=O.block?(O.bt||0)+dt:0;
    if(K2.b&&O.sta>0)O.sta=Math.max(0,O.sta-dt*35);else if(!K2.b)O.sta=Math.min(100,O.sta+dt*25);
  }
  const oAi=ai;
  ai=function(dt){
    if(window.__2P&&cur&&cur.kind==='2p'){p2Tick(dt);return}
    if(window.__SPAR){try{window.__mvTimers&&window.__mvTimers(dt);if(window.__sparTick)window.__sparTick(dt)}catch(e){}return}
    return oAi.apply(this,arguments);
  };
  const oDmg2=damage;
  damage=function(f,t,d,txt,blocked){
    const r=oDmg2.call(this,f,t,d,txt,blocked);
    if(window.__2P&&f===P&&!blocked)O.sup=Math.min(100,O.sup+10);
    return r;
  };

    grab:()=>window.grab&&window.grab(O),sup:()=>{if(O.sup>=100)punch(O,'s')}
  };

  document.body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<div id="modesm" class="modal hide" style="z-index:320"><div class="box"><h2 class="mk" style="color:#fbbf24;margin:0 0 4px">🎮 GAME MODES</h2><div style="text-align:center;font-size:12px;color:#94a3b8">These pay their own coins and never touch your win record or streak.</div><div id="mmList"></div><button class="btn" id="mmClose" style="margin-top:12px">CLOSE</button></div></div>');
  const P2C=['#fecaca','#bfdbfe','#bbf7d0','#fde68a','#e9d5ff','#fbcfe8'];
  function openMenu(){
    SV=load();
    const L=$$('mmList');
    const names=twNames(),m=M0();
    const twHtml='<div class="tw '+(names.length?'warn':'ok')+'"><h4>'+(names.length?'⚠ TWISTS ON: '+names.join(', '):'✅ NO TWISTS ON')+'</h4><div class="st">'+(names.length?'While a twist is on, wins, losses, streak, coins, achievements and the leaderboard do not count.':'Your matches count toward your record, coins and achievements.')+'</div>'+(names.length?'<button class="btn" id="twOff">TURN ALL OFF</button>':'')+'</div>'+
      '<div class="sec">Twists &amp; practice</div><div class="tw">'+TW.map(t=>'<div class="tr"><div class="tx"><b>'+t[1]+' '+t[2]+'</b><small>'+t[3]+'</small></div><button class="sw3'+(m[t[0]]?' on':'')+'" data-tw="'+t[0]+'" aria-label="'+t[2]+'"></button></div>').join('')+'<div class="tr"><div class="tx"><b>🥊 Practice</b><small>No match end, health returns. Great for trying moves.</small></div><button class="sw3'+(G.practice?' on':'')+'" data-tw="practice" aria-label="Practice"></button></div></div><div class="sec">Play a mode</div>';
    const bf=(window.TRN&&TRN.best)?TRN.best():'';
    L.innerHTML=twHtml+[
      ['🌊','SURVIVAL','Endless waves. Your health carries over (+30 between waves) and bots get tougher every wave.','Best: wave '+SV.surv.best+' · runs '+SV.surv.runs,'surv'],
      ['👹','BOSS RUSH','Five giant bosses back to back with armor and nasty moves. Health carries over (+35).','Clears: '+SV.boss.clears+' · best '+SV.boss.best+'/5','boss'],
      ['⏱','TIME ATTACK','Beat 5 bots as fast as you can. One loss and the run is over.','Best time: '+(SV.ta.best?fmt(SV.ta.best):'--'),'ta'],
      ['🎮','2-PLAYER','Two people, one device. P1: bottom buttons or A/D/W/J/K/L/E/G. P2: top buttons or ←/→/↑ and / . , ; \' [','P1 '+SV.p2.p1+' – '+SV.p2.p2+' P2 (all time)','p2'],
      ['🤖','BOT TRAINER','Build your own bot, or let it learn how YOU fight and then fight it.',bf,'trn']
    ].map(([ic,t,d,r,id])=>'<div class="mc"><div class="ic">'+ic+'</div><div class="tx"><b>'+t+'</b><small>'+d+'</small><div class="rec">'+r+'</div>'+(id==='p2'?'<div class="sws" id="p2sw">'+P2C.map(c=>'<i data-c="'+c+'" style="background:'+c+'" class="'+(c===SV.p2c?'on':'')+'"></i>').join('')+'</div>':'')+'</div><button class="btn go" data-m="'+id+'">'+(id==='trn'?'OPEN':'PLAY')+'</button></div>').join('');
    L.querySelectorAll('button[data-m]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
      const m=b.dataset.m;$$('modesm').classList.add('hide');
      if(m==='trn'){window.TRN&&TRN.open&&TRN.open();return}
      ({surv:survival,boss:bossRush,ta:timeAttack,p2:twoPlayer})[m]();
    });
    L.querySelectorAll('[data-tw]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
      const k=b.dataset.tw;
      if(k==='practice'){const pb=$$('bPrac');if(pb)pb.click();else G.practice=!G.practice}
      else twSet(k,!M0()[k]);
      refreshPill();openMenu();
    });
    const off=$$('twOff');if(off)off.onclick=()=>{twAllOff();refreshPill();openMenu();try{toast('All twists off ✅ your matches count again')}catch(e){}};
    const sw=$$('p2sw');if(sw)sw.querySelectorAll('i').forEach(i=>i.onclick=()=>{SV.p2c=i.dataset.c;save();sw.querySelectorAll('i').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('on',x===i))});
    $$('modesm').classList.remove('hide');
  }
  window.MODES.summary=summary;window.MODES.begin=k=>{if(cur)return null;cur={kind:k};return cur};window.MODES.endRun=endRun;window.MODES.setBadge=setBadge;window.MODES.open=openMenu;window.MODES.runFight=runFight;window.MODES.start={surv:survival,boss:bossRush,ta:timeAttack,p2:twoPlayer};
  $$('mmClose').onclick=()=>$$('modesm').classList.add('hide');
  /* lobby tile */
  function addTile(){
    const tiles=$$('lobTiles');if(!tiles||$$('bModes'))return;
    const b=document.createElement('button');b.id='bModes';b.className='btn';b.style.cssText='flex:1 1 0%;min-width:150px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#0ea5e9,#6366f1)';
    b.innerHTML='<span class="ti">🎮</span><span class="tn">MODES</span><span class="ts">Survival · Boss Rush · 2P</span>';
    b.onclick=openMenu;tiles.insertBefore(b,tiles.children[1]||null);
  }
  addTile();
  /* one place for modes: the Settings > Fun Modes button is replaced by MODES */
  try{const fb=$$('bFun');if(fb)fb.remove()}catch(e){}
  const hs=document.createElement('style');hs.textContent='#bFun{display:none!important}';document.head.appendChild(hs);
  setTimeout(()=>{refreshPill();const n=twNames().filter(x=>x!=='Practice');if(n.length){try{toast('🎲 '+n.join(', ')+' still ON from before. Open MODES to turn it off.')}catch(e){}}},1800);
})();

<script>
/* ===== BOT TRAINER: build your own bot (3 slots), or let it LEARN how you fight by sparring.
   Save key: db_trainer. Trained bots show up in the lobby "Opponent" list. ===== */
(()=>{
  if(typeof startMatch==='undefined'||!window.MODES)return;
  const $$=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const LS={g:k=>{try{return localStorage.getItem(k)}catch(e){return null}},s:(k,v)=>{try{localStorage.setItem(k,v)}catch(e){}}};
  const EVT=window.EVT=window.EVT||{h:[],on(f){this.h.push(f)},emit(k,v){this.h.forEach(f=>{try{f(k,v)}catch(e){}})}};
  const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));
  const SPECIALS=[['','None'],['combo','Double hits'],['slam','Ground slam super'],['ink','Ink blast super'],['tele','Teleport behind you'],['royal','Lightning on hits'],['ghost','Ghost jumps'],['robo','Robot dashes']];
  const EXPR=[['smiley','😀 Smile'],['angry','😠 Angry'],['cool','😎 Cool'],['shocked','😲 Shocked'],['happy','😄 Happy']];
  const HATS=[['none','No hat'],['horns','Horns'],['tophat','Top hat'],['bunny','Bunny ears'],['crown','Crown'],['halo','Halo'],['antenna','Antenna']];
  const COLS=['#bbf7d0','#fecaca','#bfdbfe','#fde68a','#e9d5ff','#fbcfe8','#e2e8f0','#475569'];
  const SL=[
    ['aggr','⚔ Aggression','How often it attacks'],
    ['speed','⚡ Speed','How fast it thinks and moves'],
    ['react','👀 Reflexes','How fast it reacts to your punches'],
    ['power','💥 Power','How hard its hits are'],
    ['block','🛡 Extra blocking','Blocks your punches more'],
    ['dodge','🏃 Dodging','Dodges away from your punches'],
    ['jump','🦘 Jumping','Hops over attacks and around'],
    ['dash','💨 Dashing','Dashes in to close the gap'],
    ['grab','🤼 Grabbing','Grabs you when you block']
  ];
  const newBot=i=>({made:false,name:'MY BOT '+(i+1),c:COLS[i%COLS.length],e:'cool',h:'none',hs:1,bs:1,sp:'',learned:false,s:{aggr:50,speed:50,react:50,power:50,block:30,dodge:30,jump:20,dash:20,grab:30}});
  let TD;
  function load(){
    try{const o=JSON.parse(LS.g('db_trainer')||'null');if(o&&o.bots&&o.bots.length===3){o.bots.forEach((b,i)=>{b.s=Object.assign(newBot(i).s,b.s||{})});return o}}catch(e){}
    return{bots:[0,1,2].map(newBot),sel:0};
  }
  TD=load();
  const save=()=>LS.s('db_trainer',JSON.stringify(TD));

  function toBot(b){
    const s=b.s;
    return{n:String(b.name||'MY BOT').toUpperCase().slice(0,12),s:b.sp||'',c:b.c,e:b.e,h:b.h,hs:b.hs,bs:b.bs,shirt:'tee',shirtColor:b.c,
      m:{tick:+(1.6-s.speed*.012).toFixed(3),atk:+(.5+s.aggr/100).toFixed(3),dmg:+(.7+s.power*.007).toFixed(3),react:+(.5+s.react/100).toFixed(3)},
      tr:{block:s.block/100,dodge:s.dodge/100,jump:s.jump/100,dash:s.dash/100,grab:s.grab/100},trn:true};
  }

  /* ---------- lobby opponent list ---------- */
  function syncOptions(){
    const sel=$$('bot');if(!sel)return;
    sel.querySelectorAll('option[data-trn]').forEach(o=>o.remove());
    TD.bots.forEach((b,i)=>{if(!b.made)return;const o=document.createElement('option');o.value='trn'+i;o.dataset.trn='1';o.textContent='🤖 '+b.name+' (trained)';sel.appendChild(o)});
  }
  syncOptions();
  setInterval(()=>{const sel=$$('bot');if(sel&&TD.bots.some((b,i)=>b.made&&!sel.querySelector('option[value="trn'+i+'"]')))syncOptions()},2000);
  const oSM=startMatch;
  startMatch=function(){
    const sel=$$('bot');
    if(sel&&/^trn[0-2]$/.test(sel.value)){
      const i=+sel.value.slice(3),keepV=sel.value,b=toBot(TD.bots[i]);
      BOTS.push(b);const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=String(BOTS.length-1);o.dataset.mode='1';sel.appendChild(o);sel.value=o.value;
      try{return oSM.apply(this,arguments)}
      finally{const k=BOTS.indexOf(b);if(k===BOTS.length-1)BOTS.pop();o.remove();sel.value=keepV}
    }
    return oSM.apply(this,arguments);
  };

  /* ---------- AI extras for trained bots ---------- */
  const oAi=ai;
  ai=function(dt){
    const r=oAi.apply(this,arguments);
    try{
      const b=G.bot,tr=b&&b.tr;
      if(!tr||G.mode!=='match'||G.over||G.paused||G.intro>0||G.online||window.__2P||window.__SPAR||(O.stun>0))return r;
      const near=dist()<REACH+.3,thr=P.punch&&near&&!O.dodge;
      if(thr&&!O.block&&O.sta>15&&Math.random()<tr.block*dt*7)O.block=true;
      if(thr&&canAct(O)&&Math.random()<tr.dodge*dt*5)dodge(O);
      if(thr&&canAct(O)&&!O.jump&&Math.random()<tr.jump*dt*3)jump(O);
      if(canAct(O)&&!O.jump&&Math.random()<tr.jump*dt*.25)jump(O);
      if(dist()>2.2&&canAct(O)&&Math.random()<tr.dash*dt*1.3)dash(O);
    }catch(e){}
    return r;
  };

  /* ---------- sparring: the bot watches how you fight ---------- */
  let cnt=null,sparC=null,sparT=0,punchT=2;
  ['punch','dodge','jump','dash'].forEach(n=>{
    const o=window[n];if(typeof o!=='function')return;
    window[n]=function(f,...a){if(cnt&&window.__SPAR&&f===P&&canAct(f)&&!(a[0]==='s')){cnt[n]++}return o.call(this,f,...a)};
  });
  if(typeof window.grab==='function'){const og=window.grab;window.grab=function(f){if(cnt&&window.__SPAR&&f===P&&canAct(f)&&(f.gcd||0)<=0)cnt.grab++;return og.apply(this,arguments)}}
  window.__sparTick=function(dt){
    if(!cnt||G.mode!=='match'||G.paused||G.intro>0)return;
    cnt.t+=dt;if(G.keys.b)cnt.block+=dt;
    P.hp=100;O.hp=100;
    const left=Math.max(0,45-cnt.t);
    MODES.setBadge('🧠 SPARRING  ·  '+Math.ceil(left)+'s  ·  fight normally, the bot is watching');
    // dummy: walks up and throws slow punches so you have something to block / dodge
    if(!(O.stun>0)&&!O.punch&&!O.dodge){
      const dd=dist();
      if(dd>MIND+.4){O.tx=Math.max(-BOUND,Math.min(BOUND,O.x+Math.sign(P.x-O.x)*Math.min(dt*2,dd-MIND)))}
      punchT-=dt;if(punchT<=0&&dd<REACH){punchT=1.6+Math.random()*1.4;punch(O)}
    }
    if(cnt.t>=45&&!cnt.done){cnt.done=true;endSpar()}
  };
  function startSpar(){
    const c=MODES.begin('spar');if(!c){try{toast('Finish or quit the current mode first')}catch(e){}return}
    sparC=c;cnt={punch:0,dodge:0,jump:0,dash:0,grab:0,block:0,t:0,done:false};punchT=2;
    window.__SPAR=true;
    $$('trnm').classList.add('hide');
    MODES.setBadge('🧠 SPARRING');
    MODES.runFight({n:'SPAR DUMMY',s:'',c:'#e2e8f0',e:'smiley',h:'none',hs:1,bs:1,m:{tick:1,atk:1,dmg:.2,react:.2},nogr:true},{diff:'easy',need:2,keep:true,pn:'YOU',bn:'SPARRING'});
  }
  function endSpar(){
    const c=sparC,k=cnt;sparC=null;cnt=null;if(!c)return;
    const t=Math.max(10,k.t),pr=k.punch/t,br=k.block/t,dr=k.dodge/t,jr=k.jump/t,dsr=k.dash/t,gr=k.grab/t;
    const b=TD.bots[TD.sel],s=b.s;
    s.aggr=Math.round(clamp(pr/1.8*100,10,100));
    s.speed=Math.round(clamp((pr+dr+jr+dsr+gr)/2.6*100,10,100));
    s.block=Math.round(clamp(br/.35*100,0,100));
    s.dodge=Math.round(clamp(dr/.22*100,0,100));
    s.jump=Math.round(clamp(jr/.3*100,0,100));
    s.dash=Math.round(clamp(dsr/.3*100,0,100));
    s.grab=Math.round(clamp(gr/.12*100,0,100));
    s.react=Math.round(clamp(40+(br/.35+dr/.22)*30,30,100));
    s.power=Math.round(clamp(40+pr/1.8*40,40,90));
    b.learned=true;b.made=true;save();syncOptions();
    EVT.emit('trained',1);
    const msg='Learned from 45s: '+pr.toFixed(1)+' punches/s · block '+Math.round(br*100)+'% of the time · '+(k.dodge)+' dodges · '+k.jump+' jumps · '+k.dash+' dashes · '+k.grab+' grabs';
    MODES.endRun(c,()=>{openUI();$$('trnNote').textContent='🧠 '+msg})
  }

  /* ---------- UI ---------- */
  const css=document.createElement('style');
  css.textContent=`
  #trnm .box{max-width:440px;max-height:92vh;overflow:auto;text-align:left}
  #trnm h2{text-align:center;margin:0 0 6px}
  #trnm .tabs{display:flex;gap:6px;margin:6px 0 8px}
  #trnm .tabs button{flex:1;margin:0;padding:8px 4px;font-size:13px}
  #trnm .tabs button.on{background:#7c3aed;border-color:#c4b5fd}
  #trnm h4{margin:12px 0 4px;font-size:12px;letter-spacing:.08em;color:#94a3b8;text-transform:uppercase}
  #trnm .sl{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr auto;gap:0 8px;align-items:center;margin-top:7px}
  #trnm .sl b{font-size:13px}#trnm .sl small{grid-column:1/-1;font-size:11px;color:#94a3b8;margin-top:-2px}
  #trnm .sl span{font-weight:700;color:#fde68a;font-size:13px}
  #trnm input[type=range]{width:100%;grid-column:1/-1;accent-color:#a78bfa;height:26px}
  #trnm input[type=text],#trnm select{width:100%;margin-top:4px}
  #trnm input[type=text]{padding:9px 11px;border-radius:10px;border:1.5px solid #334155;background:#1e293b;color:#fff;font:600 16px Fredoka,system-ui;outline:none}#trnm input[type=text]:focus{border-color:#a78bfa}
  #trnm .sw2{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:7px;margin-top:6px}#trnm .sw2 i{width:30px;height:30px;border-radius:50%;border:3px solid #475569;display:inline-block}#trnm .sw2 i.on{border-color:#fbbf24;box-shadow:0 0 0 3px #fbbf2455}
  #trnNote{font-size:12px;color:#c4b5fd;margin:6px 0;min-height:16px;text-align:center}
  #trnm .acts{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:8px;margin-top:10px}#trnm .acts .btn{margin:0;font-size:14px;padding:11px 6px}
  `;
  document.head.appendChild(css);
  document.body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<div id="trnm" class="modal hide" style="z-index:325"><div class="box"><h2 class="mk" style="color:#a78bfa">🤖 BOT TRAINER</h2><div class="tabs" id="trnTabs"></div><div id="trnNote"></div><div id="trnBody"></div><div class="acts"><button class="btn" id="trnLearn" style="background:#7c3aed">🧠 LEARN FROM ME<br><small style="font-weight:500;font-size:11px">45s sparring</small></button><button class="btn go" id="trnFight">⚔ FIGHT IT</button></div><button class="btn" id="trnClose" style="margin-top:10px">CLOSE</button></div></div>');
  function render(){
    const T=$$('trnTabs');
    T.innerHTML=TD.bots.map((b,i)=>'<button class="btn'+(i===TD.sel?' on':'')+'" data-i="'+i+'">'+(b.made?'🤖':'➕')+' '+(b.name||'BOT').slice(0,9)+'</button>').join('');
    T.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(x=>x.onclick=()=>{TD.sel=+x.dataset.i;save();render();$$('trnNote').textContent=''});
    const b=TD.bots[TD.sel];
    let h='<h4>Name</h4><input type="text" id="trnName" maxlength="12" value="'+String(b.name||'').replace(/"/g,'&quot;')+'">';
    h+='<h4>Fighting style</h4>';
    SL.forEach(([k,l,d])=>{h+='<div class="sl"><b>'+l+'</b><span id="trv_'+k+'">'+b.s[k]+'%</span><small>'+d+'</small><input type="range" min="0" max="100" step="1" data-k="'+k+'" value="'+b.s[k]+'"></div>'});
    h+='<h4>Special move</h4><select id="trnSp">'+SPECIALS.map(([v,l])=>'<option value="'+v+'"'+(b.sp===v?' selected':'')+'>'+l+'</option>').join('')+'</select>';
    h+='<h4>Look</h4><div class="sw2" id="trnCol">'+COLS.map(c=>'<i data-c="'+c+'" style="background:'+c+'" class="'+(b.c===c?'on':'')+'"></i>').join('')+'</div>';
    h+='<select id="trnEx">'+EXPR.map(([v,l])=>'<option value="'+v+'"'+(b.e===v?' selected':'')+'>'+l+'</option>').join('')+'</select><select id="trnHat">'+HATS.map(([v,l])=>'<option value="'+v+'"'+(b.h===v?' selected':'')+'>'+l+'</option>').join('')+'</select>';
    h+='<div class="sl"><b>Head size</b><span id="trv_hs">'+Math.round(b.hs*100)+'%</span><input type="range" min="0.8" max="1.3" step="0.05" id="trnHs" value="'+b.hs+'"></div><div class="sl"><b>Body size</b><span id="trv_bs">'+Math.round(b.bs*100)+'%</span><input type="range" min="0.8" max="1.4" step="0.05" id="trnBs" value="'+b.bs+'"></div>';
    $$('trnBody').innerHTML=h;
    const touch=()=>{b.made=true;save();syncOptions();render0()};
    function render0(){T.querySelectorAll('button')[TD.sel].textContent='🤖 '+(b.name||'BOT').slice(0,9)}
    $$('trnName').oninput=e=>{b.name=e.target.value.toUpperCase().slice(0,12)||'MY BOT';touch()};
    $$('trnBody').querySelectorAll('input[data-k]').forEach(i=>i.oninput=()=>{b.s[i.dataset.k]=+i.value;$$('trv_'+i.dataset.k).textContent=i.value+'%';b.learned=false;touch()});
    $$('trnSp').onchange=e=>{b.sp=e.target.value;touch()};
    $$('trnEx').onchange=e=>{b.e=e.target.value;touch()};
    $$('trnHat').onchange=e=>{b.h=e.target.value;touch()};
    $$('trnHs').oninput=e=>{b.hs=+e.target.value;$$('trv_hs').textContent=Math.round(b.hs*100)+'%';touch()};
    $$('trnBs').oninput=e=>{b.bs=+e.target.value;$$('trv_bs').textContent=Math.round(b.bs*100)+'%';touch()};
    $$('trnCol').querySelectorAll('i').forEach(i=>i.onclick=()=>{b.c=i.dataset.c;$$('trnCol').querySelectorAll('i').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('on',x===i));touch()});
  }
  function openUI(){TD=load();render();$$('trnm').classList.remove('hide')}
  $$('trnClose').onclick=()=>$$('trnm').classList.add('hide');
  $$('trnLearn').onclick=startSpar;
  $$('trnFight').onclick=()=>{
    const b=TD.bots[TD.sel];b.made=true;save();syncOptions();
    const c=MODES.begin('trn');if(!c){try{toast('Finish or quit the current mode first')}catch(e){}return}
    $$('trnm').classList.add('hide');
    MODES.setBadge('🤖 '+b.name+' · fight!');
    const diff=$$('diff').value||'normal';
    MODES.runFight(toBot(b),{diff,need:2}).then(r=>{
      if(!r||r.quit)return;
      MODES.endRun(c,()=>{
        const w=r.won;if(w)EVT.emit('trainedwin',1);
        MODES.summary(w?'🏆':'💥',w?'YOU BEAT '+b.name+'!':b.name+' WINS!',w?'#4ade80':'#f43f5e',(w?'Nice! Turn the sliders up to make it tougher.':'Your bot got you. Tune it down, or train it again.')+'\n(Trainer fights don\u2019t change your record or coins.)',openUI);
      });
    });
  };
  window.TRN={get cnt(){return cnt},open:openUI,best:()=>{const m=TD.bots.filter(b=>b.made);return m.length?'Your bots: '+m.map(b=>b.name).join(', '):'No bots yet'},get data(){return TD}};
})();

</script>
<script>
/* ===== PROFILE: earnable titles + badges, buyable nameplates + card banners =====
   Save key: db_prof (new). Owning nameplates/banners adds W.own['plate:x'] / ['banner:x'] (additive, nothing removed). */
(()=>{
  if(typeof W==='undefined'||typeof getStat==='undefined')return;
  const $$=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const LS={g:k=>{try{return localStorage.getItem(k)}catch(e){return null}},s:(k,v)=>{try{localStorage.setItem(k,v)}catch(e){}}};
  const EVT=window.EVT=window.EVT||{h:[],on(f){this.h.push(f)},emit(k,v){this.h.forEach(f=>{try{f(k,v)}catch(e){}})}};
  const J=k=>{try{return JSON.parse(LS.g(k)||'null')||{}}catch(e){return{}}};
  const DEFP={title:'',plate:'',banner:'',feat:[],earned:[],cnt:{grab:0,counter:0,slam:0,juggle:0,parry:0,hits:0,combo:0,wave:0,boss:0,ta:0,trained:0,twin:0,matches:0}};
  let PR=(()=>{const o=J('db_prof');return Object.assign({},DEFP,o,{cnt:Object.assign({},DEFP.cnt,o.cnt||{}),feat:o.feat||[],earned:o.earned||[]})})();
  const save=()=>LS.s('db_prof',JSON.stringify(PR));

  /* ---------- what you've done ---------- */
  function facts(){
    const st=getStat(),ach=Object.keys(J('db_ach')).length,md=J('db_modes'),tr=J('db_trainer');
    const own=Object.keys(W.own||{}).length;
    return{
      w:st.w||0,streak:st.b||0,ach,own,coins:W.c||0,
      s1:!!J('db_story').done,s2:!!J('db_story2').done,s3:!!J('db_story3').done,
      wave:Math.max((md.surv&&md.surv.best)||0,PR.cnt.wave),
      boss:Math.max((md.boss&&md.boss.clears)||0,0),
      ta:(md.ta&&md.ta.best)||0,
      trained:(tr.bots||[]).some(b=>b&&b.made)||PR.cnt.trained>0,
      c:PR.cnt
    };
  }

    body.querySelectorAll('.it').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>pick(b.dataset.id));

  function pick(id){
    const got=unlockedSet();
    if(tab==='titles'){
      if(!got.has(id)){const a=ACH.find(x=>x[0]===id);$$('pnote').textContent='🔒 '+a[2]+': '+a[3];return}
      PR.title=PR.title===id&&id!=='rookie'?'':id;save();applyCard();render();
    }else if(tab==='badges'){
      const a=ACH.find(x=>x[0]===id);
      if(!got.has(id)){$$('pnote').textContent='🔒 '+a[2]+': '+a[3];return}
      const i=PR.feat.indexOf(id);
      if(i>=0)PR.feat.splice(i,1);else{if(PR.feat.length>=3)PR.feat.shift();PR.feat.push(id)}
      save();applyCard();render();
    }else{
      const isP=tab==='plates',L=isP?PLATES:BANNERS,key=isP?'plate':'banner',it=L.find(x=>x[0]===id);
      const own=!it[2]||W.own[key+':'+id];
      if(own){PR[key]=id;pend=null;save();applyCard();render();return}
      const pid=key+':'+id;
      if(pend!==pid){pend=pid;render();$$('pnote').textContent='Tap again to buy for 🪙'+it[2];return}
      if(W.c<it[2]){$$('pnote').textContent='Need 🪙'+(it[2]-W.c)+' more';pend=null;render();return}
      W.c-=it[2];W.own[pid]=1;try{saveW()}catch(e){}pend=null;PR[key]=id;save();applyCard();try{SFX.buy&&SFX.buy()}catch(e){}
      try{showStat()}catch(e){}render();$$('pnote').textContent='Unlocked! 🎉';
    }
  }
  function open_(){render();$$('profm').classList.remove('hide')}
  $$('pclose').onclick=()=>$$('profm').classList.add('hide');
  window.PROF={open:open_,check};
  /* lobby tile */
  function addTile(){

  }
  addTile();
  applyCard();
  setTimeout(check,1500);
  setInterval(()=>{const l=$$('lobby');if(l&&!l.classList.contains('hide')){applyCard();check()}},4000);
  new MutationObserver(()=>{const l=$$('lobby');if(l&&!l.classList.contains('hide')){setTimeout(()=>{applyCard();check()},300)}}).observe($$('lobby'),{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
})();

<script>
/* ===== LIVE FACES IN THE LOBBY + EVERY FIGHT =====
   Same idea as the Story faces: the 3D head blinks, and the expression follows what is happening.
   - Lobby / customizer: blinks, makes a random mood every few seconds, and acts out emotes.
   - Fights (any mode, online too): angry when punching, evil on a super, ouch / dizzy when hit,
     smug when blocking or landing a hit, worry on low health, dead on KO, laugh on a win, cry on a loss.
   Never touches saved data. The player's own face is always put back. Story cutscenes / Story 2 fights keep using faces3d.js. */
(()=>{
  if(window.__F4||typeof THREE==='undefined'||typeof fighter!=='function'||typeof G==='undefined')return;
  window.__F4=1;
  const now=()=>performance.now();
  const mine=new WeakSet();
  const blinkCache=new WeakMap();
  const NOBLINK=/^(cool|robot|dead|xeyes|blank|dizzy|sleepy|square|catface)$/;
  const tex=(ex,ms,bl,ink)=>(window.F3&&F3.tex)?F3.tex(ex,ms,bl,ink):null;
  const headM=f=>{if(f.__h4===undefined)f.__h4=(f.head&&f.head.children.find(c=>c.material&&c.material.map))||null;return f.__h4};
  const inkOf=f=>{try{return (f.dc&&f.dc.cfg.fc)||'#111'}catch(e){return'#111'}};
  const exprOf=f=>{try{return (f.dc&&f.dc.cfg.expr)||''}catch(e){return''}};
  function st(f){return f.__f4||(f.__f4={orig:null,mine:null,cur:'',nextBlink:now()+1200+Math.random()*2500,blUntil:0,mood:null,moodUntil:0,nextMood:now()+5000+Math.random()*5000,hurtUntil:0,smugUntil:0,hitSeen:0})}

  /* blink version of the player's OWN face: white out the eyes and draw closed lids */
  function blinkTex(orig){
    let t=blinkCache.get(orig);if(t)return t;
    try{
      const src=orig.image,S=src.width||512,k=S/512;
      const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=S;const x=c.getContext('2d');
      x.drawImage(src,0,0,S,S);x.scale(k,k);
      x.fillStyle='#fff';x.fillRect(120,168,125,100);x.fillRect(267,168,125,100);
      x.strokeStyle='#111';x.lineWidth=20;x.lineCap='round';
      for(const cx of[180,332]){x.beginPath();x.moveTo(cx-34,212);x.quadraticCurveTo(cx,238,cx+34,212);x.stroke()}
      t=new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);mine.add(t);blinkCache.set(orig,t);return t;
    }catch(e){return null}
  }

  function setMap(f,t,key){
    const hm=headM(f),s=st(f);if(!hm||!t)return;
    if(s.cur===key&&hm.material.map===t)return;
    s.cur=key;s.mine=t;mine.add(t);hm.material.map=t;
  }
  function restore(f){
    const s=f.__f4,hm=headM(f);if(!s||!hm)return;
    if(s.orig&&s.cur!==''){hm.material.map=s.orig}
    s.cur='';s.mine=null;
  }
  /* keep faces3d from ever remembering one of my textures as "the original" */
  function guard(f){const s=f.__f4;if(s&&s.orig&&f.__orig&&mine.has(f.__orig))f.__orig=s.orig}
  /* if something else swapped the face (customizer / rebuild), adopt that as the new original */
  function adopt(f){
    const hm=headM(f),s=st(f);if(!hm)return false;
    const m=hm.material.map;
    if(!m)return false;
    if(!s.orig||(m!==s.orig&&!mine.has(m))){s.orig=m;s.cur='';s.mine=null}
    return true;
  }

  const EM={wave:'happy',hype:'happy',dance:'happy',clap:'happy',salute:'proud',bow:'proud',laugh:'laugh',facepalm:'worry',sleep:'sleepy',shrug:'derp',robot:'smug',moonwalk:'cool',flex:'proud'};
  const MOODS=['wink','happy','smug','proud','think'];

  function pick(f,o,fight){
    const s=st(f),t=now();
    if(!fight){
      /* lobby / customizer */
      if(f.em&&f.em.n)return EM[f.em.n]||(/^i_/.test(f.em.n)?null:'happy');
      if(t<s.moodUntil)return s.mood;
      if(t>s.nextMood){s.mood=MOODS[Math.floor(Math.random()*MOODS.length)];s.moodUntil=t+1300;s.nextMood=t+6500+Math.random()*6000;return s.mood}
      return null;
    }
    if(f.fall)return 'shock';
    if(f.ko||f.hp<=0)return 'dead';
    if(f.win)return 'laugh';
    if(o&&o.win)return 'cry';
    if(f.gbBusy)return 'evil';
    if(f.hit>0||f.stun>0)s.hurtUntil=t+420;
    if(t<s.hurtUntil)return (f.stun>.3)?'dizzy':'ouch';
    if(o){if(o.hit>0&&!s.hitSeen)s.smugUntil=t+800;s.hitSeen=o.hit>0?1:0}
    if(f.em&&f.em.n)return EM[f.em.n]||(/^i_/.test(f.em.n)?'smug':'happy');
    if(f.punch>0)return f.pk==='s'?'evil':'angry';
    if(f.dodge>0)return 'wink';
    if(f.air>0)return 'shock';
    if(f.block)return 'smug';
    if(t<s.smugUntil)return 'smug';
    if(f.hp<30)return 'worry';
    return null;
  }

  function tick(f,o,fight){
    if(!f||!f.head||!f.g||f.g.visible===false)return;
    if(!adopt(f))return;
    const s=st(f),t=now();
    if(t>s.nextBlink){s.blUntil=t+130;s.nextBlink=t+2200+Math.random()*3200}
    const bl=t<s.blUntil;
    const ex=pick(f,o,fight);
    if(ex){
      const ms=(ex==='laugh')?[1,2,1,0][Math.floor(t/130)%4]:(ex==='shock'&&f.fall)?[2,1,2,2][Math.floor(t/90)%4]:0;
      const tx=tex(ex,ms,(bl&&ex!=='dead'&&ex!=='dizzy')?1:0,inkOf(f));
      if(tx){setMap(f,tx,ex+'|'+ms+'|'+(bl?1:0));return}
    }
    if(bl&&!NOBLINK.test(exprOf(f))){const b=blinkTex(s.orig);if(b){setMap(f,b,'blink');return}}
    if(s.cur!==''){restore(f)}
  }

  setInterval(()=>{
    try{
      if(document.hidden)return;
      const E=window.S2E;
      const story=!!(E&&E.on&&!E.susp)||!!(G.bot&&G.bot.s2);
      [P,O].forEach(f=>{if(f)guard(f)});
      if(story){[P,O].forEach(f=>{if(f&&f.__f4&&f.__f4.cur!=='')restore(f)});return}
      const fight=G.mode==='match';
      if(fight){tick(P,O,true);tick(O,P,true)}
      else{tick(P,null,false);if(O&&O.g&&O.g.visible)tick(O,null,false)}
    }catch(e){}
  },60);
  window.F4={tick,restore};
})();

</script>
<script>
/* ===== FLOATING-PLATFORM MAPS (ring-outs) + TILT QUAKE SUPER =====
   - Some existing maps (Space, Lava Pit, Snowy Peak, Rooftop, Cyberpunk, Space Station, Desert Canyon) now have a
     fighting platform with nothing around it. Get knocked past the edge and you fall into the void and lose the round.
   - Walking and dashing stop you at the edge, only knockbacks, throws and the tilt can send you over.
   - TILT QUAKE (new super in the Supers shop): the whole arena tilts for 7s and your foe slides downhill.
   Offline fights only. Does not touch saved data. */
(()=>{
  if(typeof arena!=='function'||typeof anim!=='function'||typeof THREE==='undefined')return;
  const T=THREE;
  const HALF=7;
  const VOIDMAPS=new Set(['space','lava','snow','roof','cyber','station','canyon']);
  const V=window.__VOID={k:null,plat:null,floor:[],extra:[],tilt:null,HALF};
  const clampB=x=>Math.max(-BOUND,Math.min(BOUND,x));
  const on=()=>!!V.k&&!G.online&&!(G.bot&&G.bot.s2);
  const keyOfName=()=>{try{const n=document.getElementById('arT').innerText;return Object.keys(ARENAS).find(k=>ARENAS[k][0]===n)||null}catch(e){return null}};

  /* ---------- build the platform on top of the existing map ---------- */
  function build(k){
    V.k=null;V.plat=null;V.floor=[];V.extra=[];V.tilt=null;
    if(!VOIDMAPS.has(k)||typeof arenaG==='undefined'||!arenaG)return;
    const g=arenaG,kids=g.children.slice();
    const [nm,bg,fl,ac]=ARENAS[k];
    /* hide the endless floor + its ring, and scenery that would be floating over nothing */
    kids.forEach((c,i)=>{
      const r=Math.hypot(c.position.x,c.position.z);
      if(i<2){c.visible=false;V.floor.push(c)}
      else if(k!=='space'&&r>13){c.visible=false;V.floor.push(c)}
    });
    const plat=new T.Group();
    const top=new T.Mesh(new T.BoxGeometry(HALF*2,1.2,5.4),new T.MeshStandardMaterial({color:new T.Color(fl).lerp(new T.Color(ac),.28).multiplyScalar(1.35),roughness:.55,metalness:k==='cyber'?.5:.05}));top.position.y=-.6;top.receiveShadow=true;plat.add(top);
    const under=new T.Mesh(new T.BoxGeometry(HALF*2-2.4,2.4,3.4),new T.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x1e293b,roughness:.9}));under.position.y=-2.4;plat.add(under);
    const tip=new T.Mesh(new T.BoxGeometry(HALF*2-5.5,2.2,2),new T.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x0f172a,roughness:.95}));tip.position.y=-4.6;plat.add(tip);
    const em=new T.MeshBasicMaterial({color:ac});
    [[0,2.7,HALF*2+.04,.1,.1],[0,-2.7,HALF*2+.04,.1,.1]].forEach(([x,z,w,h,d])=>{const b=new T.Mesh(new T.BoxGeometry(w,h,d),em);b.position.set(x,.03,z);plat.add(b)});
    [-HALF,HALF].forEach(x=>{const b=new T.Mesh(new T.BoxGeometry(.1,.1,5.5),em);b.position.set(x,.03,0);plat.add(b)});
    /* centre line + hint markers near the edges */
    const mid=new T.Mesh(new T.BoxGeometry(.06,.02,5.2),new T.MeshBasicMaterial({color:ac,transparent:true,opacity:.35}));mid.position.y=.01;plat.add(mid);
    [-HALF+.9,HALF-.9].forEach(x=>{for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const b=new T.Mesh(new T.BoxGeometry(.5,.02,.22),new T.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xef4444,transparent:true,opacity:.55}));b.position.set(x,.012,-2+i);plat.add(b)}});
    g.add(plat);V.plat=plat;
    /* the abyss far below */
    const mkPlane=(col,y,op)=>{const m=new T.Mesh(new T.PlaneGeometry(220,220),new T.MeshBasicMaterial({color:col,transparent:op<1,opacity:op}));m.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;m.position.y=y;g.add(m);V.extra.push(m);return m};
    if(k==='lava'){V.lava=mkPlane(0xf97316,-7,1)}
    else if(k==='snow'){mkPlane(0x64748b,-9,1)}
    else if(k==='canyon'){mkPlane(0x3b1204,-12,1)}
    else if(k==='roof'||k==='cyber'){mkPlane(0x020617,-14,1);const gr=new T.GridHelper(220,44,ac,ac);gr.position.y=-13.9;gr.material.transparent=true;gr.material.opacity=.35;g.add(gr);V.extra.push(gr)}
    else{mkPlane(0x000000,-16,.55)}
    V.k=k;
    setVis();
  }
  function setVis(){
    const o=on();
    if(V.plat)V.plat.visible=o;
    V.floor.forEach(c=>{c.visible=!o});
    V.extra.forEach(c=>c.visible=o);
  }
  const oArena=arena;
  arena=function(k){const r=oArena.apply(this,arguments);try{build(k)}catch(e){console.warn('void',e&&e.message)}return r};
  try{build(keyOfName())}catch(e){}
  setInterval(()=>{try{setVis()}catch(e){}},400);

  /* ---------- falling ---------- */
  function startFall(f){
    f.fall=1;f.fallT=0;f.fvx=(f.x>0?1:-1)*1.5;f.__s0=f.g.scale.x||1;f.__trail=0;
    f.stun=99;f.punch=0;f.pend=null;f.block=false;f.em=null;
    text('AAAAAAH!');try{SFX.dodge&&SFX.dodge();SFX.hit&&SFX.hit()}catch(e){}
    ring(f.x,.05,0,0xffffff,3,.4,true);shake(.35);
    scream(f);
  }
  /* a falling scream: a wobbly note that slides down, quieter as they get far away */
  function scream(f){
    try{
      const AC=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;if(!AC)return;
      const ctx=window.__f4ac||(window.__f4ac=new AC());if(ctx.state==='suspended')ctx.resume();
      const o=ctx.createOscillator(),l=ctx.createOscillator(),lg=ctx.createGain(),g=ctx.createGain();
      o.type='sawtooth';l.frequency.value=9;lg.gain.value=26;l.connect(lg);lg.connect(o.frequency);
      const t0=ctx.currentTime,v=(window.SFX&&SFX.vol!=null)?SFX.vol:1;
      o.frequency.setValueAtTime(720,t0);o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(150,t0+1.25);
      g.gain.setValueAtTime(.0001,t0);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.07*Math.max(.2,Math.min(1,v)),t0+.08);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,t0+1.3);
      o.connect(g);g.connect(ctx.destination);o.start(t0);l.start(t0);o.stop(t0+1.35);l.stop(t0+1.35);
    }catch(e){}
  }
  function resetFall(f){
    if(f.fall){f.fall=0;f.fallT=0;f.__gy=0;try{f.g.scale.setScalar(f.__s0||1);f.g.position.y=0;f.root.rotation.x=0;f.root.rotation.z=0}catch(e){}f.__hidFall=false}
  }
  const oRF=resetFighters;
  resetFighters=function(){[P,O].forEach(resetFall);V.tilt=null;if(V.plat&&arenaG)arenaG.rotation.z=0;return oRF.apply(this,arguments)};

  /* ---------- tilt ---------- */
  const TDUR=7,TAMP=.13;
  function tiltK(){const t=V.tilt;if(!t)return 0;const u=t.t;if(u<.7){const a=u/.7;return a*a*(3-2*a)}if(u>TDUR-1){const a=Math.max(0,(TDUR-u)/1);return a*a*(3-2*a)}return 1}
  function tiltSuper(f,t){
    f.sup=0;text('TILT QUAKE!');try{SFX.sup()}catch(e){}
    const dir=t.x>f.x?1:-1;
    V.tilt={t:0,dir};
    ring(f.x,.05,0,0xfbbf24,12,.9,true);ring(f.x,.05,0,0xffffff,7,.6,true);burst(f.x,0xfbbf24,50);shake(1);
    try{const e=$('flash');if(e){e.classList.remove('f');void e.offsetWidth;e.classList.add('f')}}catch(e){}
    if(!(t.jump||t.dodge)&&!(t.fall)){damage(f,t,10,'QUAKE!');t.stun=Math.max(t.stun||0,.6)}
    setTimeout(()=>text(V.k&&on()?'SLIDE THEM OFF!':'THE FLOOR TILTS!'),550);
  }
  const oRes=resolve;
  resolve=function(f,kind){
    if(kind==='s'&&f===P&&G.pc&&G.pc.super==='tilt'&&!G.online&&!(typeof NET!=='undefined'&&NET.role==='guest')){try{tiltSuper(f,foe(f))}catch(e){console.warn('tilt',e&&e.message)}return}
    return oRes.apply(this,arguments);
  };

  /* ---------- per-fighter, after the base animation ---------- */
  function post(f,dt){
    if(G.mode!=='match'){if(f.fall)resetFall(f);return}
    /* tilt clock (advance once per frame, on P) */
    if(f===P&&V.tilt){
      if(!G.over&&!G.paused)V.tilt.t+=dt;
      if(V.tilt.t>=TDUR||G.over){V.tilt=null}
    }
    const k=tiltK(),th=V.tilt?-V.tilt.dir*TAMP*k:0;
    if(f===P&&arenaG){arenaG.rotation.z+=(th-arenaG.rotation.z)*Math.min(1,10*Math.max(dt,.016))}
    const slope=arenaG?Math.sin(arenaG.rotation.z):0;
    const lift=f.__lift||0;
    /* slide downhill: the foe slides fast, you are anchored */
    if(V.tilt&&k>.2&&!G.over&&!f.fall&&!f.gbBusy&&!(window.__GB&&__GB.cur&&(__GB.cur.t===f))){
      const sp=(f===P?.5:2.6)*k;
      f.tx=clampB(f.tx+V.tilt.dir*sp*dt);
    }
    if(on()){
      /* walking / dashing can't carry you over the edge */
      const slide=V.tilt&&k>.2&&f===O;
      if(!f.fall&&!slide&&!(f.stun>0)&&!f.air&&!f.jump){const m=HALF-.3;if(f.tx>m)f.tx=m;if(f.tx<-m)f.tx=-m}
      if(!f.fall&&!G.over&&!f.air&&!f.jump&&!f.gbBusy&&!(window.__GB&&__GB.cur&&__GB.cur.t===f)&&Math.abs(f.x)>HALF+.12)startFall(f);
      if(f.fall){
        f.fallT+=dt;const ft=f.fallT;
        const HANG=.32,d=Math.max(0,ft-HANG);
        const sg=f.fvx>0?1:-1;
        /* hang in the air for a heartbeat (arms windmilling), then drop faster and faster while tumbling */
        f.x+=(ft<HANG?.25:1)*f.fvx*dt;f.tx=f.x;f.g.position.x=f.x;
        f.__gy=ft<HANG?Math.sin(ft/HANG*Math.PI)*.12:-(8.5*d*d);
        const flail=Math.sin(ft*34);
        const hy=1.75+flail*.5,hz=.55+Math.cos(ft*34)*.25;
        f.hl.position.set(0,hy,hz);f.hr.position.set(0,1.75-flail*.5,-hz);
        f.fl.position.set(0,.5+Math.max(0,flail)*.45,.38+flail*.3);f.fr.position.set(0,.5+Math.max(0,-flail)*.45,-.38-flail*.3);
        f.root.rotation.x=sg*(ft<HANG?-.35*Math.sin(ft/HANG*Math.PI/2):-.35+d*d*7+d*3);f.root.rotation.z=0;
        f.root.rotation.y=Math.sin(ft*9)*.2;
        try{limbs(f)}catch(e){}
        const sc=(f.__s0||1)*Math.max(.28,1-d*.55);f.g.scale.setScalar(sc);
        f.stun=99;
        const gy=f.x*slope+f.__gy;
        f.g.position.y=gy;f.__wy=true;
        /* wind streaks behind them */
        f.__trail-=dt;if(f.__trail<=0&&ft>HANG){f.__trail=.045;try{blob(f.x+(Math.random()-.5)*.5,gy+1.4+Math.random()*1.2,(Math.random()-.5)*.4,0xffffff,.16,.45,1)}catch(e){}}
        /* camera follows them down */
        if(!f.__dead){try{lookTo.y=Math.max(-4.5,gy*.65+.8);camTo.y=Math.max(-1.5,2.0+gy*.25)}catch(e){}}
        const FL=V.k==='lava'?-6.8:-9;
        if(gy<FL&&!f.__dead){
          f.__dead=true;
          try{
            if(V.k==='lava'){ring(f.x,-6.7,0,0xf97316,7,.7,true);ring(f.x,-6.7,0,0xfde047,4,.5,true);burst(f.x,0xf97316,50);SFX.boom&&SFX.boom()}
            else{ring(f.x,-8.8,0,0xffffff,6,.8,true);burst(f.x,0xcbd5e1,24)}
          }catch(e){}
          f.g.visible=false;f.__hidFall=true;
          text(V.k==='lava'?'BURNED!':'FELL INTO THE VOID!');
          /* hp is set AFTER endRound so no finisher thinks this was a normal KO */
          if(!G.over)endRound(f===O);
          f.hp=0;
          try{lookTo.y=1.2;camTo.y=1.5}catch(e){}
        }
        return;
      }
    }
    {const want=lift+f.x*slope;if(Math.abs(want)>.001||f.__wy){f.g.position.y=want;f.__wy=Math.abs(want)>.001}}
  }
  const oAnim=anim;
  anim=function(f,dt,t){const r=oAnim.apply(this,arguments);try{post(f,dt)}catch(e){}return r};
  /* a fighter that has been hidden by a fall comes back on the next round */
  const oRF2=resetFighters;
  resetFighters=function(){[P,O].forEach(f=>{f.__dead=false;if(f.__hidFall){f.g.visible=true;f.__hidFall=false}});return oRF2.apply(this,arguments)};
})();

</script>
</body></html>
